Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda; *aṣṭakam* – eight verses; *suṣṭhu* – with a proper devotional spirit; *paṭhanti* – recite; *nityam* – regularly; *gaura* – from Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu; *udaya* – risn; *ācārya* – from the disciplic succession of bona fide spiritual masters; *vicitra* – wonderful; *śaktyā* – with potency; *te* – they; *kṛṣṇa* – of Lord Kṛṣṇa; *vārtām* – message; *pravadanti* – preach; *bhūmyām* – in this world.

**These eight verses describe, in small parts, the unlimited glories of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. The authors of these verses pray that all those who read them regularly and with the proper devotional spirit may become wonderfully empowered by the ācāryas descended from Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to preach the message of Lord Kṛṣṇa all over the world.**

Presented with faith on the most auspicious occasion of Vyāsa-pūjā, 1980, by

*Your most insignificant disciple,*  
Jayatīrtha Goswami

## Kīrtanānanda Swami

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Prabhupāda,

I want to offer some nice words to praise your greatness and thank you for coming to rescue me from this abominable condition of material life, but I am entirely helpless to do so. I feel like little Dhruva, who wanted to pray the Lord, but was incompetent to do so. Kṛṣṇa, however, very kindly touched his lips to His conchshell, and then only could he offer nice prayers. I am simply begging you to kindly touch my lips, my ears, my eyes, my heart, my everything. I want to see you as you are; I want to hear your nectarean words always; I want to be able to repeat only what you have said; and most of all, I want a heart that is soft like your lotus feet.

So many times we had the opportunity to be with you personally, to cook, massage or in some way try to assist you, but still we could never know you or understand you. For years we used to dream of your coming to live in your palace, but still we don’t have you.

I am reminded of Lord Dāmodara being chased by Mother Yaśodā. Because He was naughty, she wanted to bind Him with ropes. Of course, this is impossible, but still, for His pure devotee He agreed to be bound up.

My dear lord, Śrīla Prabhupāda, there is no question of binding you. You are as good as the independent Supreme Personality of Godhead. But Hopefully, we can become bound up in this Kṛṣṇa consciousness service. You are everything in this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement: the goal, the sustainer, the master, the witness, the abode, the refuge, the dearmost friend – the basis of everything. Now, by your grace, we are begging you to use us in your service in any way you desire: for success or failure, honor or dishonor, happiness or distress, you may kick us or embrace us. You can do anything you like.

*We remain yours eternally,*  
Kīrtanānanda Swami

## Pañcadraviḍa Swami

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

The 747 lands and taxies to a gradual halt. The jet engines whir and then gradually become silent . Then a tumultuous kīrtana begins, as devotees dance and throw flowers, while you majestically descend the stairs of the ramp and stand, surrounded by so many ecstatic devotees, “Śrīla Prabhupāda, jagad-guru, has arrived!” “Whole world saved, Śrīla Prabhupāda!” Millions have arrived for your reception, and they offer you their obeisances, ārati, and fragrant flower garlands.

A grand reception has formed in the chamber of my heart. A teeming multitude of persons crowd, pushing to have a glimpse of you. I am there, waiting, with a garland in my hand. Now, as you walk down the passageway, I push my way forward and place the garland on you as you smile, and say, “Jaya.” Next, I decorate your head with fresh sandalwood paste and fall prostrated before you, my eternal spiritual master.

I am remembering the pageantry of your arrival in so many cities of this planet. How many arrivals and receptions there were as you benedicted so many Kali-yuga cities with your presence and lionlike preaching! And as I remember how hard you worked, how you never stopped preaching, I pray to follow in your footsteps. Although I am most insignificant in the presence of so many more qualified disciples and granddisciples of yourself, nonetheless, I pray that I may serve your mission by always preaching.

I cannot see you, as one on a cloudy day cannot see the sun. The sun is there, of course, but the clouds obstruct one’s vision. In the same way, you are always there, Śrīla Prabhupāda, being present in your instructions. But as my vision is covered by the clouds of māyā, I too often forget you, and this is my great misfortune. But nonetheless, somehow or other, I am praying to always be engaged in your service and thus always remember you.

It is not an easy thing to follow in your footsteps, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and so as Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura sings, I am praying to Lord Caitanya to surrender my mind, my body, and everything in your service. I am very attached to this illusory, material existence and not at all advanced, but I am praying that somehow or other I may gain a taste for serving you, and thus surrender myself completely at your lotus feet.

Your lotus feet are my only refuge, and apart from your lotus feet, I would have died long ago, alone and afraid in the gurgling mire of material existence. As man caught in quicksand cannot himself, so I, too, would be most hopeless if I were not able to cling to your lotus feet. I am most afraid of material life and of falling prey to illusion. Please do not push me away, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as one might brush an insignificant insect off himself, but allow me to fix myself as a particle of dust at your lotus feet. Now, in this lifetime, I pray to surrender unto you.

*Your most fallen servant,*  
Pañcadraviḍa Swami

## Prabhupāda-kṛpa Goswami

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! What can I do?  
The chance for touching your lotus feet  
is now gone, and I am left  
wishfully thinking that it could be yesterday,  
and at your feet, sitting on the mat in the garden  
of your Vṛndāvana, I could again  
be gently massaging your lotus-soft soles  
with mustard oil and love.

The chance to purify my mind and body and words  
has only come with the sweet remembrance  
of the days we spent together, with me as your  
humble and dependent servant, a small boy  
reliant upon your kindness for my entire existence,  
my greatest fear being only that I should lose  
your association and be left out alone  
in a cruel and unkind place, this material world.

I only lived to see your smile and smile with you.

I ever wanted to sit by your desk and hear  
your nectarean words come flowing forth,  
like the juice from some transcendental desire tree  
which gives immortal life to its recipient.

A glass of water? A cooling fan? Or perhaps I can do  
some other simple, menial task for your pleasure.

I only live to have that chance – some service at your command.

This higher taste I never knew before you  
gave me shelter, and because of it  
I became alive and fresh,  
revived by your sweet instruction.

I remember the days we spent and long  
for your association. You became my life and breath  
because you gave me your protection,  
and I never had any benefactor  
who could be as merciful as you.

Let me pray constantly to Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa for the chance  
to keep the idea of serving you within my heart,  
and going on from those sixteen months  
now out to serve you stronger  
by carrying out your instruction, go spread  
this mercy to others  
and give them the nectar of your association.

Let others also be endowed with this –  
the cooling shade of your lotus feet and  
the shelter of your protection – as it comes to them  
through your books, which you carefully prepared,  
as I and others slept,  
planning for the whole world’s salvation.

We should carry them as the most precious jewels  
because they came from you and are the final products  
of your hard work, sincere desire, and genuine attempt  
to please your spiritual master.  
You only asked us to do this one task,  
to give out whatever we have got from you,  
but fallen and miserable  
and too attentive to Māyā’s cheating demands,  
I fear I will never fulfill your aspirations  
and the debt that I owe you.

Oh, Śrīla Prabhupāda! What can I do? Your remembrance  
is all that saves me.

Let the time that I spent in your company  
remain in my heart  
as the constant reminder that I am nothing  
but your Prabhupāda-kṛpa.

*Your helpless son and follower,*  
Prabhupāda-kṛpa Goswami

## Rāmeśvara Swami

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

I come before you as a beggar. I beg to become your eternal servant and never be separate from you. You are the first person that I ever felt a desire to completely serve and love. Since you revealed that ISKCON is your body, we can direct our love and service to your Society. You once told me that it is GBC men’s duty to “think of the world first, and your country second.” That is real renunciation, free from sectarian and party spirit, free from illusory attachments to home and “private life.” To simply be an “ISKCON man,” like a puppet, with you as my eternal master, is the perfection of my life. But I seem to be easily covered by forgetfulness and therefore always need to be lifted by your mercy. The conditioned soul’s heart is always contaminated by some degree of lust, anger and greed, which causes this forgetfulness. This contamination causes the mind to become attached to profit, adoration, worship and distinction. This unhealthy condition is so deeply rooted that I am certain that I can never become your pure servant and instrument by my own endeavor. So I am simply praying to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu for His mercy that these impurities may be removed from my heart. I know that this mercy is especially received through the intimate association of the devotees, who are always keeping one another situated on the razor’s edge of devotional service. Sometimes they push one back to the razor’s edge with a rose and sometimes with a thunderbolt, but either way, according to the circumstances, their association is the greatest blessing and protection. In the association of the devotees I am seeing Your Divine Grace’s hand moving your puppetlike servants forward in their Kṛṣṇa consciousness. That is your display of love for all the devotees, that you are still present within ISKCON, manifesting your mercy and plans and always guiding and protecting us. It is clear to me that even though, from the point of material vision, you have departed, you can actually appear through your representatives at any moment, simply by your sweet will. This vision is giving me renewed life and freeing me from material conceptions about your transcendental disappearance. I can serve you more purely by taking shelter of the devotees, and thus free myself from the illusion that I have been left alone to become Kṛṣṇa conscious by my own means. You once said, “Physical presence is for neophytes.” You have left us your transcendental instructions, which are the means to attain your eternal association. You have left us your ISKCON Society, which gives us direction for our service and devotion. And you have left us the association of the devotees to keep us from deviating. As we ecstatically remember you on this Vyāsa-pūjā day, I feel a deep indebtedness for your giving us the path of perfection, and can only humbly pray that I take this mercy and become qualified to serve you by increasing this ISKCON Society and pushing on your mission to make the world Kṛṣṇa conscious.

*Your unworthy servant,*  
Rāmeśvara Swami

## Rūpānuga dāsa

O Vaiṣṇava commander in chief, only you can lead us.

You once told me not to worry if the *rākṣasas* would not listen to spiritual reason. You said, “Never mind, the power is in the people.” The people remained spiritually disinherited until your arrival. When will they become enlightened and learn from you what real freedom is? Please empower us to liberate them from the atheistic influences of modern civilization.

You humbly passed by the barking dogs at the government gates and went directly to the people to give them your mercy. You showed a little needle at the gates, but a plow was in your baggage – the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. Spiritual revolutions take time. You prepared for four decades before beginning your movement to bring about a change in the impious lives of the people. You were the first to bring Vaiṣṇavism out of India. You have sown spiritual seeds, the fruits of which cannot now be imagined. (Whatever we are doing you have already done; we are simply poor imitations.)

By Lord Kṛṣṇa’s arrangement, Arjuna was illusioned within himself because of his attachments, but when he was fully purified he was able to fight those who were against the Lord and defeat them. Our fight is not only against the illusions within but also against the illusions without – those *asuras* who are in the jurisdiction of *māyā* because they keep thinking they can win against God by manipulating His material energy, misusing their free will at the cost of the precious human lives of the conditioned souls.

The Bāṇāsuras and Hiraṇyākṣas of ancient history stalked the universe seeking someone who could defeat them. When Bāṇāsura heard from Lord Śiva that a competent person would finally come to defeat him, he was filled with great pleasure, and he always waited for that day when that suitable fighter would come to cut down his strength. Even now, in the hope of enjoying materialistic pleasures, such powerful fools are planning how to destroy masses of people in the pincers of opposing regimes. Puppet armies of various colors try to shake the earth with their heavy boots.

We, your servants, are serving them notice. As those ancient opponents of the universal order were vanquished by the Lord of the universe, their modern imitators, who will not allow their atheistic mentality to be transformed by the mercy of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, will by your mercy be conquered. Please guide us through this Kari-yuga battlefield as Lord Kṛṣṇa guided Arjuna through the Battle of Kurukṣetra.

O Vaiṣṇava commander in chief, lead us to victory!

Rūpānuga dāsa

## Satsvarūpa Goswami

“O self-illumined one, the great saintly persons who have crossed over the ocean of nescience by the help of the transcendental boat of your lotus feet have not taken away the boat. Ii is still lying on this side.”

If something is made easy or successful, that gives great facility for the person who makes it. But also it’s good for those who follow him. This is the greatness of the methods of *bhakti-yoga* that Śrīla Prabhupāda has introduced, according to time and place. These methods, of course, he received from his spiritual master Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī. The basic principle was introduced by Lord Caitanya: *harer nāma harer nāma harer nāmaiva kevalam*. Śrīla Prabhupāda has introduced it according to time and place and has created world-saving Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. By his personally taking Kṛṣṇa consciousness to heart he proved that without doubt *he* could stay always in the shade of the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa and go back to Godhead. Just by chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, taking *prasāda*, always preaching about Kṛṣṇa, he showed us in his life that it was not difficult to do, rather, it was blissful. So if we follow – when we see a pure devotee who does follow, who does live in love of Kṛṣṇa, who does give up material desires – then we see actually it’s not difficult; it’s ecstatic. We saw our own fathers struggling to maintain their families – very difficult, not happy. But then we saw Śrīla Prabhupāda – just by serving Kṛṣṇa, he was very happy. So that was desirable.

By his surrendering to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa he was carried back to Godhead, but in such an exemplary way that others can, just by following his instructions, do the same thing. The scriptures give a simile that one gets into a boat to cross over the ocean to save himself. So by the normal process he crosses in the boat and he and the boat are gone. But you, me and all other ordinary people who didn’t get in the boat – how will we cross? You can’t fashion such a boat on your own. Śrīla Prabhupāda was magnificently surrendered to his spiritual master, and so he got into the boat of the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa by his devotional expertise. But the greater miracle done by Śrīla Prabhupāda is that he has crossed but the boat is still here. The process recommended for crossing the ocean of nescience is easy not only for the devotee but for common persons who follow the devotee. *Mahājano yena gataḥ sa panthāḥ*. All we have to do is sincerely follow the orders of Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Satsvarūpa Goswami

## Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami

A Minister’s Prayer  
O merciful master,  
Timely you came,  
Freely distributing  
The Lord’s holy name.

The pious, the sinful,  
You make no distinction,  
Their material existence  
Cast into extinction.

Mahāprabhu’s mission  
In your hands was placed,  
To offer the whole world  
Kṛṣṇa’s sweet name to taste.

Within the heart  
Selfish desires ceasing,  
Kṛṣṇa-bhakti  
Ever increasing.

Enter a minister  
With lust, anger and greed,  
But in his heart  
You have planted a seed.

Powerful those forces  
Which lay within,  
Causing the minister  
Repeatedly to sin.

But not so powerful  
As the seed you have sown,  
And with your care,  
The plant that has grown.

Arrogant, proud,  
A wayward minister,  
But by your influence,  
No longer sinister.

Evil forces  
Tried to attack,  
But with your knowledge  
You forced them back.

False prestige and pride  
Cut by your sword,  
I seek your shelter  
O merciful lord.

Stripped of illusions,  
A naked soul,  
I wish to fulfill  
Life’s cherished goal.

Please take my hand  
And guide me true.  
In exchange, my life  
I consign to you.

The śāstra proclaims you  
As “servitor Lord,”  
Free of false motives  
Service without reward.

Austerity is your wealth,  
Bhakti your rule.  
Now I surrender;  
Please make me your tool.

To heaven or hell  
By your order I shall go  
To preach the glories  
Of the eternal soul.

Eastward bound  
To a God-forgotten land,  
Departing with your order,  
Beneath your blessing hand.

Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami

## Vāsudeva dāsa

*sādho jīvo vā maro vā*

A saintly person may either live or die, for during his life he is engaged in serving the Lord, and after death he serves the Lord as His eternal servant. Thus life and death are the same for a saintly person, for in both he serves the Lord constantly.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, not a single day is passed without remembering you; we think of you day and night. How fortunate we were to have had the rare opportunity to sit at your lotus feet and hear you speak, to speak with you personally and receive personal instructions from you occasionally. We are feeling deeply moved by these sweet remembrances. You are present in your books and instructions, but that does not satisfy us. Your physical absence is unbearable for us. By your causeless mercy you have pulled us out from this material entanglement, but we were not able to serve you directly in return. We are in your debt birth after birth.

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet, which alone can give shelter to the fallen souls. We beg for a drop of your mercy. Jaya Prabhupāda!

*Your humble servant,*  
Vāsudeva dāsa

## Vyāsa-Pūjā Homages from ISKCON Centers Around the World

## Adelaide

Blinded we all were, going astray,  
till you come, Śrīla Prabhupāda, with the holy name.

Your were the Lord’s representative,  
and you brought us a chance to live.

Our lives were just a series of one-night stands,  
but now, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have made them grand  
by teaching us the goal of life, kṛṣṇa-prema.

There are many persons said to be worthy of respect,  
but you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are above such a sect.

You are our most auspicious lord,  
for you give not just a temporary refrain,  
but life eternal, the greatest gain.

How can we thank you for your matchless gifts,  
which give us the ultimate lifts  
in consciousness? Oh, yes!

Challenging the scientists, you say, “Missing links?  
The missing links are the brains of the scientists.”  
In such a perfect way you clear away  
the darkness of the materialistic sway.

Our live for you, O dear master and lord,  
is not the love of the mundaner’s fraud,  
for we are bound by a conditionless cord.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, from your lotus feet  
are emanating the soft moonlike rays of your mercy so deep.

We want to take up your mind in mine,  
to preach to the conditioned souls,  
“This is not yours; this is not mine.  
Material enjoyment, it’s simply a crime.”

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, keep us in your connection.  
Don’t ever let us lose your recollection.

How staunchly you walked and talked,  
oh, so proud to be Kṛṣṇa’s servant,  
and we, with our minds full of desire,  
stumbling in your retire.

We are the most miserable souls;  
we have not seen what are the real goals.

On your Vyāsa-pūjā day, by your causeless mercy,  
may you grant us your shelter, we pray.

Your most insignificant servants of ISKCON Adelaide.

Kṛpākara dāsa adhikātī / Satyasena dāsa adhikātī / Kuntibhoja dāsa adhikātī / Mahā-vaikuṇṭha-nātha dāsa adhikātī / Sanātana dāsa adhikātī / Kṛṣṇa-prasāda dāsa adhikātī / Aravinda dāsa adhikātī / Śikhipuccha-dhāra dāsa brahmacārī / Puruṣottama dāsa brahmacārī / Brahmā dāsa brahmacārī / Akrūra dāsa brahmacārī / Pāñcajanya dāsa brahmacārī / Jaleyu-devī dāsī, Kaustubha / Kṛṣṇa-pramavatī-devī dāsī / Kātyāyanī-devī dāsī / Ramaṇīya-devī dāsī, Vinoda, Bhakti / Devī-devī-priya-devī dāsī, Kamala-locana / Bhūmi-devī dāsī, Gopāla Kṛṣṇa-devī dāsī, Gopāla Kṛṣṇa / Lalitā-devī dāsī / Mandākinī-devī dāsī / Lakṣmī-devī dāsī / Uttarā-devī dāsī / Mr. and Mrs. Sutherland

## Ahmedabad

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most worthless obeisances at your holy lotus feet.

Indeed, we cannot imagine the feelings of the *gopīs* when they condemned Lord Brahmā for creating eyes which blink, obstructing their vision of Lord Kṛṣṇa. But we are feeling similarly about the material time element which separates us disciples from our most merciful spiritual master. We cannot understand our great fortune that we able to associate with you personally, we were able to talk with you, laugh with you and sit with you. This is certainly a very rare fortune. The association of the pure devotees of the Lord is certainly the most blessed event in anyone’s life. And you are not an ordinary pure devotee, you are pure representative of Kṛṣṇa. You came into this terrifying Kali-yuga to change the face of the age. You have introduced a new element, by which even the most degraded, the most wretched, the most useless people can be blessed with the ultimate goal of life – love of God. It is only your phenomenal causeless mercy that this great event has happened. Therefore, you are a very special emissary, sent by Kṛṣṇa. Anyone who comes in contact with you, directly of indirectly, is a very fortunate soul. Even though we are very degraded, we are very proud to be your disciples and to have been able to enjoy your association at the time your were on the planet. This ecstatic association is glorified in all the Vedic scriptures as inevitable for obtaining the mercy of the Supreme Lord. As Jaḍa Bharata explains:

*rahūgaṇaitat tapasā na yāti*  
*na cejyayā nirvapaṇād gṛhād vā*  
*na cchandasā naiva jalāgni-sūryair*  
*vinā mahat-pāda-rajo-’bhiṣekam*

**“My dear King Rahūgaṇa, unless one has the opportunity to smear his entire body with the dust of the lotus feet of great devotees \[*****mahājanas or mahātmās*****\], one cannot realize the Absolute Truth. One cannot realize the Absolute Truth simply by observing celibacy, strictly following the rules and regulations of householder life, leaving home as a vānaprastha, accepting** ***sannyāsa*****, or undergoing severe penances in winter by keeping oneself submerged in water or surrounding oneself in summer by fire and the scorching heat of the sun. There are many other processes to understand the Absolute Truth, but the Absolute Truth is only revealed to one who has attained the mercy of a great devotee.” (*****SB*****5.12.12)**

The same thing is stressed by Prahlāda Mahārāja when he instructed his father about the science of bhakti-yoga. He says:

*naiṣāṁ matis tāvad urukramāṅghriṁ*  
*spṛśaty anarthāpagamo yad-arthaḥ*  
*mahīyasāṁ pāda-rajo-’bhiṣekaṁ*  
*niṣkiñcanānāṁ na vṛṇīta yāvat*  
*\[SB 7.5.32\]*

Unless human society accepts the dust of the lotus feet of great mahātmās—devotees who have nothing to do with material possessions—mankind cannot turn its attention to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. Those lotus feet vanquish all the unwanted, miserable conditions of material life.

However, our lives were not ordinary material lives, but very sinful lives. Generally, people are interested in sense gratification without performing too many sinful activities. But who could be more sinful than we? We were not just *karmīs*; we were *vikarmīs*. We were on our way to hell a t neck-breaking speeds. But somehow or other, by your most compassionate, loving, humble, kind, and irrefutable appeal to Kṛṣṇa, He arranged for you to save us. This is beyond our understanding.

Even though we are not fine, first-class devotees, still we are on the right path, and just as it is stated in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*:

*maivaṁ mamādhamasyāpi*  
*syād evācyuta-darśanam*  
*hrīyamānaḥ kāla-nadyā*  
*kvacit tarati kaścana*

**“ ‘Because I am so fallen, I shall never get a chance to see the Supreme Personality of Godhead.’ This was my false apprehension. Rather, by chance, a person as fallen as I am may get to see the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Although one is being carried away by the waves of the river of time, one may eventually reach the shore.” (SB 10.38.5)**

Now, even though we have not reached the goal, we have this assurance that somehow or other, someday, we will reach the goal. We have this satisfaction that we are passing by this dense darkness of material life, and this satisfaction is so great that the unfortunate *karmīs* cannot even dream of this. All the wealth, beauty, fame, and knowledge of this material world cannot bring a fraction of this satisfaction of solely taking shelter of the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Generally, in the material world, when a leader passes away, the followers become weak and fall away. But to everyone’s surprise, we are feeling your direct presence in all the aspects of your mission. All your senior disciples are so united and so conscious of their responsibility that your movement is progressing very nicely, even in spite of your physical absence. This proves that this movement is spiritual, and Kṛṣṇa and you are directly controlling it. Now that you have passed away from this material world, you are supervising your movement from spiritual sky and directing every aspect of it.

Your glories are unlimited, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Even the highest demigods like Brahmā or Śiva cannot describe your glories fully. Just as Kṛṣṇa is unlimitedly glorious, so also are His pure devotees. When Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu asked Rāmānanda Rāya, “Who is the most famous person in the world? Rāmānanda Rāya replied, “A pure devotee of the Lord is the most famous.” Your Divine Grace is famous not only here in this insignificant universe, but you are famous all over the three worlds. Everyone in the spiritual sky must be talking about how you gloriously spread the holy name to every town and village. Therefore, naturally you are the most famous in all the worlds.

When Jesus Christ left, he left only twelve disciples, and his glories are spread all over world. You left hundreds of disciples, so your glories will spread even more than Jesus Christ’s. The real message of Christ is lost, due to the luck of continuous disciplic succession. But your message will live forever in your books and your disciples, who will never fail you. Your books do not leave any room for misinterpretation. They are very clearly declaring the supremacy of Lord Kṛṣṇa. From the beginning to the end, you never compromised.

Everything about you is first class. Your books, your lectures, your speaking, your singing, your walking, your talking, your laughing, your everything was always first class, and everyone was always impressed and attached to you, just like to Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa endows all these perfect qualities upon His pure devotee. You are not only a pure devotee but you are *śaktyāveśa-avatāra*, who have changed the course of Kali-yuga. Simply by your advent, Kali-yuga has become a worshipable age now. In other yugas, this boon of *hari-nāma* which you have spread to every town and village to the most sinful of men is not there. This is all described in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam:*

*kaliṁ sabhājayanty āryā guṇa jñāḥ sāra-bhāginaḥ*  
*yatra saṅkīrtanenaiva sarva-svārtho 'bhilabhyate*  
*(Bhāg. 11.5.36)*

**“Those who are advanced and highly qualified and are interested in the essence of life know the good qualities of Kali-yuga. Such people worship the Age of Kali because in this age simply by chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa** ***mahā-mantra*****.” one can advance in spiritual knowledge and attain life’s goal.”**

Thus everything becomes blissful by your mercy. How wonderful it is that the most sinful age is made worshipable. That is your miracle. You can make the most sinful man a pure devotee, and the most sinful age worshipable.

You are so kind, you have given us everything, more than we could ever ask for. Everything is there in your books, your very self. You have provided us with the most beautiful of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. You have shown us the most sublime process of devotional service to Kṛṣṇa, and you have given us the association of the most wonderful devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa. So kindly now give us strength to appreciate fully this mercy you have showered upon us. Please give us intelligence by which we can please you more and more and then come to your lotus feet. We do not aspire for liberation, which is waiting at your feet, but we aspire only for the dust of your lotus feet, for there we can obtain Kṛṣṇa. *Kṛṣṇa-prāti yāha haite*.

We have started to develop your mission here, in the land of Gujarat, which is supposed to be very pious because the people are naturally vegetarian and worship Kṛṣṇa. They have nice temples in Dvārakā, Dakor, etc. They are all worshipers of Śrī Nāthajī, the lifter of Govardhana Hill. You told us once in Bombay that this Śrī Nāthajī Deity actually belongs to our *sampradāya*. Śrīla Mādhavendra Purī dreamt of Śrī Nāthajī and discovered Him in a jungle and installed Him at Govardhana. Later on, He was transferred to the Vallabha-sampradāya by Rūpa Gosvāmī. However, the Vallabha-sampradāya members are claiming that Śrī Nāthajī appeared to Vallabha Ācārya. Anyway, since, Śrī Nāthajī is nondifferent from Gopāla, originally manifested to Mādhavendra Purī, we have decided to install Him in your proposed temple in Ahmedabad. Then all these Gujaratis, who are so much devoted to Śrī Nāthajī, can come and worship Him here, in Ahmedabad. I am sure that by your potency this Śrī Nāthajī will become as famous as Śrī Nāthajī of Nāthadvāra. Then Śrīla Mādhavendra Purī will be very pleased that His Gopāla Deity has again manifested in the line of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Our only ambition in life is to please the *ācāryas*, among whom you are the foremost. But we are very wretched and have no good qualities. Kindly let us just remain in the shade of your lotus feet, and give us the strength to spread this great mission to every unfortunate jīva of this miserable material world. Please give us the strength to remember your transcendental pastimes every day, at every moment, and not only on this one day. That will be the perfection of our lives.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Your humble servants in ISKCON Ahmedabad.

Yaśomatīnandana dāsa / Abhinandana dāsa / Nārada Ṛṣi dāsa / Śukadeva dāsa / Rūpa Gosvāmī dāsa / Śāradendu dāsa / Vraja-nātha dāsa / Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Rādhā-kuṇḍa-devī dāsī / Dharmakalā-devī dāsī / Gaurāṅga / Haridāsa / Nanda

## ISKCON Athens

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances millions of times at your lotus feet.

On this Vyāsa-pūjā day we are once again remembering and tasting the sweetness of your transcendental glories and are marvelling at the depth of your divine mercy and kindness. Fully realizing our degraded condition of life, you set out from your peaceful home at Rādhā-Dāmodara to fill the world with the brilliant rays of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Bhagavad-gītā*, and the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra.* Putting aside all personal considerations, you incessantly labored to establish and spread this society throughout the world. In 1976, when you visited us in Toronto, you were consulted as to how you would like your speaking program arranged. Knowing that you were racing hard to complete *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, we did not want to inconvenience you. To this you replied, “I have already accepted great inconvenience to do all this travel at such old age. Now I shall do whatever you desire.”

Being fully patient and tolerant of our mad Western habits, you gently took us and in our hearts planted the seed of the creeper of devotion. You watered it with your limitless ocean of love and compassion. Carefully tending to all the fragile creepers, you showed us how to remove the weeds of lust, greed, anger, and envy and thus watched the creepers grow into your ISKCON society of first-class devotees. Once, when you were asked if you possessed mystic powers, you said, “Yes, my power is that I have turned *yavanas* and *mlecchas* into Vaiṣṇavas.”

Your deep realization and love of Kṛṣṇa can never be fathomed, and the extent of your mercy will never be known. You are brilliant ray of Lord Caitanya’s mercy, having descended to this world just to fulfill His order. You have fulfilled the prophecy of Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, and you superexcellently have fulfilled the order of your divine master Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura.

On this day we are intensely feeling your presence before us and are considering that your descent into this world is the greatest act of selfless love that has ever been performed. We are realizing how you are our eternal lord, master, savior, and dearmost intimate friend. Please be kind to us and glance across us with your beautiful lotus eyes. Our desire is to always follow your instructions and give our lives to help expand your society throughout the universe. By preaching your message we hope in this live to always have your blessed association and, at the time of giving up this body, come to you and keep your association eternally.

Everything we are and that we possess is the gift of your love and mercy. Please bless us that we will continue to execute your order and taste your divine love. Your fame is spread throughout the three worlds.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your eternal servants,*

Ayodhyāpati dāsa / Vṛṣa-kṛti dāsa / Śrīkārī-devī dāsī / Kṛṣṇa Kumar Khare / Evangelos Cucorinis / Bhakta Jorgos / Bhakta Jorgos / Bhakta Babis / Dimitri / Ilios / Evy

## Atlanta

The One Who Loves Me Best  
It’s not that I lament ’cause I haven’t got a cent  
(I’m not entrapped by money)  
It’s not that I cry ’cause I’m destined to die  
(I know what to do at the time of death  
But it’s an all-pervading feeling, heavy with regret  
(Ah, Prabhupāda, Prabhupāda)

He always gave me more that I knew to ask him for  
(Looking out for my best interests)  
Absorbed all my past crimes, all the uncontrolled times  
(Peace of mind was just a dream then)  
And he gave me a song to sing for all my life  
(Ah, Prabhupāda)

More than a magic charm – the song of God  
(Ah, Prabhupāda, Prabhupāda)

Now he’s traveled on, I’m not sure where he’s gone  
(He comes and goes at his pleasure)  
He gave me his maps to save me from the traps  
(Why am I such a slow learner?)  
It’s an uphill climb to the mountain’s peak  
(Ah, Prabhupāda)

Gotta keep climbing, tho’ I’m so weak  
(Ah, Prabhupāda, Prabhupāda)

If I’m faithful and sincere, in my heart he’s always here  
(I’m not alone; I know he’ll help me)  
And his words are nectar sweet, let me sit at his feet  
(I’ll be his expert servant)  
I am blind but he can see, and he’s promised to take me  
(Ah, Prabhupāda)

Out of this dark night into eternal daylight  
(Ah, Prabhupāda, Prabhupāda)

## Auckland

To our dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*guravo bahavaḥ santi*  
*śiṣya-vittāpahārakāḥ*  
*durlabho sad-gurur devi*  
*śiṣya-santāpa-hārakaḥ*

“Many are the gurus who are expert  
in stealing their disciples’ wealth,  
but rare is that spiritual master  
who can give us spiritual health!”

These are the words of Lord Śiva,  
right even for this day and age,  
with so many rascals posing  
as this or that guru or sage  
With deceptive smile and sleight of word  
they capture the public mind.

Such a lamentable state of affairs –  
blind men leading the blind.  
But in the darkness, a shining light  
appeared to show us the way.  
Amidst all the world’s confusion,  
a transcendental ray.

Your, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so mercifully came  
to give what none could give.  
You saved us from our miserable life  
and taught us how to live.

You taught us the purpose of life and how  
to reach complete perfection –  
how to be free from māyā’s control  
and under Kṛṣṇa’s direction.

How to please and serve the Lord  
in so many different ways.  
You took exactly into consideration  
our circumstance, time, and place.

You freely gave us the holy name  
if only we would hear.  
You taught us details of spiritual life.  
The price? A submissive ear.

You taught us the meaning of bhakti,  
the best path for all mankind,  
the path to perfect happiness in life,  
which we could never find.

And so although we were blinded  
and always led astray,  
you came to us to guide us  
and turn our night to day.

For this we are ever grateful  
and resolved in all we do  
to offer our lives at your lotus feet  
and surrender unto you.

Your most incapable servants,

## Austin

**Homage to Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Stepping off the Jaladuta ship  
You may gave appeared like a refugee,  
King of kings and lord of lords  
Dressed in perfect humility.

History to be rewritten  
By the movement of your pen;  
How the world fell without a shot  
Into the hands of sinless men.

Challenging insurmountable dynasties of Kali  
With japa beads in your hand,  
Your army was not recruited  
As you declared war on Māyā’s land.

You caused treason in the ranks of sinners  
And we deserted to join you;  
Knowledge burned the arsenals of ignorance;  
Everywhere the flag of Kṛṣṇa flew  
Across the oceans of impersonalism  
Like an admiral you set sail,  
Leaving the sharks of atheistic theories  
In the jaws of Vaiṣṇava whales.

Whit forty rupees and a trunk of books  
Your wealth may have been disguised  
As you brought our ransom into part  
Beneath unsuspecting eyes.

From the bowers of Vṛndāvana  
To the Bowery slums  
You smuggled the fruits of love of God  
And gave them freely to everyone.

Your name and fame and pastimes  
Become sweeter as we chew  
Your sublime instructions, born of love,  
And kindly left by you.

Please bless us with surrender  
To the sweet will of God,  
That we may return back to home  
And serve you purely, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Begging to remain your servants,

## Austria

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ*  
*paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande*  
*śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

*he guro jñānada dīna-bandho*  
*svānanda-dātaḥ karuṇaika-sindho*  
*vṛndāvanāsīna hitāvatāra*  
*prasīda rādhā-praṇaya-pracāra*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

At least this one time in the year, on the occasion of offering our homage unto you on this day of your divine appearance in this world, we are forced to face the reality – how poor our spiritual life still is, and how little we have developed our love and devotion for you. It gives us pain to realize how all year we are engaged in so many activities under the heading of “Kṛṣṇa consciousness” (and, I guess many times under different headings too), and still we are not able to find the proper words to rightfully glorify you.

A first-class disciple is known by his ability to fulfill the desires of his spiritual master even prior to the guru’s expressing them. We have to admit that we do not even know all of your desires and instructions laid down in your books, what to speak of fulfilling and following them. Yet there is no use in lamenting; there is no one to but us. Now, if we take up your mission, Śrīla Prabhupāda, by pushing forward your movement to all corners of this globe, surely, although we are deaf and dumb, our lives will have some value.

There is no limit to this ISKCON movement, there is no limit to the spreading of this ISKCON movement, there is no limit to the distribution of your books, and there is no limit to the conditioned souls waiting to receive your mercy through the preaching efforts of this ISKCON movement. Surely our task and labor of preaching, making programs and plans to bring the fallen souls of this planet in touch with Kṛṣṇa consciousness, will never find an end, will never come to a close. And definitely we will never be able to repay our debt to you. But still, as you have kindly told us, by trying to preach as vigorously as you did (although we will never be able to even remotely come close to your most glorious example) we will be able to please you. And that pleasure of yours is the ultimate goal of our lives.

All we finally want to say is this: Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for conducting us into this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Thank you for giving us a place as servants of your pure servant under the shelter of your lotus feet. Please allow us to continuously serve you by spreading your mission to every person in this country. Kindly excuse us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for being uneducated, misbehaved, sentimental, and foolish, but please engage us eternally in your devotional service, life after life. We wish to close this humble offering with your own words:

Had you not come  
Who had told  
The message of Kṛṣṇa  
Forceful and bold  
That’s your right  
You have the mace.

Save me a fallen  
Your Divine Grace.

*Your fallen servants of the Austrian Yātrā,*

## Baltimore

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this most blessed anniversary of your appearance day.

This year has marked the publication of Volume II of *Śrīla Prabhupāda-līlāmṛta*, the authorized biography of your pastimes on the planet earth. By your causeless mercy, you have remained with us in the form of your divine *vāṇī* and in the form of the chronicle of your divine *līlā*.

As part of our evening class, we are now regularly reading the narration of your pastimes, along with the study of your instructions, and are thus feeling your personal presence very intensely. Hearing of your “glorious, selfless adventures on behalf of the Supreme Lord” eases the numbing pain of separation from you and tightens the bonds of attachment to your lotus feet. of separation from you and tightens the bonds of attachment to your lotus feet.

As we struggle to carry forward the mission of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement under the guidance of your eternal instructions, the hearing and chanting of your *līlāmṛta* is “the nectarine of life” and the source of all our vitality.

*Your disciples and granddisciples,*

## Benelux

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On behalf of all the devotees at Śrī Rādhā-deśa dhāma, please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. May that dust be the goal of our lives.

On this most auspicious day of your appearance we are meditating upon you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We are meditating on your graceful, golden body and your blissful face with its constant expression of love for Kṛṣṇa. We are meditating on the way you sit on the *vyāsāsana*, singing “Jaya Rādhā-Mādhava” with great ecstasy. We are remembering your personality, so kind and compassionate, so enthusiastic and jolly. We are thinking of your wonderful activities, motivated by a pure desire to please your Guru Mahārāja. Now we are thinking of your divine instructions. These instructions are nondifferent from yourself; therefore we must take these instructions within our hearts and make them our life and soul. We are still very dull-headed, but if we learn to serve you properly, then we will be able to hear you attentively.

By meditating upon you, we discover the perfect *guru*, friend, father, and pure devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda, your birth and activities in this world were all auspicious and have brought great fortune into our lives. We want to assist you in your mission, but unfortunately we have none of your exalted qualities. We are still struggling to follow the basic principles of spiritual life. We are so much infected by this demoniac Western culture that we are deprived of the nectar of love for Kṛṣṇa. How can we get purified of this contamination, this vile, infectious disease, this epidemic of materialistic life which is consuming our whole society? How can we avoid being also consumed? Only by your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, by hearing the words emanating from your lotus mouth. By carefully studying your books and serving you with faith, our consciousness is becoming gradually purified. We are still in a very sick condition, but we are trying to get well so that we may serve you nicely. We want to become good disciples, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We want that you are proud of us as a father is proud of his children. But in our present condition we are an embarrassment.

Yours is the most ambitious project in the history of modern civilization – to lead the world out of the darkness of the Kali-yuga. Now you have left us with the responsibility to fulfill this plan of yours. We pray most fervently that we may be successful in our preaching so that you will be pleased by our service.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg to take shelter at your lotus feet. What can we do to serve you now? Shall we sing or dance or preach? We are your insignificant servants, ready to execute your every desire. All we ask is that you please help us to stop this *saṁsāra*. On this auspicious day and for the rest of our lives may our minds be always fixed on you. You are actually the goal of our lives, because wherever you are, that is where Kṛṣṇa is. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we miss you so much our hearts are aching for your association. Please accept our service in separation.

Your servants,

Rādhā-deśa Chateau

## Berkeley

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dear spiritual father, Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. On this most glorious of days, your appearance day, we bag you to please bestow your causeless mercy upon us, so that this small attempt to please your will be successful. The Supreme Personality of Godhead is completely transcendental, totally pure, and free from any influence of the three modes of material nature. As one can enter fire only by assuming fiery qualities, similarly, only a cent-percent pure devotee can approach the supremely pure person, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are qualitatively one with God in that your only desire is to fulfill the desire of the Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa. The spiritual master is known as the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead.

Generally, when a spark separates itself from the original fire, it becomes extinguished. Despite your leaving Goloka Vṛndāvana, your effulgence, Śrīla Prabhupāda, has mystically increased day by day. Within ten years the whole world was chanting and dancing in the spotlight of your pure devotion. You descended from the absolute spiritual realm, Kṛṣṇaloka, because Kṛṣṇa asked you and because you were willing to do anything to please your dearmost friend, Lord Govinda. Who can imagine what separation you must have felt?

Actually, you brought the entire spiritual world with you, because all your activities were imbued with pure devotion and performed simply to please Lord Kṛṣṇa. By your spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world more than any previous *ācārya*, we can understand that Lord Kṛṣṇa must be extremely satisfied with your selflessly pure devotional service. As your disciples and granddisciples, being completely conditioned souls, we are not qualified to approach God. Whereas you can see Kṛṣṇa personally and speak directly with Him, we cannot see beyond our gross bodily identities and unlimited sinful desires. We are so contaminated and impure that Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu would have immediately jumped in the Ganges upon glancing at our lust-filled faces. But out of your causeless mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you glanced at us, danced with us, give us your complete association in body, mind, and words, so that we could somehow or other become purified and qualified to serve Kṛṣṇa.

By the mercy of the spiritual master, one receives the benediction of Lord Kṛṣṇa. In our material covered state, we can’t directly serve the supreme spiritual person, Lord Kṛṣṇa. But by your kindness, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can render devotional service to you. Then, if you are satisfied with our small offering, you will purify it and give that service to Kṛṣṇa. And when Kṛṣṇa is satisfied, all His parts and parcels share the spiritual bliss. Pleasing one’s spiritual master through humble service is the prescribed method of qualifying oneself to again associate with the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Kṛṣṇa, in eternal life, full of knowledge and bliss.

A devotee’s whole life should be centered around pleasing his spiritual master and nothing else. In all our devotional activities (book distribution, cooking, Deity worship, honoring *prasāda*, associating with the Vaiṣṇavas), we should think: Would Śrīla Prabhupāda be pleased with what I’m thinking right now, what I’m doing right now, or what I’m saying at this moment? We can’t see or talk to Kṛṣṇa. But we can serve you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. And even this concession is your greatest kindness to us. You are so willing to avail yourself to us, risking everything just to accept our fault-filled service. If we can just push our selfish desires aside for a few minutes and carry out your orders, then maybe we can please you. And when that Vaikuṇṭha smile breaks across your lotus face, we will know that Lord Govinda has accepted our feeble attempt to please you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please pick us up again and again and fasten us firmly to your lotus feet.

*Your disciples and granddisciples in New Jagannātha Purī*

## Bhaktivedanta Ashram – Sydney

**To His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda**

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

PARA-DUḤKHA-DUḤKHĪ:  
Distressed in the distress of others.

PARA-SUKHA-SUKHĪ:  
Happy in the happiness of others.  
The whole world is looking for you,  
who are expounder of the truth,  
to end our distress and feeling blue.  
You are the only one who sincerely cares,  
giving us all the jewel of love of God,  
taking us to the cintāmaṇi fair.

SUHṚDAṀ SARVA-BHŪTĀNAM:  
You are the friend of every living being,  
you are the ever well-wisher.  
You are the sun of knowledge, rāja-vidyā rāja-guhyam,  
that lights up this dark place.  
Out of the darkness of this material world  
You have come to shed light and hope.  
No amount of praise could ever be enough  
to even touch upon your glories.  
Your life was totally exemplary, and every  
moment was dedicated to the desire of Śrī  
Caitanya Mahāprabhu.  
You had no time other than for Him;  
each action you performed would verify this.  
To spread the eternal message of Śrī Kṛṣṇa  
was the theme,  
And to shatter māyā’s covering was your dream.  
If it was not to achieve this end,  
then any activity you would not condone.  
To rescue all conditioned souls  
and to engage them in devotional service  
was your chief desire.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the greatest preacher  
of bhāgavata-dharma.  
May your glories be broadcast all over the three  
worlds and chanted by all living entities within them.

Bhaktivedanta Ashram – Sydney

## Bhaktivedanta Institute

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. One hundred and eight glories to the topmost wandering minstrel, swanlike devotee of the Lord, Your Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda, at whose feet we have taken shelter though we are shamelessly fallen. Like the swan who can take milk from water, you know how to take whatever ounce of goodness may be in those who are among the most rejected persons of this world. Thus we are inseparably attached to you.

Separation from you is too unbearable, therefore we must constantly feel that you are always here, though we know you have left this world. What can we do? This feeling of love is the root cause of all our eagerness to serve you, for how else can love be expressed except by our efforts to fulfill your desires?

Though the people of this world hardly notice it, your Institute is effectively carrying our the sacred high-level work which you entrusted to it. By the wonderful influence of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, a Nobel Laureate has surrendered and chanted Hare Kṛṣṇa with us, honored *prasāda*, and publicly declared to the world that matter comes from life. “Absolute is sentient thou hast proved,” you wrote to your spiritual master. By your inconceivable mercy this man completely reversed his former position, in which he held that “it is part of the dignity of man that atoms could know themselves.” Certainly your boundless joy at this news has filled the entire universe and is threatening to break the covering which confine us in the material world. Yet even more, *Scientific American*, the paragon of scientific materialism in the world, has, for the first time in its history, been forced to accept that quantum mechanics requires human consciousness to be part and parcel of reality. “Impersonal calamity thou hast moved.” Only the devotees of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu can understand how all these things are happening.

We are continuing to hold conferences and are meeting scientists all over the world, distributing books and *prasāda*. You have said that anyone who can make the student community Kṛṣṇa conscious will definitely see Kṛṣṇa, so we are vigorously continuing our preaching and by your mercy meeting with great success. You have patiently taught us that the pride of intellectualism can never defeat one who is allowed to serve the lotus feet of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Please bless us that we may never become mere actors in so many different roles, but based on genuine scientific understanding, let us steadily progress on the path of pure devotional service, though it may take millions of lifetimes.

Your utterly insignificant servants of Bhaktivedanta Institute,

Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Swami / Ātreya Ṛṣi dāsa adhikārī / Pūrṇaprajñā dāsa adhikārī / Garuḍa dāsa adhikārī / Haladhāra dāsa adhikārī / Ravīndra-svarūpa dāsa adhikārī / Sadāpūta dāsa adhikārī / Mādhava dāsa brahmacārī / Dayādhara dāsa brahmacārī / Bhāgavata-darśana-ācārya dāsa vanacārī / Rasānanda dāsa brahmacārī / Viśuddha-sattva dāsa brahmacārī / Yajña dāsa brahmacārī

## Bhaktivedanta Swami Guru-kula

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

If it is at all possible to reciprocate your love for your disciples, to whom you have given, without remuneration, the purpose and mission of human life, Kṛṣṇa consciousness, then grant us the intelligence to know your desire, fulfill your instructions, and glorify you properly. If within your *guru-kula* we learn and teach these things, then our education will be successful. Kindly accept our offering, although it can never be sufficient, because your glories are unlimited.

Your pure devotion to Kṛṣṇa and resolute surrender to the Vaiṣṇava paramparā – this wonderful energy of devotion is pervading your astounding accomplishments. You are the topmost Vaiṣṇava, exhibiting in full all the qualities of a pure devotee. You are the most expert personality, master of all the arts and sciences of preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We can relish your *bhakti* through your prolific writing of Vaiṣṇava literature. If our hearts are stonelike, still they are pierced with the ecstasy of your song as you serve as the spiritual maestro of Vaiṣṇava sound.

You are the world teacher of Lord Caitanya’s golden age, and as you are His emissary, the whole world will become your *guru-kula*, where all living entities will reside humbly like slaves, only for your benefit, and therefore achieve the mercy of Kṛṣṇa.

Both students and teachers are your children, and as you are our spiritual father, we are feeling secure in your love as adherence to your absolute instructions is bearing the fruit of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

In so many ways your love for Kṛṣṇa, which we are lacking, is emanating from your person, and in this way, although we have a foolish desire to escape spiritual life, you keep us entrapped in the network of love which you have expanded in your society of Kṛṣṇa consciousness and in your guru-kula.

*Your servants,*

Guru-kula teachers

## Bhaktivedanta Swami Guru-kula Student Offerings

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble prayer. Although I am incapable of explaining your glories, still, due to your greatness, I must on your holy Vyāsa-pūjā day at least say something, out of obligation. I am very obligated to you, because you did what no one else dared to do. You went to the Western countries just to save us fallen souls. If you had not come to America, I would still be a nondevotee, experiencing the miseries of material life. You are the most compassionate and selfless person in the universe. You left such a beautiful place as Vṛndāvana, and the even more beautiful Rādhā-Dāmodara Mandira. You gave up the calm atmosphere of Vṛndāvana so suitable for meditation: the peacocks, parrots and monkeys, the beautiful courtyard with the *samādhi* of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, and above all, the wonderful inhabitants of Vṛndāvana. You gave up all this, not for yourself, but to save the fallen souls. This is not a very hard thing to give up for the materialist, but for the *sādhu* it is the most difficult place to give up.

*na ca tasmān manuṣyeṣu*  
*kaścin me priya-kṛttamaḥ*  
*bhavitā na ca me tasmād*  
*anyaḥ priyataro bhuvi*

**“There is no servant in this world more dear to Me than he, not will there ever be one more dear.” (Bg. 18.69)**

As it is stated in this verse, the most dear servant of the Lord is the preacher. I therefore know that you are one of the most beloved devotees of the Lord. I hope you accept this humble offering of mine, and I hope that someday, by your transforming mercy, I will be able to become a worthy disciple of Your Divine Grace.

Your servant and disciple,  
Līlā-smaraṇa dāsa (age 17)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

O dear Gurudeva, Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your lotus feet again and again. You are most merciful and kind to all the living entities who are suffering at every single step and are drowned in the ocean of birth and death.

There are millions of living entities who have become conditioned by the laws of material nature, and they are wandering throughout the planetary systems of this universe in different bodily forms.

*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, the deliverer of the fallen souls. His mercy turns the dumb into eloquent speakers and enables the lame to cross mountains.”**

Therefore, the mercy of Śrīla Prabhupāda is still coming through his transcendental books. Śrīla Prabhupāda is present in his instructions and is directing us. We just have to have faith in him and serve his instructions as our life and soul.

Your most fallen and insignificant servant,  
Prabhāva dāsa (age 17)

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I was born in the darkness of ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.”**

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

I Offer my most prostrated obeisances unto your lotus feet. As you know, I am the most fallen. You please kindly give me one drop of your mercy so that I can glorify you on this most auspicious day of your appearance.

I was entangled in the false concept of material life, and somehow or other you mercifully picked me up from this miserable place. You were not obliged to come and preach and to accept so many troubles, to come to the Western countries and deliver so many souls, but because you were dedicated to the order of your spiritual master and Kṛṣṇa, therefore you were ready to accept any difficulty to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Because you are the most dear servant of Kṛṣṇa, your fame is spread throughout the three worlds, and your fame will continue to exist. You have unflinching faith in and devotion to Kṛṣṇa, and therefore you have all the good qualities of the demigods. This is stated in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (5.18.12):

*yasyāsti bhaktir bhagavaty akiñcanā*  
*sarvair guṇais tatra samāsate surāḥ*  
*harāv abhaktasya kuto mahad-guṇā*  
*manorathenāsati dhāvato bahiḥ*

**“One who has unflinching devotion for the Supreme Personality of Godhead has all the good qualities of the demigods. But one who is not a devotee of the Lord has only material qualifications, which have little value. This is because hi is hovering on the mental plane.”**

So many so-called big leaders and spiritualists had so many material qualifications, and so many people became attracted to them, but presently we can see that they are no longer popular. They had no spiritual qualifications, and all their qualifications were of no value. You were, therefore, the only bona fide representative of Kṛṣṇa, because you were simply preaching the pure message of Caitanya Mahāprabhu, without any self-motivation.

Even in your physical absence we should try to carry on your mission of preaching to the conditioned souls. You are still present with us through your instructions. I should simply try to take your instructions to me as my life and soul. Otherwise, of what use is my life? Please allow me to serve you.

Your humble servant,  
Dvārakādhīśa dāsa (age 17)

## Bogotá

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Falling in the dust of your transcendental lotus feet and praying for your eternal benediction, we would like to offer this poem on this auspicious day of the opening of your pastimes in this world.

As the air  
picks up different smells  
in different bodies,  
my consciousness was hell.

I thought I was rich,  
very famous and strong,  
but I was serving the witch.  
My desires were wrong.

This world is illusion,  
you kindly have shown.  
The ga-ga-ga of the children.  
The production keeps going.

It was by your love  
you have opened our eyes.  
Let us help you to save them –  
you are preaching so nicely.

All of Dakṣa’s sons  
were highly qualified.  
Then Nārada Muni  
cut their last attachment-ties.

But when you reached us –  
stoned, drunk, and lazy –  
you gave us prasāda;  
the effect was amazing.

When we shaved our heads  
and our dhotīs were tied,  
we felt the new life.  
Your smile was so bright.

They thought we were crazy,  
changing sex and money  
for serving the guru,  
they taking stool to be honey.

Just to imitate a dog  
in the nightclub they are playing.  
In the street they kick the dogs,  
and the cows they are slaying.

The scientist shows the public  
what already exists,  
then he receives the Nobel prize –  
“God does not exist.”

You defeated the rascals –  
you discovered they cheat.  
They claim to be scholars,  
taking drugs, eating meat.

If a man is not pure  
all he does is exploit.  
Communist and capitalist –  
they are all dacoits.

All the bhogī-yogīs  
tell the karmīs they’re God:  
“Just do what you like;  
put your checks in this pot.”

But you have proven to us  
that they are wrong.  
Without paramparā-guru  
one will never be strong.

O spiritual master,  
please guide us forever.  
Māyā and envy are strong;  
we are in their winds like a feather.

Your pure disciples are  
our only hope.  
By serving your servants  
one unties māyā’s rope.

Your nectarean words  
and glancing smiles  
fill our hearts,  
give us new life.

Wherever you are,  
we’d like to be,  
to drink the nectar of your lotus feet.

Whoever met you  
can never forget.  
Whoever served you  
will never regret.

Whoever accepted your lotus feet  
will surely be freed from lust and greed.  
Whoever made you his life and soul –  
soon he’ll be with you, the highest goal.

O spiritual master,  
never leave us alone.  
Reveal in our hearts  
what you want us to do.

By serving you  
and accepting the śāstra,  
our spiritual life  
will be saved from disaster.

How could anybody  
really understand you?  
Your heart is so soft,  
like the morning dew.

Even when we were falling,  
you picked us up.  
A devotee who falls  
is like a jewel uncut.

Out of our sight  
your instructions shine brightly  
to enlighten our way.

We never can pay  
your oceans of mercy  
for hearts that are dirty.

Thus you have saved us  
from eternal decay.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda! Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your fallen disciples in Columbia remain, their hearts filled with separation.*

## Bolivia

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Three long years have passed since you left – three hard years in which I have had to learn to live without your company. It is said that a Vaiṣṇava dies to live, but it is hard to understand this when it is about our own spiritual master, our true father. It is also said that the pure devotee is even more merciful than Kṛṣṇa, for he gives Kṛṣṇa’s mercy to all, without distinction. Because of this mercy, I was able to meet you and serve you. Then you left. But please do not go from my heart. Let me stay under the refuge of your lotus feet.

Daily, *māyā* plays with me as if I were a puppet, showing me as nectar what is only poison. Only you are free of all cheating. You make *māyā* fail. Only you can liberate me from her influence.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! When will I place my heart completely at your feet? When will I understand that no material attraction is real? When will I understand that material pleasure is only chewing the chewed?

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! Your infinite mercy is raining on the whole planet as your transcendental literature! You are the empowered incarnation of Lord Caitanya who has introduced the revolution of consciousness in every corner of the world.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! Only by your grace will the future generations find an authentic refuge from the increasing miseries of this age of Kali. Your power and mercy are so clearly manifested in these eleven *gurus*, who are representing you so nicely!

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! Let my heart merge in the effulgence of your smile! Let this stonelike heart melt in the sweetness of your words! Let this mind full of animal propensities understand a little of the greatness of your personality.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! My eternal father, you are my only refuge and the refuge of all suffering humanity. You are the humble servant of Bhaktisiddhānta. Give me enough intelligence to be an aspirant of a humble servant at your lotus feet! O Śrīla Prabhupāda! How and when will my falseness go away in order for me to be a sincere disciple? As time passes, difficulties grow, and I feel more helpless, like a simple little bird in a great storm. If I get some ability, it is only because of Your Divine Grace.

O Your Divine Grace! You are never absent from us. You are more present than ever in your teachings, which are destroying the hearts of stone of all the materialists of the world.

O Your Divine Grace! I wish I could have the same capacity to serve you as to lament. We will never be able to evaluate how fortunate we were to have had your association, which cannot be obtained by the richest of most powerful person, but only by the causeless mercy of the pure devotee of the Lord. By your association we have been changed from animals into Vaiṣṇavas. Our debt is so great that we could not repay you even by having thousands and thousands of lives.

O Your Divine Grace! I know I do not deserve to be in your service, but please do not let me become entrapped by the illusory energy! Give me the refuge of the shadow of your lotus feet, for without it I am completely useless and without hope if getting out of the puzzle of lust.

O Your Divine Grace! on this day of your appearance, I humbly ask from the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa the benediction of having enough intelligence to remain always in this movement, bringing the message of *Bhagavad-gītā* and your instructions to all the fallen souls that need your mercy. I am sure that now you are so near to Kṛṣṇa, you are overseeing even more directly this movement. Thank you, Your Divine Grace!

*Your useless servants,*  
The residents of Bolivia Mandira

## Bombay

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our only hope.

Without your guidance, mankind can only stumble along  
The thorny path of Kali’s night of ignorance  
Without any knowledge of the goal of life,  
Suffering eternally in the clutches  
Of Māyā, madly struggling  
To find safety and happiness in a world of illusion.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! We were too near to you to estimate your greatness.

Just as Vasudeva and Devakī could not understand Kṛṣṇa’s greatness,  
So our intimacy with you hid from us  
Your exalted position.

Who is there in history who can compare to you?

Who, even among the divine incarnations,  
Has done what you have done?

According to learned scholars, Kṛṣṇa’s greatest quality  
Is His mercy in delivering the fallen souls.

Who has displayed this mercy liberally than you?

Lord Caitanya, the most munificent incarnation of Kṛṣṇa,  
Is celebrated for having delivered Jagāi and Mādhāi,  
But you have delivered hundreds and thousands of Jagāis and Mādhāis.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the parama-guru, the ideal spiritual master.

No one else in history has displayed  
The extreme mercy, the tolerance, the compassion,  
The humility, the deep love and the unflinching faith in Kṛṣṇa and guru  
That you have done.

Therefore, we feel we are not exaggerating  
When we say that you are the greatest of all.

You are even greater than Kṛṣṇa, because  
Kṛṣṇa likes His devotee to be greater than Himself.

This is real love, and you demonstrated this reciprocation perfectly.

How is it possible that, year after year,  
You tolerated our constant offensiveness?

You yourself admitted it was like trying to wash coal.

But still, you never faltered in your vision  
Of a true Vaiṣṇava community based on love and trust,  
And therefore, before you left, you made us promise  
That everything would go on nicely in ISKCON:  
“Your love for me will be shown by how much you cooperate with each other.”

In your last public talk in Bombay,  
In the spring of 1977,  
You spoke to us on *divya jñāna hṛde prokāśita:*  
“He fills my heart with transcendental knowledge.”

You revealed that, in this way, your spiritual master  
Was always with you, guiding you from within,  
And you appealed to us to understand practically  
The meaning of this crucial line which we sing every morning at guru-pūjā.

But we could not understand your intention.

We could not accept that you would be leaving us soon,  
That you were begging us to awaken our spiritual consciousness,  
Our perception of you within our hearts.

Caught up in the externals of devotional life,  
We could not really enter into  
The spirit of Vaiṣṇava existence.

But with your divinely ordained and perfectly timed disappearance,  
We suddenly found ourselves forced to open our spiritual eyes.

With the sense of responsibility you had imbued  
In our hitherto careless hearts,  
We realized that we had to rise to meet the situation,  
To rise above all external and apparent differences  
And see each other with the eyes of divine love,  
As Vaiṣṇavas, transcendental servants of Kṛṣṇa.

Thus, in your disappearance also you taught us  
And brought us to a new level of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please forgive us for all our offenses.

We are neophyte Vaiṣṇavas, if we can claim to be Vaiṣṇavas at all.

We have no understanding of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.  
We could not enter into the mysteries of pure love of Kṛṣṇa,  
Having been so contaminated by a materialistic civilization  
Centered on and wholly absorbed in sense gratification,  
And not having been serious enough of sincere enough  
To become free from its pernicious influence.

You taught us that a pure devotee is free from all material desire,  
And said that your real mystic power was the greatest one,  
That you could sit in a room full of beautiful women  
And not become in the least agitated.

Confident of your sublime instructions,  
Which have never failed us,  
We are proceeding to follow your direction  
And example as best we can,  
And we have no doubt that one day –  
Even if it be only after millions of births  
Of suffering Māyā’s kicks – we have no doubt that one day,  
By your inconceivable mercy,  
We shall also come to that platform  
Of real, pure love of Kṛṣṇa, and  
In a spiritual body decorated with all ecstatic symptoms,  
We will dance and live with Kṛṣṇa,  
In complete absence of material consciousness,  
Immersed in full awareness of Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we want to serve you.

Although it seems impossible to accomplish,  
Still the intense desire is burning in our hearts  
To become pure devotees of Kṛṣṇa,  
Because only then can we properly serve you.

Only then could we carry out the great task that  
You have entrusted to us –  
To create a civilization based on spiritual values  
In the midst of Kali-yuga,  
And thus provide a shelter in which all the world  
Will find it natural and simple to love Kṛṣṇa.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg of you,  
Please, please, please let us serve you!  
Let us distribute your books.

Let us preach your most scientific message.

Let us develop ISKCON farm communities everywhere.  
Let us train millions of devotees.

Let us work together under the guidance of you GBC,  
Unbreakably united by our love for you,  
In spiritual association with you,  
So that the entire universe will surrender to you,  
And thus automatically become happy by chanting  
Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare,  
Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

Your servants of ISKCON Bombay,

## Boston

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your soothing feet thousands of times, and again, and again. All glories showered upon you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Whatever devotion we have for you in our hearts has been created by you by your mere presence in our lives; yet so scanty is that devotion compared to its deserve. May our entire devotion to you, on this day of your appearance, be properly focused on your glories. Then, in turn, may that properly focused devotion be increased to deserving proportions. Uncountable multiplications must be executed to capture such deserving devotion – again, possibly by your grace alone. Is it within our power? And then, most importantly, may that devotion never escape us but rather manifest forever in loving service to you, our beloved spiritual master, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

As our devotion to you began by your entering our lives – truly causelessly, even uninvited by our wretched selves, but on the order of your Guru Mahārāja to deliver us all – so also, by your appearance on this Vyāsa-pūjā day you have brought devotion and service to Kṛṣṇa into the lives of millions, again by your mere presence. This is your grace.

And even greater grace is your books, for now the mark of your appearance is indelibly left. In this way you will go on for centuries appearing in the lives of fortunate souls, giving them knowledge of Kṛṣṇa, devotion to Kṛṣṇa, love of Kṛṣṇa. The Kṛṣṇa movement thus marches on, with the fearless Śrīla Prabhupāda still in the lead! Others may not see you so readily, leading us; but we know that by your entrance into our world and into our lives you will always remain with us. Your appearance means that you will stay forever with those who serve you. Such is the quality of love you distribute – truly eternal. And by your thus remaining with us, our devotion only grows, just as it began by your appearance in our lives. Eternal and ever-increasing love: who else can offer this? Our lovable object is so perfect.

We have reserved a very special place for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, befitting Your Divine Grace. It is to be found within our hearts and within our service – that very special place that nobody else could possibly occupy. Upon the resolve to avoid all offenses in chanting the holy name, we have made a fit place for you to occupy. And we come bearing you gifts, the best of all of them being a world more Kṛṣṇa conscious. This is our dedication to you on this Vyāsa-pūjā day. To this end we have all courage and confidence, due to our employing the same formulas you have given us. Your instructions, your books, your worship, your mission – you have fully equipped us to carry out your service. What you have given us is perfect; you have given us yourself, upon your appearance. There is no limit to your mercy.

As your disciples, we are now caring for you in this way, laboring to propagate your teachings unchanged, all the while keeping you within this very special place within our heart. This is our strength. Don’t let us stray. Your mercy is thus unlimited.

Your servants of Boston ISKCON,
