## Calcutta

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

The universe has no existence without the presence of the sun, similarly, for us this world has no existence without you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. As the sun illuminates the three worlds, with all the living entities from Lord Brahmā down to the *indragopa* insect, similarly, you infuse the light of transcendental knowledge in the dark, impenetrable hearts of all conditioned souls.

The delicate touch of your lotus feet is the perfection for which mother earth performs penances; the transcendental sound emanating from your lotus lips is the sweet perfection ether longs to embrace; the cooling glance of your lotuslike eyes is the perfection for which Lord Brahmā’s creation has manifest herself in subtle hues. Your divine presence gives meaning to everything; you are like the transcendental touchstone, which dovetails everything in Lord Kṛṣṇa’s service. Thousands of scientists and philosophers are relentlessly trying to delve into the secret of life, but they are bound to eternally taste defeat unless they surrender unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda – the personified revelation of the Absolute Truth.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so expert. In this age of Kali-yuga everyone’s heart is like a desert, barren and unyielding, but due to your causeless mercy, millions of luxuriant devotional creepers are growing, unchecked, in these parched hearts.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are eternally present in your books. Pray, heave our lust-laden consciousness to sublime spiritual service at the lotus feet of the supremely beatific Lord Govinda.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have entered those hearts who rushed to you, they pressing you lotus feet in warm ecstatic embrace, never ever having met another simply beautiful personality like you.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, how can reason and erudition reciprocate to the unreserved love you showered on us, which crept up to warm our hearts with a blissful feeling, making us touch the moon?

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, your childlike chuckle, deep vibrant voice, you noble, swanlike bearing, impeccable nose, up in the air, your walking with short, still steps – your walking-stick, how fortunate!

On this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, the devotees of Calcutta Yātrā prostrate themselves before your lotus feet, holding straw in the teeth. They nurture one hope in their hearts, that one day the world will awaken from its sinful slumber and behold this city of Calcutta, which has given birth to the mercy incarnation, the most illustrious disciple of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura Prabhupāda. All glories to Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Jagad-guru Abhaya Caraṇāravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda.

## Carriere

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace, our eternal father and protector. We are not very good at composing literary articles, and we feel very dull-headed when there is the occasion to praise you. However we feel compelled to make an effort to glorify your wonderful personality, even though we are not qualified to do so.

So great is your spiritual influence upon us that we remain engaged in your work year after year, despite all obstacles and distractions of *māyā*. But on our own, we would have soon forgotten our real business of serving Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Lord. So to remind us at every moment, you have given us this sublime ISKCON movement, with its purified devotees, benedicting Deities, philosophical literatures, strengthening *prasāda*, marble palaces, ecstatic festivals, grave responsibilities, chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa, happy demigod children, rural communities, simple devotional chores, and absorbing hard work. Everything in Kṛṣṇa consciousness came from you, so it should not be difficult to remember you always, Śrīla Prabhupāda. But, due to weakness, our memory is failing, and we pray that you help us in this way. Bless us that we always have the intimate association of your disciples and the devotees of your ISKCON movement, and in this position we hope to be of some use to Your Divine Grace in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness around this planet earth.

Your servants at New Tālavana,

## Chandigarh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this auspicious day of Nandotsava, the day following Janmāṣṭamī, in the year 1896, on order of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, you made your appearance in this world, at Calcutta, in the family of a pure devotee, Gour Mohan De. Your appearance is just to save the fallen souls. What else could be the mission of one fully absorbed in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, whose only desire is the satisfaction of Lord Kṛṣṇa?

Our great-grandfather and spiritual preceptor, Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, predicted that soon a devotee would come to spread the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu all over the world to every town and village. And it was in the year of your appearance that Ṭhākura Bhaktivinoda sent his book *Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu: His Life and Precepts*, a short work in the English language presenting Kṛṣṇa consciousness, for the first time in Western countries, to McGill University. And this was a devotional service which you were later to expand into numerous volumes of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, a seventeen-volume presentation of *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta*, and many other books and essays. Who is there fully qualified to understand the activities of the pure devotee of the Lord?

And most glorious of all is your unprecedented example, how with great faith and determination one should carry out the orders of his spiritual master. Your pure devotion at the lotus feet of your Divine Master 108 Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Prabhupāda is the beacon of achievement for all your loving disciples desiring to cross this vast ocean of ignorance.

Remembering your every instruction to preach this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement under the banner of ISKCON with great enthusiasm, we are finding great solace even in your absence. Indeed, following your orders the servant lives eternally with you. How fortunate we are to be your eternal servants.

In honor of your appearance we would like to offer this song, written by Śrīla Bhaktivinoda, Ohe Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākura, with hope that you will bless us with greater and greater success in our preaching work and remain our lord and master eternally.

**Ohe! Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākura**

*(1) ohe!*  
*vaiṣṇava ṭhākura, doyāra sāgara,*  
*e dāse koruṇā kori’*  
*diyā pada-chāyā, śodho he āmāya,*  
*tomāra caraṇa dhori*

*(2) chaya bega domi, chaya doṣa śodhi’,*  
*chaya guṇa deho’ dāse*  
*chaya sat-saṅga, deho’ he āmāre,*  
*boshechi saṅgera āśe*

*(3) ekākī āmāra, nāhi pāya bala,*  
*hari-nāma-saṅkīrtane*  
*tumi kṛpā kori’, śraddhā-bindu diyā,*  
*deho’ kṛṣṇa-nāma-dhane*

*(4) kṛṣṇa se tomāra, kṛṣṇa dite pāro,*  
*tomāra śakati āche*  
*āmi to’ kāṅgāla, ‘kṛṣṇa’ ‘kṛṣṇa’ boli’,*  
*dhāi tava pāche pāche*

**(1) O Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākura, O ocean of mercy, be merciful unto me, your servant, and purify me by the shade of your lotus feet. Your feet I humbly hold.**

**2) Controlling my six urges and purifying me of the six faults, please give to your servant the six good qualities. Oh, give me the six kinds of holy association. I have sat down here in the hope of having your company.**

**3) Alone, I find I have no strength to chant the holy name of Lord Hari. I beg you, therefore, please be merciful, and with a particle of faith, give me the great treasure of the holy mane of Kṛṣṇa.**

**4) Kṛṣṇa is yours. You are able to give Him to me, for such is your power. I am indeed wretched and simply run after you, crying, “Kṛṣṇa! Kṛṣṇa!”**

Jaya Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākura! Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your eternal servants of Chandigarh Yātrā (Nṛsiṁha-purī),*

Trivikrama Svāmī / Jagad-guru Svāmī / Bhūmipati dāsa / Lakṣmaṇeśvara dāsa / Citraketu dāsa / Pruṣottama dāsa / Bharata dāsa / Dayal Singh / R.K. Gupta & family / L.S. Thakura & family / Mr. Kalra & family / and all the assembled devotees

## Chicago

*‘guru kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera pramāṇe*  
*guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā karena bhakta gaṇe’*

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees.” (Cc. Ādi 1.45)**

Most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

It was the summer of 1975 when I left Toronto for Chicago to obtain the dust of your lotus feet. The only desire I had was to touch the lotus feet of pure Vaiṣṇava who had done so much to spread the holy name of Kṛṣṇa throughout the world. I was informed by several devotees that it was very difficult to touch the lotus feet of Your Divine Grace.

Anyway, with that intense desire in my heart, I arrived with my family in Chicago. As soon as I parked my car outside Chicago temple, we were greeted by Jagadīśa prabhu, GBC, Minister of Education. Jagadīśa prabhu knew me from Toronto since 1969. He informed me that Your Divine Grace was staying in an apartment on Ridge Street and that *darśana* was in progress. Without wasting any time (not even coming inside the temple), we went to see you. I paid my *daṇḍavats* in the dust of your lotus feet; you were sitting on a cushion against the west wall. White sheets covered the floor of the small *darśana* room. You then showed me your causeless mercy and asked me to sit close to you. Your effulgence was so bright that I became speechless. I had never seen anything like this. You were so kind and merciful that you invited me to come to the darśana the following day.

The next day after *darśana*, I requested at your lotus feet that we could stay a little longer. You were so merciful even to a fallen person like me. You said in Hindi, “*Baḍiyā, baḍiyā* \[sit down, sit down\].” His Holiness Brahmānanda Svāmī was busy seeing off the other guests, so my wife and I were left alone with you in the room. I expressed to you that the reason for my coming to Chicago was nothing else except to touch the lotus feet of Your Divine Grace. You then benedicted me and my wife with this opportunity. This shows the greatness of the pure Vaiṣṇava like yourself, who fulfills the desires of condemned persons like me.

As stated in *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* (*Madhya* 22.54):

*‘sādhu-saṅga’, sādhu-saṅga’ – sarva-śāstre kaya*  
*lava-mātra sādhu-saṅge sarva-siddhi haya’*

**“The verdict of all revealed scriptures is that by even a moment’s association with a pure devotee, one can attain all success.”**

Now five years later, I find myself engaged in the most perfect activity of serving your lotus feet in New Sevākuñja (Chicago), where I first met Your Divine Grace.

Lord Śiva states to his wife,

*ārādhanānāṁ sarveṣāṁ viṣṇor ārādhanaṁ param*  
*tasmāt parataraṁ devi tadīyānāṁ samarcanam*

**“My dear Pārvatī, there are different methods of worship, and out of all such methods, the worship of the Supreme Person is considered to be the highest; but even higher than the worship of the Lord is the worship of the Lord’s devotee.”**

We therefore pray to you divine lotus feet that we may remain engaged in your service. Because you are a pure devotee of the Lord, everywhere you go you create benediction for all living entities by giving them the seed of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti*. Your instructions tu us as worshipable as your very self. Therefore, we repeatedly pray on this Vyāsa-pūjā day that you give us the strength to continually follow your divine instructions.

*Your worthless servants at Śrī Śrī Kiśora-Kiśorī Dhāma, New Sevākuñja (Chicago),*

## Christchurch

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ*  
*paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande*  
*śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, the deliverer of the fallen souls. His mercy turns the dumb into eloquent speakers and enables the lame to cross mountains.”**

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, who has opened my eyes, which were blinded by the darkness of ignorance, with the torchlight of knowledge.”**

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most auspicious and wonderful day. You appeared in this world, bringing the message of Lord Kṛṣṇa to the fallen conditioned souls. Boldly preaching, working day and night translating literature, establishing temples and farms, you arranged for books to be printed and distributed profusely. Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, could achieve all this. You had so much determination, even at the advanced age of seventy. Everyone else said, “NO,” but you said, “Yes, I must follow me Guru Mahārāja’s instructions to preach the message of Lord Kṛṣṇa to the Western world.” You fully accepted Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta’s instructions as your life and soul. We humbly pray that we may follow in your footsteps and take your instructions as our life and soul.

*aṁśo bhagavato ’smy ahaṁ sadā dāso ’smi sarvathā*  
*tat-kṛpāpekṣako nityaṁ tat-preṣṭha-sāt karomi svam*

**“I am a tiny part and parcel of the Supreme Lord and I am always and in every way His eternal servant. As I am always hoping for His mercy and kindness, I offer myself to His most dear servitor, my spiritual master.”**

Jaya oṁ Viṣṇu-pāda paramahaṁsa parivrājakācārya aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad Abhaya Caraṇāravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

*Your eternal servants,*

Aṣṭasiddhi dāsa adhikārī / Parivādī dāsa adhikārī / Nṛṣiṁha-prasāda dāsa brahmacārī / Jñānamūrti-devī dāsī, Hari-nāma / Rukmiṇī-devī dāsī / Jānakī-devī dāsī / Jāmbavatī-devī dāsī / Yamunā-mayī, Ayodhyādeva

## Costa Rica

*ohe!*  
*vaiṣṇava ṭhākura, doyāra sāgara,*  
*e dāse koruṇā kori’*  
*diyā pada-chāyā, śodho he āmāya,*  
*tomāra caraṇa dhori*

**“O venerable Vaiṣṇava, O ocean of mercy, be merciful unto your servant. Give me the shade of your lotus feet and purify me. I hold on to your lotus feet.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Nobody at this time would dare to say that you have left us, for certainly anybody who thinks like that reasons ill. Certainly you will live forever with us in your books. Every word, every syllable in them is a perfect composition of exquisite poetry from Vaikuṇṭha that has come to us like drops of nectar, softly falling from the lotus flower of your heart, to reveal to us the most sublime sense of our existence and to delight us with the highest spiritual pleasure.

“Now you have gone to join Lord Gaurāṅga, the great dancer, and certainly we do not find the strength to carry on alone the *saṅkīrtana* of the holy name of Hari. Please bless us by giving us just one drop of faith with which to obtain the great treasure of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.”

Every time we think how you came and stayed the first months in America, trying to give us for free the holy name of the most beautiful Lord Kṛṣṇa, tolerating all our offenses, we can only feel a faithful admiration. And may you give us the inspiration and faith to always carry on the preaching of your glories, in spite of the difficult circumstances, knowing that the order of the *guru* is the life of the disciple, and without that order it is better to give up life, because the body becomes something useless that only gives pain.

Now we can only pray that with the same compassion you have shown us, you give to these insignificant beggars the opportunity to approach your lotus feet, serving your dear servants life after life.

*Your most fallen servant from Costa Rica Mandira,*

Śrī Yamunā dāsa brahmacārī / Parīkṣit dāsa brahmacārī / Vidvān dāsa brahmacārī / Viduttama dāsa brahmacārī / Rāma-vigraha dāsa adhikārī / Bhakta Enrique / Bhakta Sergio / Bhakta Felix / Satya-devī dāsī / Mitravindā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Yolanda / Śyāmasundara / Raghunātra

## Curitiba

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our insignificant daṇḍavats at your benevolent lotus feet. We worship your feet with all humility, praying for a glimpse of pure devotion in this lifetime.

Even three months after we arrived in Brazil on the invitation of your successor in Latin America, Śrīla Hṛdayānanda Goswami, we hadn’t the slightest idea where this city of Curitiba was located. Upon arriving here, however, we discovered a well planned-out city populated by European descendants with a strong intellectual bent. Your movement has been in full swing here for over a year, and everywhere the devotees are treated with utmost respect.

Recently we have seen your mercy manifest in the form of an elegant new mansion for a temple, a new restaurant downtown, and the establishment of a comprehensive national life membership program. These things can only be due to your mercy. They *must* be, for I see no other explanation of how a band of sworn ex-hippies such as ourselves can focus their attention long enough to even *consider* accomplishing these things, let alone *do* them!

So, by your placing this unknown city on the ISKCON map, “nowhere” has become “somewhere.” And by firmly tucking hoodlums such as ourselves under your wing, “nobodies” have become “somebodies” (for to be accepted by the devotional community is the greatest credit in life). By your transcendental vibration, an unknown 431- kilometer-square graveyard has become scene of Lord Caitanya’s pastimes. And by your potent glance, this entire exotic country of Brazil, once Rāvaṇa’s domain, shall unite under the banner of Śrī Rāma-rājya.

On this most auspicious day of your appearance in this world, we beg only to become your pure servitors. By Nityānanda Prabhu’s kindness, may our flirtations with *māyā* once and for all cease. May we become staunch supporters of Lord Brahmā’s party, and thus abandon all connections with a most felonious past. May we trust in your mercy in spite of our mind’s insistence to the contrary. And may we become deserving recipients of your willing benedictions. O our dearmost eternal master, please give us your blessings!

Your “nobody” servitors from Curitiba (Brazil) Mandira,

Rukmiṇī-pati dāsa / Jaya Jagadīśa dāsa / Pāraṅgacit dāsa / Raṅgapurī dāsa / Ānandarūpa dāsa / Rāsānanda dāsa / Bhakta Jaime / Bhakta Roberto / Latā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Monica / Bhaktin Lia, Bhakta Pancho Svāmī dāsa

## Dacca

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Again on your Vyāsa-pūjā day we gladly take extra time from the duties you have given us to meditate deeply on your personality and the purpose of your mission.

How strange, the materialists will say, that we are making offerings to a person long since departed. Such sweet sentimentalism must be a fascination within the mind.

But no. Unlike material relationships, which are terminated by death, this relationship is deathless – and not just in name, nor simply as a philosophical theory.

Rather, the mature disciple sees with the eye of eternity. “I am eternal, my spiritual master is eternal, and he is inviting me to develop an eternal reciprocation of service and instruction.” Why? For the pleasure of the supreme eternal, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

This *rasa* is not limited by respective positions in time and space. It is based on the consciousness – the serving mood – of the disciple, who is engaged by the perfect spiritual master, the pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa. It is solidified by the sincere determination of the disciple to continue acting as he is instructed by his *paramahaṁsa-guru*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your activities are certainly mysterious. We never believed that you would leave us here when we were still so inexperienced. You left this world still unknown to its masses, before the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement’s full glory was established.

We have heard that for your early disciples, to print and distribute a few BTGs seemed like a momentous task. But you had given the order, and you provided the inspiration, the determination and the know-how to make it possible. The same disciples who stayed faithful to your lotus feet now lead us in pushing, pushing, pushing on the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

New frontiers open, more *bhaktas* come, problems arise and are solved – the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement expands. The future is unavoidable. The Hare Kṛṣṇa movement is becoming huge, auspicious and all-successful. And we, your disciples who a few days ago didn’t know how to hold a broom, must accept responsibility – responsibility for preaching, for book translation, publication and distribution, for training *bhaktas*, for improved standards, for favorable public opinion, for internal purity and for gorgeous Deity worship.

In the face of this enormous task, we feel ourselves utterly helpless. To you, the spiritual master without whom we wouldn’t eve know what *tilaka* is, we offer our most grateful thanks. We can do wonderful things – but only if we pray to you, taking the position of menial servants and never becoming puffed up.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we know that you have not left us, because you are manifest in the hearts and activities of your most sincere disciples. You have given us the ISKCON *ācāryas*, and they in turn are giving you to the world.

We pray that we may become ever conscious of your desire to bring everyone to Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. We pray for the strength to throw off all petty material desires and to be fixed as your eternal surrendered servants. We beg for the mercy to chant your name and dance like madmen: Jaya Prabhupāda, Jaya Prabhupāda, Jaya Prabhupāda!

In millions of lifetimes we cannot thank you enough for what you have done for us; therefore we simply offer ourselves at your lotus feet.

*Your disciples in Bangladesh,*

Prabhaviṣṇu Svāmī / Vaiyāsaki dāsa brahmacārī / Ilāpati dāsa brahmacārī / Rasika dāsa brahmacārī / Jagad-ādija dāsa brahmacārī / Vyāpī dāsa brahmacārī

## Dallas &amp; Harmony Farm

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, Fourth Canto, Chapter Twenty-eight, text 48, it is said, “O best of kings, please get up! Get up! Just see this world surrounded by water and infested with rogues and so-called kings. This world is very much afraid, and it is your duty to protect her.”

The main business of human society is to think of the Supreme Personality of Godhead at all times, to become His devotees, to worship the Supreme Lord and to bow down before Him. The *ācārya*, the authorized representative of the Supreme Lord, establishes these principles, but when he disappears, things once again become disordered. The perfect disciples of the *ācārya* try to relieve the situation by sincerely following the instructions of the spiritual master. Everyone should cooperate with this movement in order to bring about actual peace and happiness in the world.

And in your final instructions to us you said,

And I also want you to organize *varṇāśrama* society. Divide society into *brāhmaṇa* (intellectual classes), *kṣatriya* (administrators), and so on. There should be social division, just as there is a division of the different parts of our body. This will help people. Don’t waste the human form of life by sinful living. I have already given the ideas in my books, so you should all read them.

You are intelligent, all of you. Caitanya Mahāprabhu said, *para upakāra*: do good to others. Do not exploit them. Any human being has the ability to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa. Give them chanting. And also make the situation favorable. Is that clear?

So on this, your appearance day, O our divine master, we are especially feeling the heavy burden of love you have placed on us for spreading the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. We therefore fervently pray to you for the strength to be perfect disciples and to fulfill your instructions for the welfare of all living beings in this material world. All glories to you. Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servants at Śrī Rādhā-Kālacandjī Dhāma and Harmony Farm,*

## Denver

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*  
*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

It is not possible to properly glorify your wonderful activities, and we fee. like fools trying.

Your are constantly showering us with your mercy, opening our eyes with the torch of knowledge. Everyone in the material world is covered over by Māyā, shackled by lust, anger and greed, and they are totally overwhelmed by sinful activities and reactions. But because of your love for your Guru Mahārāja and the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, you saved us from the witch called Māyā. You are teaching us just as a loving father teaches his children: sometimes lovingly, sometimes very sternly. At the same time, you tolerate our many foolish thoughts and activities, patiently teaching us how to chant the holy names of God. You have told us that in this age of Kati, chanting is the only process for developing love for Kṛṣṇa, and only by that love can the burning desire of the soul be satisfied.

Gurudeva, you are kinder than Kṛṣṇa Himself, because you came to the West to deliver such low-class people as ourselves. If we can only remember your instructions and please you by preaching this Kṛṣṇa consciousness around the world, just as you fulfilled the desire of your spiritual master Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta, by preaching to us, then the magnanimous desire of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu will be fulfilled – that in every town and village people will hear the chanting of the holy name of the Lord. Lord Caitanya said that those born in India should preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness around the world. Out of millions so born, you alone took shelter of your spiritual master’s instructions and became empowered to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Because of this, you are the most dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa.

You are so great that you accepted us *mlecchas* and *yavanas* as your disciples in order to assist you in the *saṅkīrtana* movement. Every though we can barely follow your simplest instructions, you have given us the shelter of your lotus feet. No one else could of would do this. You are an ocean of mercy, the bona fide representative of Śrī Kṛṣṇa! You are our only shelter! Without your grace there is no hope of our developing love of God. And only by remembering you will we be able to push on *saṅkīrtana*.

We can never glorify you properly, because of our lack of spiritual vision. We are eternally indebted to you and can never repay your mercy. Meanwhile, your glorification is heard among great spiritual personalities throughout the universe, because you are truly *jagad-guru*. By your vigorous preaching and inspiring example, we are attempting to help you spread the chanting of Hari Kṛṣṇa around this world. We know that if we simply take shelter of your divine instructions, making them our heart and soul, that you will become pleased and shower an unlimited amount of mercy upon us.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

## Denver Farm

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Obeisances at your lotus feet thousands and thousands of times. This is our first activity of each and every day. When we arise from the darkness of sleep, we first of all offer our obeisances to you. We should always be thinking how we can appreciate the mercy which you are so freely offering. Please infuse in us the desire to always want your mercy. We fervently pray for some enthusiasm to carry out your instructions.

You have left us so many nectarean instructions. Those instructions are our very life and soul, for we are made of nothing more than your pure mercy. We cannot measure the magnitude of your kindness, nor can we possibly estimate the great fortune we have by having you as our eternal well-wisher.

Please purify us by your compassionate glance. You are just like a touchstone, for whomever you touch with your divine association gets Kṛṣṇa, who is more valuable than the most precious jewel. Our hearts are very contaminated by the association of the material world, but you are the supermost cleansing agent, capable of cleansing the dirt from even the most dirty hearts.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we owe you everything. There is no way we can possibly repay our debt to you for all you are doing for us, but we can try by always being very faithful and dedicated to following your instructions.

Please accept our humble offering on this most blessed event of your Vyāsa-pūjā day.

*Your unworthy servants,*

## Detroit

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most blessed of all days. We repeatedly offer you our most respectful obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

The great *ācārya* Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura wrote a short stanza about the influence that a pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa carries in this world even after his passing:

He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die  
When thou art living still in sound!  
The Vaiṣṇavas die to live, and living try  
To spread the holy name around!

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are one such pure devotee of the Lord. So, while it is natural for us to be sorrowful that you have seemingly left our presence, we can know for certain that you will never pass away, for you are “living still in sound.” As you yourself said. “I will never die. I will live forever in my books.” In the days and years to come, may we come to remember you in the same way that you remembered your spiritual master: “He lives forever in his divine instructions, and the follower lives with him.”

Although you have removed your glowing golden *vapuḥ* form – your transcendental body – form our vision, you remain eternally present in the form of your infallible instructions – your *vāṇī* form. In the Vedic literature it is stated that the disciple can associate with the spiritual master either through his spiritual caster’s *vapuḥ*, physical presence, or *vāṇī*, presence in the form of his transcendental instructions. Very few of us were so very fortunate as to receive the benediction of personal association with your effulgent *vapuḥ* form. But if anyone simply follows the instructions you have left in your books, they will most certainly be very intimately associating with your most blessed *vāṇī* form.

Your *vāṇī* is not only “matchless gift.” Indeed, your matchless gift to us and the people of the world are without limit. And still you are giving us gifts of such great value that we cannot even begin to appreciate your mercy in full. You have so mercifully given us eleven expansions of yourself in the form of the disciples you chose to be your personal representatives to carry on the glorious disciplic succession.

Considering all that you have given us, what suitable gift is there that we can offer you in appreciation on this blessed Vyāsa-pūjā? What can we offer you of any significance? We could offer you our body, mind and words in full surrender, but factually these things are already your rightful possessions, just as a menial servant is completely the property of his master. We can only try to offer you ourselves in total surrender, hoping to become your humble instruments in fulfilling the desire of your Guru Mahārāja and Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to have this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement spread to every town and village.

By a drop of your mercy please bless us that we may carry on the movement that you have started. Please bless us that we may work together in harmonious surrender, trying to please Your Divine Grace by spreading the message of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gītā* to even the darkest corners of the globe.

Prabhupāda! When will these devotees, obtaining your mercy, become blessed? We are feeling low, fallen and devoid of all strength and intelligence. Please forgive our many offenses against your lotus feet and bless us with your eternal service.

*Feeling unworthy to receive your mercy, we beg to remain your humble servants and Deva-sadana Mandira,*

## Dublin

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. Now that you have left our vision, please be merciful on us and manifest strongly your desire so that we may not go astray. The ocean of this material world is tossing. Looking at the present world situation, the immediate future is very uncertain, and it seems that we really don’t know what to do about it except to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa. Yet you used to say, “To idea to depend on Kṛṣṇa is very nice, but you also have to have some plan, and then Kṛṣṇa will help us.” But what is that plan? Shall we not rather sell all the marble, and put it into the printing and preach a storm as long as this is possible? Is this a good plan, and then Kṛṣṇa will help us?

That is our poor request and prayer on the auspicious occasion of your appearance day. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, guide us through your intimate disciples, our senior Godbrothers, who have taken responsibility as *guru* and GBC, and lead your ISKCON through all present and future situations. Let us not try to cross oceans by taking shelter of the dog’s tail of our mind.

*Your humble servants,*

## Fiji

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and eternal obeisances at your most divine lotus feet. Since your so-called disappearance from this world, you presence has been ever increasingly felt within our hearts.

When Mahārāja Priyavrata was dissatisfied that half of the universe was in darkness, he created, by the power of his devotional service, another sun, Thus he lighted the entire universe. Similarly, your good self, feeling so much compassion for the fallen souls who are suffering due to the dense darkness of this age of Kali, have created another sun. Your Bhaktivedanta purports to the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* are giving light to the entire world. By the inconceivable power of your love for Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and your indefatigable determination to fulfill the order of your spiritual master, you have distributed the dazzling light of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* throughout the world. Your light has penetrated even into the darkest inner recesses of our hearts, where the ordinary sunlight cannot even reach. Thus you are greater than Mahārāja Priyavrata. You have given the light of both the sun and the moon, Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda, to the world.

No one can measure your influence in giving light to the world, because you have inspired your disciples to go on increasing the distribution of Your *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. You have proven that even the most lowborn can become *guru* by sincerely serving the order of the bona fide spiritual master. Mahārāja Priyavrata had perfect sons, and he left the of the universe to them. Like him, you were fully responsible. You trained your best disciples as *gurus* and left the world in good hands. Everything you did was perfect. We can only pray to remember you always in our efforts to carry on with your work.

Simply by remembering your unlimited accomplishments we can begin to understand the greatness of Kṛṣṇa. With no material assets you conquered the world. Simply by your complete surrender to and dependence upon Kṛṣṇa you did the impossible. Mahārāja Yudhiṣṭhira had great brothers to help him to conquer the world. Lord Rāmacandra got help from a powerful army of monkeys. But you created your own army, still, your “monkey soldiers” are floating your books on the ocean of nescience. The more we can distribute your books, the more there is a bridge for crossing the ocean of nescience. The knowledge which you have given to us makes this ocean appear very small. Your purports enable us more and more to always keep our minds on Kṛṣṇa. We pray that we can be good representatives of your most perfect self, and that in some small way we may contribute to the spreading of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

You were so kind that you even came to such a small place as Fiji. On map, Fiji is difficult to find, but you came and established Kṛṣṇa Kāliya in everyone’s heart. Now your *mūrti* sits on the *vyāsāsana*, commanding everyone’s to remember your lotus feet and awakening Kṛṣṇa consciousness in everyone. Your representative, Śrīla Tamāla Kṛṣṇa Goswami, is carrying on your mission, initiating many disciples and guiding everyone as you guided his. Please bless us that we may always think of your pleasure. We want only to please you and to fulfill your prediction that Fiji become the first Kṛṣṇa conscious country. Thank you for giving light in this dark age of Kali.

*Your disciples in Fiji,*

## France

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, the world garlands you, world *ācārya*, with scented words of gratitude. Though the praise flows like an endless river, no one can capture by his thoughts, words, or feelings the extent of gratitude owed you for your incomparable work on behalf of the conditioned souls of this world. We offer repeated obeisances to the memory of you, which lingers in this world like the magic smell of some exotic flower. Its essence permeates our every sound, sight, taste, smell and touch. It colors and transforms our whole perception of the world about us. It crystallizes our understanding, and thus makes Kṛṣṇa consciousness an irrefutable reality. Being sons of Kali, we choose to turn our backs to that evidence and entertain a vision illusion as suits our perverted desire. But we do so with a heavy heart, knowing that we are wrong, and longing to respond to your poem about the spiritual world.

By the will of Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Lord, time inexorably grinds the future in an endless pursuit of the Lord’s inconceivable plan. Life and things are caught in the meshes of destiny. The struggle to free oneself from its afflictions but nourishes its binding force. There is no escape. As waves on the ocean of eternity, years turn centuries and centuries into eons. Throughout, the living entity transforms itself into countless varieties of bodies that spell different shades of pain. Through his mutations he keeps intact only a faint, fleeting hope that lands nowhere, like some soft bird flying into unlimited space. You have come to give this seed a landing place, where it may sprout into devotional service.

Once upon a time, in the dark age of Kali, in the twenty-eighth *divya-yuga* of the Vaivasvata Manu, the population of the planet earth suffered greatly under the illusion of a material concept of life. The entire population had lost contact with the spiritual dimension of life. With hearts seeped in despair, hopeless, they sought frantically the favors of transitory happiness. And Maheśvara, the Supreme Lord, displaying again His inconceivable mercy, appeared as one of them and showed them the path of liberation – the congregational chanting of the *mahā-mantra*: Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare, Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s appearance is like that of the sun, piercing faintly at first on the horizon and gradually flooding with light the entire hemisphere. We, as disciples of Nitya-līlā-praviṣṭa His Divine Grace Abhaya Caraṇāravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, take full shelter at his feet, recognizing him as the full and complete representative of the Lord for this age.

Devotional service is like a great science for the respiritualization of mankind, and the *saṅkīrtana* movement is the very heart of this science. Many great *ācāryas* have explored different aspects of devotional life as proposed by Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Śrīla Prabhupāda, by his unalloyed and selfless dedication, became full and sole heir to their great discoveries. Their vision he took within his heart and amplified it until it reached the outermost limits of the universe. It was he who, endowed with inconceivable potency, reversed the flood of Kali. It was he who, with unfathomable mercy, made devotional service accessible to our childlike grasp. It was he only who made devotional service available to the millions. He is truly the savior of the world, and because his lotus heart never strays from the lotus feet of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, he is to be worshiped as being as good as Kṛṣṇa Himself.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, small is our mind; we are the child of lust. Weak is our determination. Forgetful of the vision you wanted to imprint within us, we falter in our faith and are overcome by the influence of material habits. As we struggle from day to day, concerned with the things of life, we feel totally ineffectual in carrying our your instructions. We barely maintain our senses and are ashamed of the shambles within our minds. Devoid of any spiritual depth or potency, we hold to one single-minded conclusion, one article of faith: as the sun has light, so you carry incomprehensible mercy. Let us abide by the shelter of your lotus feet, although we do not merit such consideration.

*Your most unworthy servants,*

Paris Temple

## Gainesville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our fallen obeisances at your lotus feet.

As every year passes now, we can more and more appreciate what you have done (and are still doing). As we struggle for our own purification and endeavor to give Kṛṣṇa to the conditioned souls, we can start to appreciate just a little what a great and powerful, eternal and confidential associate of Lord Caitanya you are. Any ability that we may have to bring a conditioned soul to Kṛṣṇa consciousness is only because of what you have left us.

By your dynamic presence you forced us to surrender to Kṛṣṇa. You only have created any saintly qualities we might posses, just as Kṛṣṇa has created the universe. And just as Kṛṣṇa can maintain the universe without the need of His personal presence, likewise, by your intelligence you have arranged for Kṛṣṇa’s glorification to continue without your personal presence.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are still everything to us, and we are begging for your mercy so that we can carry on with your mission. Please request Lord Caitanya to empower us to serve you with every bit of energy we have.

*Begging to remain your eternal servants,*

## Gauhati

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

It was for the mercy bestowed by you that we have been led through the most fallen condition of material life.

We are therefore eternally indebted to you.

If you had not left Vṛndāvana to go and preach in the Western land, I can really never see what now my position would be.

It is by your mercy alone that I have been enabled to carry on Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission.

Bless me that I may never forget your kind words of instruction, no matter what my condition may be.

Preaching in India was so dear to you, and so as you have asked me to do, may I be able and follow you.

O spiritual master of mine, may you, with your mercy divine, Empower me with the potency to convince the people with the most sublime necessity – Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*Your eternal servants,*

Bhavabhūti dāsa / Durgama dāsa / Trikivāsa dāsa / Vaikuṇṭha dāsa / Rāmāvatāra dāsa / Bhakta Kishinlal / Bhakta Pradip / Bhakta Subbash / Bhakta Gopal / Bhakta Kuñja Bihari / Maṅgalā-gopī-devī dāsī / Ānandā-gopī-devī dāsī

## Germany

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

It is so wonderful how your personal presence is ever-manifest in the form of Śrīla Satsvarūpa Mahārāja’s nectarean account of your transcendental pastimes on this planet. For thousands of years, devotees will relish not only your *vāṇī* in the form of your divine books, but also your *vapuḥ* in this book by your sincere disciple. Your appearance before us is truly eternal. You will truly remain the spiritual master of all Vaiṣṇavas from all corners of the earth, now and forever. Who but a madman could fear the loss of your continued presence and protection?

O blessed spiritual master, we are astonished at your boundless compassion for us. Personally, I am most wormlike and fallen, practically nothing more than a pies of barely human refuse, who has somehow come into association with you and your dearmost servitors, the ever-increasing members of your ISKCON. Yet you are so kind to us, giving us your continued shelter and teaching us directly in so many ways that it would be difficult to describe them all. Despite all of my shortcomings, I can say with certainty that I cannot shake off my memory of you, crippled and depraved as I am. You know my heart, and you refuse to let me go. Oh, when will I become worthy of this attention?

The glories of your pastimes will be spread all around the world and will sustain humanity for a hundred centuries of more. I pray that I will become qualified to assist in this blissful task. Having seen you in my life, having spoken to you, having served you directly, no matter how briefly, I feel like a living gold mine. A gold mine is a dark, dirty thing – ignorant and even frightening. But yet, if one can somehow endure the trouble of obtaining the ore and refining it, he can become astronomically wealthy. I am carrying the raw are of *ajñāta-sukṛti* – unconscious service to you – in my heart. Let me purify that place, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so that I can have the full wealth of your blessings.

Somehow I must contribute to the spreading of your glories to others – then I can have you. I have heard Christian philosophers say, “Christ is not in the church. Christ is with the sinners. The real Christian preacher goes out of the church into the world of the sinners to meet him.” As strange as it may seem, these words have brought me closer to an understanding of you and your real desires. You are *patita-pāvana* – the savior of the fallen – and *dīna-bandhu* – the friend of the fallen. If I want to mine the lustrous gold of your spiritual form from the cavernous depths of my filthy heart, I have to go out among the fallen and find you through preaching. I accept this intellectually, but please help me realize this practically.

I survey the map and see where countless souls lie sleeping. When will awaken to you association? When will they get your *vāṇī* and *vapuḥ*, which, by your mystical potency, are readily available even to a wretch like me? After all, am I any better than the Mongolians or Turks? Why can’t millions of Chinese or Burmese have you, if I can? The *whole world* is more worthy than I, and if the whole world became Kṛṣṇa conscious with the exception of this one puffed-up monkey, you would be so much more pleased.

Fortunately, the principal task for making the world Kṛṣṇa conscious is given to great souls who have fully surrendered to you lotus feet. They will surely be successful. But still, I feel you pressing me forward. “Don’t hang back. Help them!” Your voice is gentle, but firm. How can I help but be swept ahead by the tide of devotees who are flooding the world with your glories?

*Your servants of ISKCON Germany,*

Nava-Jīyada-Nṛsiṁha-kṣetra

## Gītā-nāgarī

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. Gītā-nāgarī consists only of your mercy. The souls who work here as writers, farmers, businessmen, gardeners, builders, cooks, and teachers are all composed of your mercy. Not one of us could be doing what we are doing other than by your divine grace. Once, in Australia, you were explaining to the devotees assembled in your room that if one remains celibate between the ages of sixteen and twenty-one, one’s brain becomes so fertile for spiritual realization. Everyone was in embarrassed silence, thinking of the withering effect of their sixteenth through twenty-first years. Your glance circled the room, examining the devotees. Understanding our lamentation, you added, “But, if you chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, everything will be all right anyway.” That is what we are all made of. “Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, and everything will be all right.” despite our gaping disqualifications. That is the power of your mercy.

Your interception of our karmic journey to the netherworlds and unlimited suffering was magnificently executed. No one else but you could have captured our hearts and locked them up with such compassionate deftness. You always thought so big, for it was your desire to save everyone, not just a few. But in your big vision, you never turned away any tiny soul who came seeking shelter at your lotus feet. I personally remember you most for that, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are so big, but still you care for little me. You care because Kṛṣṇa care. Because of your kindness, I cannot doubt Kṛṣṇa’s.

As you gave me the job of working with the children of the movement, your blessings are most obvious to me there. These children are especially composed of your mercy. They are so pure. They are receiving such a good chance to use their human life correctly. They are growing up with unlimited faith and are single-pointed in desire. They want to be the servants of your servants. No television, no movies, no boy/girl social pressured warp their minds. They are clear recipients of your mercy in the form of the *guru-kula* system. Even nondevotees appreciate the results of our “Early to bed early to rise, makes a man healthy, wealthy, and wise” practical education system. Any health, wealth, and wisdom they will have is simply your gift.

Here at Gītā-nāgarī, the children all want to serve Śrīla Satsvarūpa Mahārāja. Śrīla Satsvarūpa Mahārāja is great because he has been molded by you. “Become great by serving the greatest.” He is your mercy upon all of us here. Because Śrīla Satsvarūpa Mahārāja has allowed himself to become your instrument, we are all eager to serve him, for in him, we find you.

Right now, I feel happy because I am engaged in glorifying you directly. But so many times I am miserly, miserable, angry, and lonely.

So although on your birthday it is required that I make the offering, still I wish to beg a blessing from you. My humble request on this Vyāsa-pūjā day is that in rain or shine, in sickness of in health, in victory or in defeat, we servants of yours at Gītā-nāgarī-dhāma will keep always mindful of your instructions. To those instructions, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg to cling. They are our identity; they are our shelter; they are our very life itself. Thank you for not kicking us away.

*Your servants at Gītā-nāgarī*

## Guadalajara

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Please, Gurudeva, accept our humble obeisances unto your lotus feet.

Whenever an *ācārya* comes, following the superior orders of the Supreme Personality of Godhead of His representative, he establishes the principles of religion. Unfortunately, when the ācārya disappears, rogues and nondevotees take advantage and immediately begin to introduce unauthorized principles in the name of so-called *svāmīs*, *yogīs*, philanthropists, welfare workers and so on. The *ācārya*, the authorized representative of the Supreme Lord, establishes these principles, but when he disappears, things once again become disordered. The perfect disciples of the *ācārya* try to relieve the situation by sincerely following the instructions of the spiritual master. So, we are trying to do this. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have the progressive path of salvation. The whole cosmic creation becomes at once identical with the Lord as soon as service in relation with the Lord is rendered under the guidance of a bona fide spiritual master. The expert spiritual master knows the art of utilizing everything to glorify the Lord, and therefore, under his guidance, the whole world can be turned into the spiritual abode by the divine grace of the Lord’s servant.

Gurudeva, let us sing like Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura:

*jogyatā-vicāre, kichu nāhi pāi,*  
*tomāra karuṇa-sāra*  
*karuṇā nā hoile, kāndiyā kāndiyā,*  
*prāṇa nā rākhibo āra*

**“Upon examining my value, I find that I have none; your mercy is essential to me. Therefore, if you are not merciful, I will constantly weep, and I will not maintain my life any longer.”**

So, we are always asking for your mercy, Gurudeva. Please don’t kick us away. You can solve all our problems, because we know that the supreme spiritual master is Kṛṣṇa, who is therefore known as *caitya-guru*. This refers to the Supersoul, who is sitting in everyone’s heart. He helps from within, as stated in *Bhagavad-gītā*, and He sends the spiritual master, who helps from without. You are the external manifestation of the *caitya-guru*, or the spiritual master sitting in everyone’s heart.

Lord Caitanya said that His spiritual master told Him, “It is very good that You have attained such a perfectional stage of love of Godhead. Because of Your attainment, I am very much obliged to You.” The father becomes more enlivened when he sees his son advance beyond himself. Similarly, you take more pleasure in seeing your disciples advance than in advancing yourself. So, we are trying to advance by means of the *saṅkīrtana*, and in that way, give you so much pleasure.

Please accept again our humble obeisances unto your divine lotus feet.

## Guatemala

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this appearance day of Your Divine Grace, please let us thank you for pulling us from the ocean of nescience. You are everything for us. Hearing of your words and activities is by far the most relishable thing we know. Your pastimes have captured our hearts. Your transcendental presence is very wonderful, always offering guidance and inspiration at every moment. Only when one is able to get out of the clutches of hypocrisy will he be able to recognize you in the form of this movement. For the materialistic man you are not present, but for your devotees it is an ever-fresh discovery of your delicate presence. You yourself are the form of this movement. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, bless us by letting us always remain in this movement, so in this way we will never be lost again. We are all your children; we need your direction at every step. Please give us the strength to push on this *saṅkīrtana* movement, and protect us from all lusty desires, so we may work cooperatively for this foal, allowing us to represent you in the best way.

We offer repeated obeisances to the memory of you, which lingers in this world like the smell of some exotic flower. Its essence permeates our every sound, sight, taste, smell, and touch. It colors and transforms our whole perception of the world about us. It crystallizes our understanding and thus makes Kṛṣṇa consciousness an irrefutable reality. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can you manifested in the ISKCON devotees. They are all worshipable, by your mercy. They are taking up the business you gave them, and what to speak of the powerful *gurus* you have appointed. They are undoubtedly empowered by you to preach. We especially see you in them. Gurudeva, your mercy is all we are made of, and if we cannot obtain it, we are less that nothing. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the shelter of your lotus feet.

## Guyana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet again and again on this auspicious day of your appearance in this mortal world to deliver the simple but sublime message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to the people of the Western world, nay, indeed, to the entire universe. O Jagad-guru, you are to be honored as much as the Supreme Lord, because you are the most confidential servitor of the Lord. All the Vedic scriptures proclaim your glories.

Like the effulgent sun, in the presence of which all darkness is immediately dissipated, your message has removed the darkness of ignorance from our hearts. O master! Now be merciful upon us and grant us the boon of constant love and devotion to your lotus feet. O Prabhupāda, we have been burning in this vast ocean of materialistic sense enjoyment and mental agony for millions upon millions of births past, and we can only be released by fully surrendering ourselves to your instructions.

“Wake up! Wake up!” You are calling out to us. “How long will you continue to sleep on the lap of the witch Māyā? Take this medicine, which is the only cure to your material disease of the repetition of birth, death, old age and disease.” So by giving us this joyful process of chanting, dancing and *kṛṣṇa-prasāda*, you have placed us firmly on the path back home, back to Godhead.

May we now immerse our minds completely in the service of your lotus feet and ever strive to follow your sacred orders. O Prabhupāda, at this dark period in the history of the world you are the salutary light, beckoning mankind to find freedom and end misery. The radiance of your light causes the forces of impersonalism and voidism to flee, and the total atheistic foundation of society is fast crumbling by the invincible blows of your immortal teachings. O master! May your glories continue to expend. May we, your disciples and granddisciples, excel in the sunshine of your glory and remain resolute to push on your movement in Guyana.

We beg you that we may remain always in your loving service.

Bhūtādi dāsa / Bhakta Cecil / Girjanauth / Parsram Ramarattan / Hardat / Premarajh

## Haridaspur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

You are enjoying now in *nitya-līlā*.

We are feeling the burden of your separation here in Haridaspur. Please forgive us; we are crying out for you upon this most joyous occasion. It is the only lamentation worthy of grief.

Just as every day we are trying to understand and feel the reciprocation with our *nāmācārya* Śrīla Haridāsa Ṭhākura, every day we are trying to understand and feel your loving reciprocation. *Vede gāya jāhāra carito*.

Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura called you “poet.” It was no mundane ornament of literary expression of simple encouragement to his disciple, for you have understood the true utility of poetry – “What is the use of a bowman’s arrow or a poet’s poetry if they penetrate the heart but do not cause the dead to spin?” (*Cc. Antya* 1.195) Your books, your poetry, your emotional ecstasies are out only direct link to you. Thus, we are eternally pleading to our *nāmācārya* that we may be more conscious of you, by one line, by one word. And we are praying to you to help our ISKCON family become more conscious of our *nāmācārya* Śrīla Haridāsa Ṭhākura.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! There will never be another *ācārya* like you. Like Śrīla Haridāsa Ṭhākura, you are an eternal personality, without diminution, and more conspicuous by your absence. When you came to Śrī Māyāpur, you did not see it as just another “big-time” project, and when you came to Śrī Haridaspur you did not see it as just another “small time” project. All were spiritual projects, not measurable by walls, bricks and acres of land.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! Help us to understand that there are no higher or lower devotees; help us to understand the meaning of *guru*, of *sat-sādhu-saṅga*, of ISKCON, of Śrīla Haridāsa Ṭhākura’s appearance in a *yavana* family, of your teachings beyond the politics and science of mundane intelligence, of your appearance in a higher-caste family – of how you are our eternal father and have graced all of us impartially and equally. Essentially, you have simply told us to follow your instructions and preach.

Above and beyond the obstacles, we will preach:

ISKCON-founder-ācārya Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!  
Nāmācārya Śrīla Haridāsa Ṭhākura ki jaya!

*Your eternal servants,*

Bhakti-prabhāva Svāmī / Tīrthapada dāsa / Sukhāmṛta dāsa / Acyuta Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Gaura-Govinda dāsa / Tapana dāsa / Hlādinī-śakti-devī dāsī / Nitya-sevā-devī dāsī / Vrajapriyā-devī dāsī / Śāntilatā-devī dāsī

## Hartford

Please accept our most humble obeisances, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please engage us in your service forever and empower us to glorify you in this way ever more and more!!

Our celebration on this day of your appearance is the celebration of your association. To us, your appearance *means* your association. And how we can endlessly celebrate your glorious association!!

This day of Vyāsa-pūjā refers to the worship of Śrīla Dvaipāyana Vyāsa, the literary incarnation of Kṛṣṇa, as well as to the entire disciplic succession. Vyāsa-pūjā could not be more fittingly titled to honor your appearance day. Your literary powers are so tremendous that one is compelled to think of Śrīla Vyāsadeva when thinking of your accomplishments in the literary field. And the blessings of the entire disciplic succession most clearly were yours, as can be evidenced by your accomplishments in your preaching, making of devotees, opening of temples, establishing farm communities, etc. Not only have you won our hearts, but clearly you have the heartfelt wishes of the entire *guru-paramparā*.

To establish what you have in such a short period of time evidences a superhuman effort on your part. You once characterized yourself by saying that if there was one quality that was outstanding in your character, it was that you were never lazy. Even as a little boy, you were never lazy. None of our strongest men were able to keep up with you pace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, despite our youthful energies. Your efforts were fully successful just in terms of your endeavor alone – all for the pleasure of your Guru Mahārāja.

Once when asked how to make the most rapid advancement in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, you replied, “To follow very strictly all the rules and regulations, even the little ones.” Yet what you have required of us in this regard was so lenient compared to your Guru Mahārāja. So we must very strictly take to the spiritual regulations you have given us, for which you personally set such a perfect example. We have all confidence and conviction in your words that if we strictly adhere to the basic spiritual disciplines that you have entrusted us with, all of our efforts will be successful, just as you have promised us.

These glories of yours and countless more are our sustenance – not to hear them and meditate upon them and be inspired by discussing them in the association of your disciples would mean that we would perish. Thus we celebrate Vyāsa-pūjā, considering the meaning of your appearance in this world and in our lives.

*Your servants at Hartford ISKCON,*

Trai dāsa / Vālmīki dāsa / Devarata dāsa / Sahadeva dāsa / Bhakta Jimmy / Bhakta Avery / Bhakta Frank / Bhakta Russ

## Hong Kong

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

How can I write in praise of you,  
this tiny insignificant soul?  
I’m bound by incessant material desires  
and so envious of the Whole  
Than I even think I can help you  
and as your friend bring all to Kṛṣṇa near;  
Help you who need no help from a rascal  
Like myself, who knows nothing but fear.  
But still, your order tolls clearly in my ears  
As a bell through the thickest cloud:  
“Bring Bhagavad-gītā to Red China …  
Give the Hari-nāma. And chant it LOUD!”  
Only then shall I feel I’ve helped you  
In your mission so pure and sweet,  
When that last fool casts aside his suffering  
And dances the dance of your lotus feet.

*Your eternal servants,*

## Honolulu

**The Captain of Our Ship**

Somehow we have fallen and left our sweet home,  
where Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa and all Their friends roam  
To come to a world that is ghastly and cold,  
where death is awaiting each time we grow old.

From species to species like pearls on a thread –  
to even consider at once makes one dread.  
We’re lost in this ocean so gigantic and vast,  
with stormy rough weather the eternal forecast.

Impersonal aquatics with razor-sharp teeth  
attack from the surface, and also beneath.  
But by you sweet mercy and unlimited grace,  
you’ve come to save us from this horrible place.

The ship known as ISKCON was constructed by you  
and built to facilitate an unlimited crew.  
The maps you have brought bear the Bhāgavata name,  
as first taught by Kṛṣṇa – they’re exactly the same.

And showing us how that divine service pleases,  
you’ve taught us the art to make favorable breezes.  
The most perfect captain you always display,  
so now, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to you we must pray.

Please keep us aboard and train us to row,  
and please take us with you wherever you go.

Your eternal servants,

## Houston

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most heartfelt obeisances. Although we try to acknowledge our gratitude to Your Divine Grace at every moment, we appreciate this special opportunity to express our love for you and reaffirm our full commitment to assist you in spreading Lord Caitanya’s movement all over the world.

In attempting to write this humble offering, we are confronted with our own inadequacies and the certainty that within the entire history of the Brahma-gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava-sampradāya, there have never been any disciples less qualified than ourselves. As our savior and eternal spiritual master, you have shown yourself to be the greatest Vaiṣṇava*-ācārya* of all time. We say this in love, but an objective study reveals that no one else has ever succeeded in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness so widely, to so many people, in so brief a period of time, as you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Even Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu Himself confined His preaching to India, and as you kindly note in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta*, He left the rest of the world to the International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

ISKCON is now established in every part of the world, but what is ISKCON but you, Śrīla Prabhupāda – an ecstatic manifestation of your loving exchange with Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. It was founded on the strength of your desire (to fulfill the order of your spiritual master), and the plan and guiding instructions (now preserved in your books, letters and tapes) are also coming from you. You also provided a structure to oversee and protect the spiritual purity and proper growth of your society (administered under the careful guidance of your appointed GBC). In other words, all aspects of ISKCON are coming from you and must be seen as nondifferent from you. In this way you have provided for our spiritual welfare and for the welfare of future generations for thousands of years to come.

Now, by your mercy, we are witnessing the further spreading of ISKCON and the awakening o Kṛṣṇa consciousness of millions of people all over the world. You have stated that this success is due entirely to the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and that if you have any credit, it is that you perfectly carried out the order of your Guru Mahārāja. Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu also set the example that one should never claim to be a direct servant of Kṛṣṇa but a humble servant of the servant of the servant of Kṛṣṇa. To carry out the order and example of one’s spiritual master guarantees success for oneself as well as for others.

Our love for you will be shown not by inventing new methods for being Kṛṣṇa conscious or spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness, but by carrying on the activities of ISKCON in the ways already approved and practiced by you. We fervently pray that we may never be deviated from this principle of making your order and personal example the very meaning and purpose of our lives. Please accept us as your eternal, unswerving servants.

*The residents of ISKCON Houston*

## Israel

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Your Divine Grace, please accept our prostrated *daṇḍavats* eternally in the dust of your munificent lotus feet. On this most blessed day of you appearance, another year has passed, and the *saṅkīrtana* movement continues to gain ground against the forces of Kali-yuga.

Because you perfectly accepted the burden of the missionary desire of your spiritual master, Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Mahārāja, we also have the opportunity to accept wholeheartedly the transcendental assignment awarded to us in love.

As explained in the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi-līlā,* the words of the spiritual master carry the potency to execute those directions. The words of the spiritual master are not different from the words of Kṛṣṇa. Because the spiritual master, by dint of his pure devotional service, has passed beyond all the material elements and thus has nothing material about him, everything that he says or does or thinks is simply a reflection of Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s thoughts, words and deeds. Therefore, by your mercy the miserable conditioned souls all over the planet can receive the fruits of transcendental association with the Supreme Personality of Godhead Himself.

In the *Bhagavad-gītā,* the Supreme Lord, friend, and enjoyer, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, is called *paraṁ brahma paraṁ pavitraṁ paramaṁ bhavān:* “You are the Supreme Brahman, the ultimate, the supreme abode and purifier, the Absolute Truth….” Only a fully pure and selfless personality can represent the absolute pure or establish this truth within the minds, hearts, and lives of an ever-increasing number of sincere souls. This is your potency, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and even though you appear to have left us, we see that by your potency so many more souls are awakening their eternal attraction for Kṛṣṇa, which is lying dormant, deep within their hearts.

A mundane person may become famous during his lifetime, but after his doglike death, gradually he is forgotten. On the other hand, the nature of a transcendental personality is just apposite. After his passing to the spiritual world, he becomes more and more well known, though he is not physically present. As we distribute your blessings in form of your transcendental literatures, more and more illusioned souls are learning of your greatness a potency.

We do not think that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is spreading without you. Rather, we see how you have prepared everything perfectly and that you are factually present directly, guiding us through the agency of your appointed “expansions.” That is another symptom of your supreme greatness: that you empower those utterly surrendered souls with nothing less than your own complete potency, and in this transcendental manner increase the devastation of māyic energy.

Despite all impediments, Kṛṣṇa consciousness is flooding the seven continents like a tidal wave, and this is another of the innumerable proofs of your position in perfect transcendental loving service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

By following you, who are the perfect teacher and most compassionate friend and well-wisher of all living entities, we are leaving behind all the inebrieties of this dark and temporary world. We had no qualification, but because of your great love and tolerance, miraculously we are able to change the consciousness of the world population at large. We know that this is your greatest desire – to bring everyone to Kṛṣṇa.

So we are expressing our deep feelings of gratitude and indebtedness by promising to remain submissive receptacles to receive your unlimited kindness and mercy, and to share it with all others without discrimination.

Here in Israel we see that everyone is needing Kṛṣṇa consciousness very much. You have given the universal panacea for all problems and inconsistencies. Slowly but surely we are proving the necessity and supreme value of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to everyone, and we know that this is the only think we can do to repay our eternal and unrepayable debt to your most magnanimous Divine Grace.

*Your grateful servants at ISKCON Israel,*

Ātmānanda dāsa adhikārī / Bhakta Nathan / Śailendriya-devī dāsī / Kamalākṣa-devī dāsī / Jalāṅgī-devī dāsī

## Italy

If I were poet, I would compose poetries  
Describing the artistic beauty of creation  
And the magic feelings arising from them.

Surely I would not describe mundane subjects,  
For I know that in them are not those divine qualities  
Which can inspire a thoughtful man to express his feelings.

When I see natural wonders I stop and think  
What an artist must be the creator of that,  
How beautiful must be the source of all these beauties.

Wandering around the world, I saw many of those.  
But the real beauty, I realized, is life,  
Which gives variety to the stagnant and dead.

But the real beauty among lives is a devotional life,  
By which all spiritual qualities are developed –  
Beautiful, artistic, and worthwhile to sing about.

Therefore, if I were a poet I would compose poetries  
Describing the artistic beauty of your character.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, among devotional lives, yours is supreme.

Those descriptions would not have limit at all  
And would arouse continuous ecstatic emotions,  
For that is certainly the perfection of human life.

Among beauties of nature, life is superior,  
Among lives, a devotional life is excellent,  
And among devotional lives, yours is supreme.

Today we are celebrating Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā,  
And you are my sat-guru, my eternal father,  
So every year I am composing an homage for you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your only wish was to save us,  
Drowning men embarrassed by thousands of miseries.  
I now pledge to forever follow your example and teachings.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I have life only because you gave it to me,  
So I have really nothing to give you that already is not yours.  
Accept this glorification as a sincere offering from this fallen soul.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you told to every one of your disciples,  
“Please, go around and preach the glories of the Lord,  
Then your life will be perfect and sublime.”

I will never be able to forget that this is your causeless mercy,  
Therefore, on this most auspicious day of your appearance,  
I pray and beg the Supreme Lord that I never be separated  
From the service of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s holy lotus feet.

*Your devotees in Italy,*
