## Jakarta

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Who is there who can adequately praise your glories, Śrīla Prabhupāda? Even for writing this Vyāsa-pūjā homage we are feeling ourselves most unqualified, just like a small child offering the father’s possessions back to the father, for whatever we may say that is worthwhile is nothing but what we have learned from you.

It is said that one may judge a Vaiṣṇava by how good a touchstone he is. By seeing how many pure and sincere disciples and granddisciples you have all over the world, we are thinking that you must certainly be the best of all the Vaiṣṇavas, for who else but you has spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness so far and wide? Your pure devotion to Kṛṣṇa makes you just like a shining sun which illuminates a world overcome by the darkness and ignorance of Kali-yuga. And even though to material vision you have departed and entered into your eternal *līlā* with Śrī Kṛṣṇa in Goloka Vṛndāvana, you are shining still through your many books and instructions, which are giving pleasure to the devotees and hope to the conditioned souls.

You have said that another ambition of the spiritual master is to see his disciples not only chant, dance and follow the regulative principles but also preach the *saṅkīrtana* movement to others in order to deliver them, for Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is based on the principle that one should become as perfect as possible in devotional service and also preach the cult for other’s benefit. Somehow or other, by your causeless mercy we have been given the task to translate and distribute your books in the Indonesian language, although we have no qualification for such work. Now we have only just begun, and already so many people are purchasing your books and asking for more. So our desire is to produce as many of your books as possible, to offer them on your *vyāsāsana* in the transcendental land of Vṛndāvana-dhāma, and to distribute them far and wide so that everyone may know of your glories as the dearmost servitor of Kṛṣṇa – that, we think, will be our best Vyāsa-pūjā homage to you, although we know that we can never repay you for saving us from the clutches of *māyā*.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your humble servants of Jakarta, Indonesia Yātrā,*

Gaura-maṇḍala-bhūmi dāsa brahmacārī / Pralambāri dāsa brahmacārī / Śukadeva dāsa brahmacārī / Vyāsa-pūjā dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Ketut Sumajaya / Bhakta Tamanbali / Aravindākṣī-devī dāsī / Kṛṣṇa-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Komang / Agus / Dewi

## Johannesburg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. Jaya Prabhupāda! Jaya Prabhupāda!

One morning in Vṛndāvana, at the *samādhi* of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Dāmodara Mandira (it was October 1972 – Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Mandira was not even a foundation), you read to us two newspaper clippings that you had just received. One told about the preaching of Śrīla Satsvarūpa Goswami and Śrīla Hṛdayānanda Goswami in Midwest America. The other said, “Kṛṣṇa Message in South Africa.”

Then you told us, “So, by the grace of Lord Caitanya, the message of his Lordship is going on nicely all over the world. You are also the members of the Society. Try to preach this message, *pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi-grāma*, \[*CB Antya-khaṇḍa* 4.126\] and you’ll get the strength the more you preach, because in the *Bhagavad-gītā* the Lord says, *na ca tasmān manuṣyeṣu kaścin me priya-kṛttamaḥ*. Anyone who is preaching the message of Lord Caitanya, Lord Kṛṣṇa, the same thing, there is no difference. Kṛṣṇa says that: *sarva-dharmān parityajya mām ekaṁ śaraṇaṁ vraja.* This is Kṛṣṇa’s message. And Lord Caitanya says, *yāre dekha tāre kaha ‘kṛṣṇa’-upadeśa*. So there is no difference. Kṛṣṇa’s upadeśa is to everyone, that everyone should give up all nonsense type of occupation. Our Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is preaching the same as Lord Kṛṣṇa said and as Lord Caitanya said. Lord Kṛṣṇa said that you surrender. He asked everyone, not only Arjuna. Through Arjuna He spoke to everyone. He was preaching His own word through Arjuna. And Caitanya Mahāprabhu is Kṛṣṇa Himself. He is preaching the same message as a devotee. So our business is to preach the same message through the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu says, *yāre dekha, tāre kaha ‘kṛṣṇa’-upadeśa*. So if we follow Caitanya Mahāprabhu, to follow Caitanya Mahāprabhu means to follow the six Gosvāmīs. This is called *paramparā* system. *Evaṁ paramparā-prāptam imaṁ rājarṣayo viduḥ*. If we follow the *paramparā* system, the same message that Kṛṣṇa says, that is also said by Caitanya Mahāprabhu, that is also said by the Gosvāmīs. Śrī Rūpa Gosvāmī said, ‘Somehow or other, try to induce people to become Kṛṣṇa conscious.’ This is Rūpa Gosvāmī’s message. Where you are sitting, this is Rūpa Gosvāmī’s place. So Caitanya Mahāprabhu also says the same thing, *yāre dekha, tāre kaha ‘kṛṣṇa’-upadeśa.* So this boys, they are preaching the same message, Kṛṣṇa message. In the paper, ‘Kṛṣṇa Message in South Africa.’ ‘Kṛṣṇa Message in South Africa.’ This is very encouraging, that Kṛṣṇa message is being spread as Caitanya Mahāprabhu desired. This is very encouraging.

“So all of you, you follow and spread this message extensively. Simply you have to, just like our little girl Sarasvatī, she also preaches. She goes to some fried, she asks, ‘Do you know what is Kṛṣṇa?’ If he says, ‘No, I do not know very much,’ she says, ‘The Supreme Personality of Godhead.” That’s all. This is preaching. It is not difficult. Simply just like a child, you go to everyone, talk to everyone, that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality of Godhead. That’s all. Everyone can do it. It is not very difficult. You believe it. You be convinced on the statement that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

“So you have taken the path of Caitanya Mahāprabhu. He will bless you if you sincerely stick to His principle. Then by His grace you will be able to preach Hs message. You follow the instructions of the Gosvāmīs, follow the path of Caitanya Mahāprabhu and try to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world, and your life will be glorified. Thank you very much.”

Who can understand the potency of your words, Śrīla Prabhupāda? Now, eight years later, we find ourselves in South Africa, trying to tell people that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality of Godhead, trying to please you, trying to remember your words. Just as Kṛṣṇa drove Arjuna into battle, your words drive us into the battle against *māyā*. Just as Arjuna was successful because of Kṛṣṇa, we will be successful if we simply follow your words. When we are asleep, your words awake us. When we are discouraged, your words encourage us. When we are sad, your words console and cheer us. And most of all, your words plunge us forward, drag us forward, force us forward. To hear you is strength. To hear you is life. May we always hear you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, until we can see you again, face to face. Please be kind and grand us this one request. Jaya Prabhupāda! Jaya Prabhupāda!

*Your most fallen servants,*

Śrutadeva dāsa adhikārī / Ādi-kartā dāsa adhikārī / Vardhanya dāsa brahmacārī / Pāṇḍava dāsa brahmacārī / Vālmīki dāsa brahmacārī / Gaura-mohana dāsa brahmacārī / Kāliya Kṛṣṇa dāsa brahmacārī / Gaura Naṭarāja dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Andrew / Daṇḍakeśa-devī dāsī / Rucirī-devī dāsī / Añjanā-devī dāsī / Līlā-mohinī devī dāsī / Gaṅgādevī-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Gail / Mādhavī / Pisīma / Joni

## Kathmandu

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

False philosophizing,  
cheating and lying,  
fame and adoration  
our only motivation.

Profit and distinction,  
instant recognition,  
but our defective perception  
yielded no perfection.

Thus we burned in the fire of māyā,  
cheated by our false desires.  
Low in intelligence and full of pride,  
we saw no ways to uplift our lives.

But then, when you knew the time was right,  
you came and conquered all the sight.  
You gave us shelter of Kṛṣṇa’s holy name,  
and thus we came to life again.

So now on this most auspicious day  
we bow before you and humbly pray.  
Now that you’ve salvaged something from our lives,  
the taunts of māyā we’ll survive.

And although we are without your vapu form,  
your personal vāṇī we adorn.  
No worldly delights for us to enjoy,  
your words alone we wish to employ.

For that will give the higher taste  
and save our souls from been misplaced,  
for wretched and fallen as we are,  
your causeless mercy will carry us far,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, always remembering  
how kind and loving you’ve been,  
we humbly submit our service,  
as shallow as it may seem.

As Kṛṣṇa states in *Bhagavad-gītā, sthāvarāṇāṁ himālayaḥ.* Of immovable things I am the Himalayas. Similarly, we also wish to remain immovably attached to your lotus feet in our effort to establish a Kṛṣṇa conscious stronghold here on your behalf.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*We fall down at your feet as your most unworthy servants.*

Prabhaviṣṇu Svāmī / Mahā-Viṣṇu dāsa / Mahā-vīrya dāsa / Mahākratu dāsa / Santoṣa dāsa / Haṁsa-gati dāsa / Dhruvanātha dāsa / Pītāmbara dāsa / Kṛṣṇacandra dāsa / Bhaktisiddhānta dāsa / Mādhāi dāsa / Kaustubha dāsa / Bhāṣyakāra dāsa / Bhakta Narayan / Bhakta Gaura / Bhakta Dave / Bhakta Bejali

Guru-kula

Ṭīkā dāsa / Ānanda / Kālī / Kamala / Rājendra / Madhusūdana

## Laguna Beach

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please allow this most insignificant servant to offer some glorification on your holy appearance day. No one can find the limit of your glories. Still, to glorify you, to try to properly understand your glories, is itself the process of self-realization (mahat-sevāṁ dvāram āhur vimukteḥ). Indeed, to glorify you is life’s work, the only engagement of your most intimate disciples. You are not like a common man, who becomes famous due to glorification. No poet or writer can add to you, but rather he becomes purified by trying to speak something about you.

It is said that the face is the index of the mind, and it is true that any ordinary man has his lusty thoughts written on his face, but how can I know you, Śrīla Prabhupāda? Your personality is as deep and fathomless as the ocean. You cannot be understood in any ordinary way.

When you left I was bewildered. I’d only seen you a few times, never talked with you. Our eyes met three times (*lava-mātra sādhu-saṅge sarva-siddhi haya)*. But somehow you have accepted me, and even though it seem to me that you left before I could know you at all, surely you have given everything required, although I am only beginning to understand how this is so. You have said that you are ISKCON. Just as Kṛṣṇa says He is the butter, the fire and the offering, so every aspect of ISKCON is manifesting through you and resting on you. In *Bhagavad-gītā* (18.15) you have written, “…the spiritual master is the transparent medium, although it is true that the experience is direct. This is the mystery of disciplic succession.” So someone may think that he can experience something of Kṛṣṇa consciousness separately, but actually you are the *kīrtanas*, the *prasāda*, the books, the temples, the worship, the art, the culture, the association of devotees, the loving exchange and purifying kicks. You are the pilgrimage to Vṛndāvana and Māyāpur. You are the preaching and the ecstasy. You constantly embrace the devotees.

I remember once when I first became a devotee, I experienced some illumination and little sincerity. I prayed fervently before your *vyāsāsana*, “Please let me become a pure devotee.” That was also you. How have you done this? I am a lowborn wretch, with only petty, dirty, selfish desires. You have placed this seed in my heart, this transcendental vision. You have taken this worm out of stool and made him dance in *saṅkīrtana* party of Lord Caitanya and take *prasāda* with the devotees.

You’ve said that spiritual pleasure comes when we desire to please Kṛṣṇa, but you only have desired, not me. And by your desire I am tasting something. I am so weak, but you’ve engaged me in preaching, an activity usually reserved only for the Lord’s most confidential devotees. You have caused me to walk across the hot parking lots with burning feet and aching back, only to meet envious stares and slamming car doors, to begin to appreciate the trouble you’ve taken for me in spite of being rejected in so many ways. You’ve made me see how proud and foolish I am and yet how fortunate. You’ve made me drink the nectar of the vision of one who has sacrificed everything at the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, even though I only wanted the poison of selfish desires.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most merciful. Lord Caitanya is accepted as the most merciful personality because He is giving pure love of Kṛṣṇa, but you alone are giving Lord Caitanya (*gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*) to the whole world, to the most fallen (*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*).

O Prabhupāda, you cannot be properly honored, but I beg for your blessing that I may always be engaged in chanting your glories, that I quickly become a strict follower of your instructions so that I may always eternally remain in the ocean of your divine association. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please be merciful to me; please accept my most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

## Lima

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humblest obeisances at your lotus feet. On this magnificent day of your merciful appearance, our intelligence falls short in its comprehension pertaining to the whole significance of such an event.

What was this world before your visitation? What was the opportunity of giving some utility to all of this human forms? As the amorous words of a mother awaken her son from a nightmare, so your words reverberated within our hearts, thus ending this horrible material dream.

You struggled against so many obstacles and endured uncountable inconveniences in order to form your ISKCON, and in this matter furnish us with the opportunity of becoming Vaiṣṇavas. It is for that reason that you are the true philanthropist and the greatest benefactor of the mankind.

As the days go by, our anxiety increases while we await that moment in which the whole world will prostrate at your lotus feet. At time we wonder as to how we shall convert the planet, but when we remember your painstaking efforts and all of your work, it is then we are in the capacity of understanding that it will automatically happen on its own accord, for it is Kṛṣṇa’s wish to glorify His pure devotee. It is not that we will make it true – you did already make it, you did already deserve it, and no one will ever change it. Simply by your mercy you are permitting us to participate in your *līlā*, thus letting us all benefit from it. Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda! Please be merciful to this foolish conditioned souls and let us forever remain the humble servants of your servants.

## London (Bhaktivedanta Manor)

*jaya jaya prabhupāda tumi prāṇa-dhāna*  
*jaya kṛpā-sindhu jaya patita-pāvana*

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most auspicious occasion of your merciful appearance in this world, we, your disciples at Bhaktivedanta Manor, are aspiring to offer you some worship at your lotus feet. We do have a few flower petals, but we would like to offer ourselves to you as completely surrendered souls. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we want to cease all external playacting and now really give up all attachment for material gain and thus simply be motivated by the single pure desire of pleasing Your Divine Grace. We have finally exhausted our attempts to make suitable arrangements for eating, sleeping, sex life and defense. By your association we have at last come to our senses so that we no longer want to serve perverted desires for the flickering pleasures of this world, but now we want to serve you. Please feel to do with us as you will. Please arrange with Lord Nityānanda and Lord Caitanya that They kindly sever any connection we have with this material life of so-called family, friendship and love. Please ask Them to give us the strength of purpose to rigidly follow all your instructions in any situation, and to bestow upon us the necessary intelligence for the execution of Their plan for the deliverance of all conditional souls as soon as possible. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, we want to serve you in a spirit of love and trust amongst ourselves, and not simply be a headache for you. We want to know how to associate properly with you (since you have, out of your wonderful mercy, accepted us as your eternal associates, although we are not fit to even approach you from a distance) and how to associate nicely with each other. We want to be able to address you with full feeling as the lord of our lives, as the very live of our lives.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, will we ever be a little useful to you?  
O Śrīla Prabhupāda, will we ever be proper recipients of your mercy?  
O Śrīla Prabhupāda, will we ever understand and also feel your compassion for the fallen souls?  
O Śrīla Prabhupāda, will we ever work even fractionally as hard as you to spread this movement?  
O Śrīla Prabhupāda, will we ever be able to take shelter of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s holy names?  
O Śrīla Prabhupāda, will we ever be able actually serve Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda?  
O Śrīla Prabhupāda, will we ever be a little useful to your beloved son Śrīla Jayatīrtha dāsa Goswami?  
O Śrīla Prabhupāda, when, oh, when will we simply tremble with love when we utter your name and remember you?

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are very dear to Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, and although we know you have saved us, we feel ourselves to be hopeless, so we are helplessly praying for your continued mercy. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your servants at Bhaktivedanta Manor

## London (Soho Street)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We, your disciples and granddisciples at Rādhā-Londonīśvara-nivāsa, fall at your feet. We feel very inadequate and fallen, yet you have kindly allowed us to engage in a service which is very dear to you. It is no secret that your mission in London was always very important to you, Because Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Gosvāmī Mahārāja Prabhupāda gave great emphasis to preaching in this city. He sent some men here in 1934, but they were unsuccessful, and he called them back. He kindly left it to you to arrange for the deliverance of the suffering souls of the streets of London town. Many years later you came yourself, sending some of your new American disciples ahead of you, and in the space of s few short autumn months in 1969, established Kṛṣṇa consciousness in these islands as a powerful force.

Just before leaving, you gave us our most treasured and most beautiful Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara. Kṛṣṇa chose to manifest Himself in all His gorgeous beauty, along with His eternal consort Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī. He simply came to please the desire of His most pure devotee and to bestow His causeless mercy on the previously unfortunate residents of this onetime capital of the demons, now become a place of pilgrimage.

You have now kindly left us the task of realizing your desire to spread the ocean of Lord Caitanya’s mercy in this dark place. The darkness is being dispelled by the lotus feet of their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara and by the transcendental presence of your beloved disciple Śrīla Jayatīrtha dāsa Goswami. We have recently celebrated the tenth anniversary of your installation of Their Lordships, and have now moved Them to a wonderful, opulent new temple right beside Oxford Street, where we have always been known for our *saṅkīrtana* activities. The new restaurant, which you always wanted, is now becoming very successful by your grace.

We are simply praying at your lotus feet that our attempt may be pleasing to you. Please bless us with your causeless mercy so that giving up all contrary desires, we may be satisfied to fully absorb ourselves only in carrying out this wonderful mission which you have given us, and taking shelter of Kṛṣṇa’s holy name, we may find our ultimate rest at the lotus feet of Śr Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara.

*Your menial servants,*

## Long Island

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our obeisances at your two lotus feet. On this auspicious Vyāsa-pūjā day, as we are celebrating your transcendental appearance within this world, please accept our humble attempt in making this offering at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the mercy incarnation of the Supreme Lord. We were simply suffering in the blazing fire of material existence, bound up by the modes of passion and ignorance, until you so kindly appeared, bringing water from the ocean of mercy. As the confidential servant of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, you have propagated the *saṅkīrtana* movement throughout the entire planet, bringing the most valuable gift in all the three worlds – the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. Your transcendental position is to exalted, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we cannot calculate your greatness. Even though we are sinful and lowborn, you have made us your menial servants and engaged us in assisting your preaching work. Please give us the strength to carry on your preaching mission and to be always fully engaged in the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your mercy is all that we are made of; please pick us up and place us as atoms at your lotus feet.

*Your eternal servants,*

Adarśī dāsa / Bhīma dāsa / Jñānāgamya dāsa / Kṛṣṇa-nāma dāsa / Śacīnandana dāsa / Yudhiṣṭhira dāsa / Ādya dāsa / Bhakta Mike / Jananī-devī dāsī / Mahāmāyā-devī dāsī / Harakāntā-devī dāsī / Rādhā-Madanamohana-devī dāsī / Janaka / Bhaktin Marico

## Los Angeles

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once wrote, “Simply a festival of flowers and fruits does not constitute worship. But the one who serves the message of the guru really worships him. The service of the message is the real meaning of the *Vedas*. Don’t be proud, brothers. Come back to this.” While we lack both the realization and the sincerity to so boldly cry out to our Godbrothers, we hope that by repeating your words, they may see beyond our own foolish envious motives and recall your prophetic warning.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you came to England in what would be your last Western world appearance, and we were astonished, shocked and some-what disappointed to see that apparently you had already left the earthly realm. “*Madhyama*-Prabhupāda,” the lionlike messenger of Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, was at last tamed by an “*Uttama-*Prabhupāda,” who was until that time unknown to us. We were informed to not sing *śrī-guru caraṇa padma*, for it disturbed you. We knew why. We wanted to think that it caused you to feel too much ecstasy, but internally we knew that our lack of realization of the meaning of the words and subsequent parrotlike chanting was the actual reason. “Every day you are chanting, but unfortunately you do not know the meaning.” Some of my Godbrothers have foolishly thought that to know the meaning one must learn the language. Later they dared and died to differ with your “absolute opinion.” Let us pray to become servants of your message and really worship the *guru*. But, just as then, we cannot face you now. Just as then, we have forgotten you now and misplaced your message somewhere in the mire of personal consideration. In your absence, intoxicated by the flattery of sentimental fools and lusty women, we have taken our assets and accepted the most royal facilities for this breathing bag of stool and foul-smelling desires, allowing you’re your transcendental form to lie ingloriously unadorned, shamelessly neglected within the cool sands of Ramaṇa Reti. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, are we so ungrateful *(guru-druha)*? If all the great mountains of the world assembled in Vṛndāvana and offered their most valuable stones, jewels and gold in service of your lotus feet, perhaps a suitable memorial could be constructed. By collecting our hearts (*vyavasāyātmikā buddhiḥ*), massive marble monuments could arise, towering in the skies of Māyāpur and Vṛndāvana, testifying to your greatness. But we are “too intelligent.” “Milk is better than water. Please accept my offering, Śrīla Prabhupāda.” You have so patiently, compassionately given us Kṛṣṇa, most dear friend, but what have we offered you in return (guru-māra-vidyā)? We have offered you our sins, and not only from the past! But what is needed now is a demonstration of love, not intelligence, meaning the sacrifice of personal desires in the service of the beloved. Now, on this most glorious of all days, let us worship you in service to your message and vow book by book, brick by brick to spread your fame throughout the three worlds. In a brief moment of sincere humility, we devote our lives as flower offering unto the lotus feet of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your servants in New Dvārakā,*

Resident Sannyāsīs

## Malaysia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We have still not recovered from the shock arising from your disappearance. It only seems like yesterday when you visited London, and even though you were so sick, still you sat amidst your loving devotees, who were dancing and chanting enthusiastically and experiencing waves of ecstasy with every nod and clap made by Your Divine Grace.

How fortunate is this planet to have been traversed by the lotus feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s pure an confidential devotee, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Sometimes we become too bewildered to understand how we, such fallen and lowly creatures, became recipients of your causeless mercy.

O Gurudeva, your glory and fame will only go on increasing as this movement gains momentum through your pure representatives, disciples and disciples of your disciples. In an interview you once said that you would consider your mission a success if you could make even one pure devotee who could perfect his life and go back to Godhead. Slowly we can begin to understand the power of the desire of the pure devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa. You, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, are changing the course of history, but in your humility you often stated, “I am simply following the instructions of my Gurudeva.” We pray that we also become as determent to carry out your instructions with implicit faith and surrender.

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ*  
*paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande*  
*bśrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto my spiritual master, the deliverer of the fallen souls. His mercy turns the dumb into eloquent speakers and enables the lame to cross mountains.”**

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, keep us eternally engaged in your divine service, unqualified as we are, for we have no other shelter than you. Please always enthuse us to perform devotional service, and accept this very meager offering on your appearance day.

Always desiring to please you,

*Your servants in Malaysia,*

Rāmaśaraṇa dāsa adhikārī / Vrajendra-kumāra dāsa brahmacārī / Bhīma dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Radha Menon / Jānakī-devī dāsī

## Manipur

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We are born in the darkest realm of ignorance. By your mercy alone we can see the noble purpose of life. The Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, is so merciful that He kindly sent you as His most confidential representative to save us. Otherwise our lives would have been completely doomed. Your appearance day is most worshipable, and on this auspicious occasion we offer our humble report.

The progress of Kali-yuga is also rapidly manifesting in this corner of the globe. People in general have lost their vision of the noble purpose and goal of human life. *Athāto brahma-jijñāsā*: the noble purpose of human life is to inquire about the nature of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Absolute Truth, for this is the message of Vedānta.

When people indulged in sinful activities like gambling, intoxication, meat-eating, illicit sensual activities, they behave like animals. These symptoms are widespread all over the world. It is like a wild forest fire that spreads uncontrolled all over the forest. However, the forest fire can be extinguished by the rain water that showers from the sky. The rain-water saves the trees which are not completely burnt and gives them life and nourishment again. Similarly, Śrīla Prabhupāda, your desire is to save people from further sinful activities and give them new hope by showering spiritual rain all over the planet. The nectarean rain is coming in the form of the message of *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. When people take this message seriously, their lives will be sublime, and they will see that noble purpose of life.

We, the insignificant disciples and granddisciples of Your Divine Grace, are making our humble attempts to distribute the message of *Bhagavat-sevā*, as you so mercifully ordered us, to every home, every town and village in this frontier corner of the world. We do not have cars and vans. However, we have a rickshaw. We do not have water inside the building. However, we bring water from a pond one-half mile away. We do not have electricity all the time. However, we have candles and a lantern. We do not have opulent *prasāda*. However, we have nice rice, *dāl*, *sabjī*. We have everything, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We have your divine blessings. We have your divine order. All the devotees, including our noble life members, are extremely enthusiastic to help your divine mission.

In recent times, in the middle of great difficulties, our humble attempt to spread the message of *Bhagavat-sevā* have begun to sprout in the minds of sincere people. They are beginning to appreciate the pure message of your divine movement. We are preaching in the form of publishing and distributing your transcendental literatures. Noble and worshipable Vaiṣṇavas of this land are helping in the translation of your divine work in the local language. We are broadcasting on the radios, publishing timely articles in the local newspapers. The essence is *Bhagavat-sevā*. Since this is your divine desire, we have firm faith that some day your desire will be fulfilled. We are requesting people to understand that the noble purpose of human life will be achieved by taking to the process, *nityaṁ bhāgavata-sevayā*, as you so tirelessly taught us. Our only strength is your divine order and mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Kindly allow us to take shelter eternally at the dust of your lotus feet. Jaya, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your most insignificant servants,*

Bhakti-svarūpa Dāmodara Svāmī / Sarva-bhāvana dāsa adhikārī / Mahāmanā dāsa adhikārī / Vana-mālī dāsa brahmacārī / Manu dāsa brahmacārī / Gītā-govinda dāsa brahmacārī / Nanda-kumāra dāsa brahmacārī / Ekanātha dāsa brahmacārī / Indrasena dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Kuñja-mohana / Bhakta Gopal / Bhakta Subhas / Bhakta Principal Birmangol / Paramahaṁsa-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Tombisana

## Mauritius

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisance unto your nectarean lotus feet.

You told us that two places in the world can easily become Kṛṣṇa conscious – Mauritius and Fiji Islands. Now we beg you to kindly empower us to fulfill this great task. Over the last few years we have been making life members and distributing books. The people are mostly receptive, and hardly anyone refuses to take at least your monthly magazine. Parliamentarians, policemen, businessmen and even students and laborers are enrolling as life members. Slowly the nectar of your instructions is diminishing the ocean of darkness and nescience.

As a result, many people are awaiting the formation of spiritual center to accommodate and facilitate their desire to surrender to and serve your lotus feet, as well as to assist and participate in the wonderful unfolding of your ever-increasing glory.

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, Gurudeva, please glance at us here in this small island and kindly shower your unlimited mercy on us, who are so insignificant. If you and Lord Kṛṣṇa want, we can fulfill your prediction with ease, but as we are foolish, stubborn and fallen, without your loving mercy we are uselessly ineffective.

Begging to be able to please and satisfy you eternally, as well as to remain the servant of your servants.

Śrīniketana dāsa / Naṭavara dāsa / Arjuna dāsa / Apavarga dāsa / Bhakta Bhagawandas/ Bhakta Oumesh / Bhakta Harry / Bhakta Anand / Bhakta Dhurendra / Bhakta Gurudhatt / Bhakta Ashok / Bhakta Rajen / Bhakta Ajav / Bhaktin Bindumati / Bhaktin Kavita

## Māyāpur

We are pay our obeisances at the lotus feet of our spiritual master, Śrīla A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who understood the desire of his master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī.

We pay our obeisances, with straw between our teeth, at the feet of our spiritual master, who illuminated the world with the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya.

We pay our obeisances at the lotus feet of our spiritual master, whose skillful hand leads all the *jīvas* to their constitutional service in Goloka.

We remember you always as the embodiment of all spiritual qualities, as the deliverer of the pure message of the *Vedas*, as the savior of the fallen of Kali-yuga, as Kṛṣṇa’s mercy personified.

We are awaiting your return to *mandira*. The monsoon has come, gusting cool rain over the fields, quivering emerald waves. Cranes descending from the dusky clouds billow their gleaming wings in welcome. Hovering on the horizon, the spire of the *janma-sthana* glows effulgent as the moon. The rhythms of *mṛdaṅga* and *karatāla*, the high-pitched melodies of children’s *kīrtana* pierce the four directions. Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī modestly offers some choice betel, while Śrī Mādhava lifts the flute to His lips, inclined to feed nectar to His devotee’s ears.

One day, we accepted you as our master. By acting as the example, you opened our hearts and planted the seed. Then you instructed details – where to cut, where to support, how to water and how to feed the infant creeper of devotion. You gave us a hint of the most secret destination, buried in the hearts of the wise. You were desiring a field of devotional creepers, tall and strong, covering the whole world.

You willed a great city in Śrī Māyāpur, vibrant with the holy name. From a small grass hut by the road you instructed details of this monumental preaching effort, and you gave us the privilege to be the builders and inhabitants of that spiritual city, the abode of *prema-bhakti*.

Therefore, without you, what are we? You are the only support of our existence, the only life of our life. Impatient for your presence, we can only try to remember your instructions exactly, while daily we offer our bodies, minds and words for your service.

Before us is the city, like a heap of snow-white pearls. See the avenues and gardens, laden with the fruits of *bhakti*! Here arises the moon, who is Śrī Caitanya, churned from the ocean of *bhakti-rasa*.Because you brought us along the narrow path to this place, our home, we pay our obeisances at your lotus feet eternally, O life of our life!

*kṣurasya dhārā niśitā duratyayā*  
*durgaṁ pathas tat kavayo vadanti*  
*dharmasya tattvaṁ nihitaṁ guhāyāṁ*  
*mahājano yena gataḥ sa panthāḥ*

*yasya deve parā bhaktir*  
*yathā deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ*  
*prakaśānte mahātrnanaḥ*

## Melbourne

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Kindly accept our most unworthy obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you often explained that Kṛṣṇa is just like the sun. He is always appearing on a new horizon. Similarly, the cry was always heard in some part of the world, “Prabhupāda is coming.” No words have ever inspired so many devotees to perform such impossible task as these three.

You once wrote that you have been cursed, because although you have so many nice palatial homes, you can only stay at most a few days in any one of them. But that was how you inspired us to maintain your temples – because we were always expecting your arrival.

Here in Melbourne we consider ourselves very fortunate, because you descended here on five different occasions and installed beautiful Deities for us, so that the gorgeous receptions could continue daily. Actually, our whole temple beautification revolved around your visits: we only pained when you were coming; we made instant gardens; new curtains appeared from nowhere; the Deities received new sets of clothes; altars and your *vyāsāsanas* were built; donations poured in; debts mounted up; and *bhaktas* joined. Your coming inspired our *saṅkīrtana*, our Ratha-yātrās, our property purchases, our interior decorating, and our Deity installations.

The list is endless, but the most unforgettable experience was that feeling of joyful expectation, fearlessness that nothing else mattered except that, “Prabhupāda is coming,” and everything has to be finished before he gets here. There were no anxieties for one’s bodily necessities. There were no time to be sick. Above all, you gave us no time to be in *māyā*. If *prasāda* came, it came; if it didn’t, the devotees kept on working, often without sleep right up until the deadline – your arrival time. Even in that, you increased our ecstasy by often arriving early and catching us in our working clothes. In the enthusiasm even casual observers (myself included) got swept up into the ocean of devotional service.

And then there were the preaching arrangements you inspired. We were so proud to be your disciples. Jagad-guru was coming to Melbourne. We wanted to tell everyone that “Prabhupāda is coming.” We would plaster every available wall and space with posters of your picture all over the city. We remembered how pleased you were in 1972 when the Melbourne Town Hall was packed with people coming to hear “Jagad-guru.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, our bungling receptions were undoubtedly full of faults, but you always accepted them graciously, and although we may now be very expert at making nice elaborate arrangements with all the proper Vaiṣṇava etiquette, we pray that we may always be able to experience the joyful expectation that “Prabhupāda is coming.”

## Mexico City

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Ācāryas, GBC, sannyāsīs and devotees in general:

We are gathered here one more year to celebrate the day of the appearance of the savior of our lives, and we can remember one verse from *Bhagavad-gītā*:

*jñānena tu tad ajñānaṁ*  
*yeṣāṁ nāśitam ātmanaḥ*  
*teṣām āditya-vaj jñānaṁ*  
*prakāśayati tat param*  
*\[Bg. 5.16\]*

This verse refers to the sun rising and illuminating everything during the day, and in the same way knowledge illuminating and destroying nescience. On this day, on the horizon of our lives rose this knowledge, or better yet, the personification of knowledge – our beloved spiritual master, Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya 108 Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktivedanta Goswami Mahārāja Prabhupāda.

All those people who have forgotten Kṛṣṇa are certainly confused under the spell of *māyā*. But those who are in Kṛṣṇa consciousness are not confused at all, as in the case of our beloved spiritual master, A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. The *Bhagavad-gītā* affirms, *sarvaṁ jñāna-plavena, jñānāgniḥ sarva-karmāṇi* and *hi jñānena sadṛśam*. This knowledge is regarded as very elevated. But then the question arises. How can one attain this knowledge? This knowledge is attained when one surrenders to the lotus feet of Śrī Guru, just as Śrīla Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura says, *divya-jñān hṛde prokāśito.* By your mercy, divine knowledge is revealed in the heart. After passing through many births and traveling all over the universe, one can get the fortune of meeting a pure devotee of the Lord, who can destroy our ignorance, as is explained in the following way:

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

This verse indicated that the spiritual master acts like the divine sun, revealing the rays of transcendental knowledge to the living entity who is confused by the darkness of ignorance. Because he is complete and perfectly transcendental, the spiritual master is never covered by nescience and is always in contact with the Supreme Personality of Godhead. As Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura has said in his prayers to the spiritual master,

*sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair*  
*uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ*  
*kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

This verse indicates that the spiritual master is the most dear and confidential servant of the Lord. All the Vedic literatures instruct us to approach a spiritual master, and it is very important that we look for a bona fide spiritual master and under his instruction learn what is Kṛṣṇa consciousness. And by Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, this searching stopped in our life when we met His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Prabhupāda had the power to dissipate all the nescience in our lives, just as the sun dissipates the darkness of night. We have to understand very clearly that even though a person may have full knowledge that he is not this body and that he is transcendental to the body, still he might not be able to distinguish between the soul and the Supersoul. But one who takes refuge under a bona fide spiritual master who is Kṛṣṇa conscious can know everything. So it should be very clear in the mind of everyone that only by finding a bona fide representative of God who has knowledge of God can one know God and his relationship with Him. We should not fall into the foolish illusion of the materialist, who cannot see God in the material world and so thinks that no one is controlling. In the same way, another fool will think, “Why can’t I see Śrīla Prabhupāda? He’s not controlling this movement.” But it is not like this. Every intelligent person knows that even though you can’t perceive Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa is controlling by way of His energies, and in the same way the sincere disciple knows that even if Śrīla Prabhupāda is not physically present, he continues to control this movement, this Kṛṣṇa conscious movement, by way of his divine arrangement. And what is the proof? The Proof is that so many people come to the movement, that many centers are opened, and that so much literature is printed, just according to his desire. This is the tangible fact that no one can deny: that everything is going according to his desire. And so, on this day, we should try to understand his divine message, falling in the dust of his lotus feet.

We can cite a passage from the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* of the story of the *brāhmaṇa* Vāsudeva, who was greatly suffering from leprosy. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu cured him, and the only thing He asked in exchange from Vāsudeva was that he would preach the instructions of Kṛṣṇa and liberate all human beings. So on this paint Śrīla Prabhupāda made us remember that all the members of this Society were elevated from the most abominable conditions of life, and he said that the only payment he wanted was that we continue preaching the instructions of Kṛṣṇa and liberating all the living entities, just as we were liberated from this abominable condition.

And now we recall the year 1972. This year was very significant, because of the great change that happened in our lives. We can never imagine having such great fortune. Different devotees went to the International Airport, and just as we arrived on the sidewalk in front of it, we saw two devotees running towards us, exclaiming, “Śrīla Prabhupāda, Śrīla Prabhupāda is here!” We got out of the car as fast as we could, and ran behind them. Still we can’t explain the surprise, the joy of approaching the place were Śrīla Prabhupāda was. We could not believe that such an exalted person would be present just before our eyes. After traveling for so long in the material world, we had the opportunity to meet with a pure devotee, just as we read about in *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta*. The situation was embarrassing, because we arrived late to receive *jagad-guru*, late to receive Śrīla Prabhupāda. Exhibiting one of his exalted qualities, he excused us, and he calmly received us. Śrīla Prabhupāda was peacefully seated, and the whole place looked like the spiritual world because there was the spiritual world. He smile shone like millions of suns dissipating the darkness of ignorance of our lives. And in this moment we could understand that the darkness of ignorance of our existence had ended. This day was the beginning of a life of constant ecstasy and a blissful existence of eternity and knowledge. Immediately after, one Hindu guest arrived and invited Śrīla Prabhupāda to come with him to the temple. Śrīla Prabhupāda generously accepted and was driven to the temple. His transcendental presence purified the city of Mexico.

In the days that passed, Śrīla Prabhupāda displayed his unbreakable desire to serve Kṛṣṇa. We went to the university, and when Śrīla Prabhupāda preached, one fool without intelligence asked, “What is wrong with taking the body of a dog?” And Śrīla Prabhupāda responded, “You will take the body of a dog in your next life, if that’s what your desire.” After, we accompanied Śrīla Prabhupāda to a television program with Jacobo Sabludowsky, who very respectfully received Śrīla Prabhupāda. In this way the whole city of Mexico had the opportunity to receive the message of the transcendental world by way of this television program. Then we went to the Masonic Lodge, and after that we left Mexico City and went to a place called Cuernavaca, where he gave a conference in the principal park of this town. Many people came to hear him, and there Śrīla Prabhupāda demonstrated his transcendental position in this material world. At the end of the conference, Śrīla Prabhupāda started toward his car. Then one drunken man approached Śrīla Prabhupāda saying, “Let me come near the great Lama.” At the same time we immediately ran to stop his approach, but Śrīla Prabhupāda told us, “There is no problem. Let him go.” And he continued on his path to the car. After that we took him to the conference room of the most elegant hotel in Cuernavaca. There Śrīla Prabhupāda gave a conference. The people who came there asked a series of intelligent questions and very much satisfied Śrīla Prabhupāda. And it was also significant because there we distributed the first book translated into Spanish, entitled *La Conciencia de Kṛṣṇa, el Sistema mas Elevado de Yoga*. One lady of advanced age approached Śrīla Prabhupāda and asked him to autograph her book.

Śrīla Prabhupāda kindly accepted with a gentle smile, and afterwards other people approached him asking for autographs. Returning to Mexico, Śrīla Prabhupāda was in ecstasy and said this was the perfection of life, to be completely engaged in the service of Kṛṣṇa. Afterwards, the following Sunday was something incredible. We weren’t able to prepare a great quantity of propaganda, but in spite of this, the temple was packed. And the main room wasn’t sufficient for all of the people, and all of them were very anxious to have the audience of His Divine Grace. And even though there were torrents of rain over the city, the people kept coming to hear the divine message of Śrīla Prabhupāda. The people gathered around his window, dancing, not caring for the rain that was getting them wet. The people didn’t care about their bodies, but were just intent to hear the divine message from the lotuslike lips of Śrīla Prabhupāda. Śrīla Prabhupāda announced that the following day they would have the first initiations in Latin America, and in this way Śrīla Prabhupāda opened the door to liberation in this part of the world, satisfying the prophecy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu:

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma.*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

**“In every town and village, My name will be chanted.”**

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-gopāla Mandira, Mexico Yātrā

## Miami

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We may try to understand you position or your glories with our limited minds, but our efforts shall fall short of success in the endeavor, as your attributes are as wonderful as the attributes and qualities of the Supreme Personality of Godhead Himself. Of course, we never are confused by the Māyāvāda that the Lord and the living entities are one, but we understand that by your intimate relationship with the Lord, the Lord exhibits His most wonderful a aspect through you, His unlimited mercy.

It is described that when Lord Caitanya appeared, He delivered the conditioned souls in three ways: by meeting them directly, by empowering a pure devotee, and by appearing before someone Himself. This second method of delivering the conditioned souls in described in the *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta* by Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Gosvāmī regarding the personality of Nakula Brahmacārī. In this regard it is stated,

*sei jīve nija-bhakti karena prakāśe*  
*tāhāra darśane ‘vaiṣṇava’ haya sarva-deśe*

**“Thus He empowered living beings (His pure devotees) by manifesting in them so much of His own devotion that people in all other countries became devotees by seeing them.” (*****Cc. Antya*** **2.14)**

In the same way that Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu entered the heart of Nakula Brahmacārī, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu manifested His presence through your activities on this earth planet during your brief stay here. Daily we are reading of your activities in the *Prabhupāda-līlāmṛta*, written by your dearmost disciple, and feeling our heart being cleansed and the seed of devotion cultivated by the transcendental remembrance of your activities. Hearing of you is as good as hearing of the Supreme Lord from the pages of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. In the future, living entities in other planetary systems, not to mention this earth planet, will hear of your glories.

It is described in regards to Nakula Brahmacārī, and the descriptions also apply to Your Divine Grace, “His body shone with the same luster as that of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and he showed the same absorption in ecstatic love of God. People came from all provinces of Bengal to see these symptoms. He advised whomever he met to chant the holy names Hare Kṛṣṇa. Thus upon seeing him, people were overwhelmed with love of Godhead.”

Not only were these same qualities manifest in yourself during your *līlā*, but you in turn have invested these same qualities in your pure representatives, who are presently infusing this universe with love of God. Simply serving, seeing, hearing of associating with these great souls well yield the perfection of human life. I pray to the Lord that I can remember my position as an insignificant servant of your pure devotee servants. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, may my remembrance of you never fade, but increase more and more each day, and may we never become bewildered in our dealings in this material world.

*Your eternal servants,*

## Mombasa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your holy and transcendental feet. I am very sorry to always be a trouble to you, not taking advantage of the unlimited kindness and mercy which you have made available to me. Even up to the time of the writing of this offering, I have not sincerely dedicated my life to pleasing you. I am a very big offender, an I would like to stop my offenses. Actually, there is very little chance that this will be possible, but in a mood of desperation, and with the help of the Master of the Intelligence, who permitted the rhymes, I am addressing the following verses to the eleven appointed *gurus*, the GBCs, my brother- and sister-devotees, all my spiritual nephews and nieces, as well as all the others who are taking advantage of the unlimited and ever-expanding mercy of you. My great hope is that by revealing myself to those who are sincere devotees, I will become purified enough to be able to approach you directly and beg forgiveness for all of my countless offenses. If it is possible to gain this forgiveness, then my next desire is to be able to dedicate my life to assisting those of your devotees who have very good intelligence and are successfully using that brain-power to carry on your mission of fulfilling the desire of Lord Caitanya.

Dear Prabhus,

**(VISION)**

I was thinking of the great glory of this auspicious day  
While gazing one evening at the moon and stars so far away.  
Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s certainly arranged a dazzling display.  
If a billboard could be made to tell the glory of this life,  
It would have to resemble this array of celestial lights.  
Prabhupāda – the bright, shining moon of this Kali-yuga night.  
The brightest stars: his books, farms, temples and pure devotees.  
A pure refreshing vision, to see his transcendental beams?  
Nay, a glimpse through a clouded eye while sleeping in a bad dream.

**(CONFESSION)**

In his shining presence a man like me can never remain.  
And now, so all of you’ll please help me as my devotion wanes,  
I’m opening up myself to you with the hope I’ll regain.  
I’m a cunning man with just the dress of a devotee.  
Secretly I’m nursing my lust for sex and bad company.  
Surrendered to gross sense passions in the way I like to be.  
I harbor infatuations for nice wife and progeny.  
I’m ever anxious to pamper and nourish my gross body.  
My frivolous mind always enjoys many delicacies  
Of false ego. My greatest reward is birth, death an disease.  
I should never be trusted because of my very pleasing speech;  
I’ve acquired it by study and practice. If you like, I can teach.  
The honey on my tongue hides the deadly poison in my heart.  
Though your preaching be noble, with my wickedness I’ll not part.  
As Rahu blocks the sun and moon, I enjoy to block you stars.  
You should recognize this imposter and keep me very far.  
For seven years I’ve listened to Śrīla Prabhupāda’s beckon,  
Yet I’ll never realize life slips to death at each second.  
And to speak of love for Kṛṣṇa – I certainly have none.

**(PETITON)**

So why continue this useless verse – the glories of a dog?  
I’m only hoping that by all your grace, I’ll soon break this fog  
Before death comes and offers me the body of a fat hog.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda is very great, and likewise you’re his men.  
Help me please to be like you and do something which pleases him.  
I humbly pray to stop this sin and qualify as your kin.

*Servants of Śrīla Prabhupāda at ISKCON Mombasa:*

Nava-yogendra Svāmī / Jānakīnātha dāsa / Abhayāśraya dāsa / Mādhavadeva dāsa adhikārī, Viṣṇurvatā-devī dāsī, Rādhikā / Rambhoru-devī dāsī, Madana-mohana / Nara-Nārāyaṇa Ṛṣi dāsa adhikārī, Vijaya-devī dāsī / Vaiṣṇava-devī dāsī, Bhakta Amin / Bhakta John / Bhakta Bill / Bhakta Luke

## Montreal

When the Lord descends in this world and chooses a particular place for His residence, that spot becomes a pilgrimage site. I this way, the causeless mercy of Śrī Kṛṣṇa and Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu made India the most blessed of all lands.

Now Your Divine Grace has transformed the whole planet earth into a *dhāma*, where pilgrims from all over the universe can now congregate to cant the glories of Śrī Śrī Rādhā Kṛṣṇa and Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

You are disseminating the seeds of devotional service everywhere, and the *mlecchas* and *yavanas* are becoming transformed into Vaiṣṇavas by the purity of your words. Therefore your fame is known all over the three worlds.

Simply being touched by a drop of the incessant flow of nectar emanating from Your Holiness, one’s heart is sanctified. Whoever is so fortunate gradually attains the platform of pure *bhakti*. May we also become like you and thus help you serve the mission of your beloved Gurudeva.

*Devotedly trying to please you,*

## Nairobi

You appeared just like the spring morning sun  
For the benefit of all, each and every one.  
You shine and benedict like no one else has done.  
Just chant and dance and see the fun.  
Daily I have to read from your books  
Because the sinful desire it surely cooks,  
And if I miss even a single day,  
Māyā, she enters in a very clever way.  
The solace I find in reading your books  
Is not to be found in any of earth’s nooks.  
This reading gives me such great insight,  
Giving me knowledge with māyā to fight.  
Of all the books being sold today,  
Yours are the only which show the real way.  
A book not yours might have some glare,  
But after reading, the end is despair.  
Who can describe Your Divine Grace  
Coming directly from that Goloka place?  
The words emanating from your lotus mouth  
Cause me to ecstatically dance and shout.  
Of all your disciples, I am the worst;  
I’m a nuisance, a hindrance, and fully cursed.  
You love your disciples, each and every one,  
Even the worst – don’t forget this lowly one.  
I have made up my mind what I want to do –  
A life of devotion and pleasing service to you.  
All benedictions are useless to me.  
I only want pure service to thee.  
O Prabhupāda, how can I be sure  
The roots of sinful desires are there no more?  
Because leaves beneath a tree may be burnt,  
But the roots in the ground may sprout again, I’ve learnt.  
O Prabhupāda, when will that day come  
When sinful desires in my heart will be reduced to none,  
And in that I can place a lotus seat  
For you to sit on while I serve your lotus feet?  
In the beginning I could not grasp what you meant.  
But I kept hearing anyhow, even while others went.  
So now I can see into this deep mystery  
That other than Kṛṣṇa, we don’t want to see.  
O Prabhupāda, when will the service I render you  
Be continuous, flowing without interruption, and pure?  
The service I render now is a hindrance, I’m sure.  
Please bless this wretch to be without offenses anymore.  
Once I was motivated while thinking of you,  
But now these same motivations are separating me from you.  
I beg of your mercy that I may think of you with love  
And chant in ecstasy your glories from above.  
My senses are trying to kill this soul,  
Dragging and urging me from the goal.  
My only refuge is to train them to serve you.  
Please bless me to purify my senses through and through.  
Please give me attraction for your service given to me;  
Half my time is wasted in mental lethargy.  
If I could use all my energy for you,  
Many people would benefit from a life anew.  
O master, my heart is full of greed and lust.  
Sometimes I cry with utter disgust.  
You are very kind; you gave me everything.  
Still, for you I have done absolutely nothing.  
Please five me one special benediction:  
For reading your books a strong addiction.  
Enthuse my mind with rapt attention.  
This, for me, is the only perfection.  
After your benediction I will also pray  
To distribute that message every day  
Without a change and without a mistake.  
The conditioned souls will gladly take.  
By not reading your books my judgement is confused.  
I worry and think how can this be used.  
But when I hear you, my judgment is right,  
Clearing the illusion of this terrible night.  
When will I be able to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa  
Without offense, with the devotion of Bhīṣma?  
Chanting is easy, anyone can see,  
But because I’m a rascal, it’s difficult for me.  
Gradually, as the time goes on,  
My purification slowly comes along.  
And only time will tell if I have heard  
Correctly from you every single word.  
When will my love for you begin to start  
With eager service right from my heart?  
Sinful and fallen is my present condition,  
Because of my hesitation to listen.  
Please give me the strength that I may stay  
To serve you sincerely day after day.  
I cannot find the strength alone,  
So I beg of you, please take me back home.  
So fortunate I am to have a relationship with you.  
The śāstras declare that you are the jagad-guru.  
So I’m begging from you to give me a place  
Amidst the ocean of your divine grace.  
Give me the guidance to become purified,  
Give me the desire, though many times you tried.  
When will I hear you within my heart?  
When will I follow your words and start?  
You are without and also within,  
Guiding me through the thick and the thin.  
Your guidance makes me feel so sure,  
Opening wide the Vaikuṇṭha doors.  
Name and fame is the material game  
Which ruins my creeper, making it lame.  
Happiness and distress I try to tame.  
When will I see them as both the same?  
You labored very hard, writing many books  
To establish eternal principles, defying the crooks.  
When will I appreciate what you have done  
By reading and preaching from every single one?  
When will this lust burning inside of me  
Be transformed to love only for thee?  
Just as bell metal is changed to gold,  
Please change my lust to love untold.  
Māyā is strong and I’m so weak.  
I am bewildered when she reaches her peak.  
Even time she attacks this fettered soul  
I become bewildered and forget the goal.  
But you have conquered māyā so strong.  
No matter what she dose, you never go wrong.  
Please keep me with you, right by your side,  
Then māyā will never take me for a ride.  
You have instructed me to glorify the Lord,  
But I don’t know why I become so bored.  
But now I am awakened to a taste so sweet –  
Glorifying the new ācāryas’ lotus feet.  
The śāstras declare as the most valuable worship  
To have with Kṛṣṇa’s devotees a friendly relationship.  
Now, as well as your worship, I worship the eleven,  
“Mad-bhakte ’py abhyadhikā” goes beyond even heaven.  
O Prabhupāda, how can I become sincere?  
I hardly mean a word of anything mentioned here.  
You are sincere – please give me a chance  
To forget this māyā and sing and dance.  
Your words illuminate like a star,  
Making me wonder who you are.  
Blessed was the day when you appeared to shine,  
Your activities and pastimes a treat divine.

Ajāmīla dāsa brahmacārī / Jānakīnātha dāsa brahmacārī / Caṇḍī dāsa brahmacārī / Vaṅka-vihārī dāsa brahmacārī / Giri-govardhana dāsa brahmacārī / Abhayāśraya dāsa brahmacārī / Kṛṣṇa Balarāma dāsa brahmacārī / Sarva-vit dāsa brahmacārī / Guru-prasāda dāsa adhikārī / Bhakta Samuel / Bhakta Billy / Bhakta Amin / Rāsa-līlā-devī dāsī / Devahūti-devī dāsī / Rādhikā-devī dāsī

## New Orleans

O spiritual master of the universe, we beg to offer you our prostrate obeisances. Please give us the strength to carry out your mission with which you have so kindly left us.

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is said that to associate with the spiritual master in the *vāṇī* (instructions) form is more important than the *vapuḥ* (body) form. This is being realized by us more and more, because even though you are not physically present, we are experiencing your association and mercy more and more.

Even when your were visiting us occasionally on your famous world tours we were experiencing this. As book distributors, we were always told that to go out and distribute was the best way to get your mercy. We believe this, and by going out every day we began to realize it.

But then, on your rare appearances at our temples (which only happened once or twice in an eternity), we were told that instead of staying back to be with you, we should go out even more and get your real mercy. All the leaders of our movement were visiting you personally, but we were always at the airport or the lots or wherever, on *saṅkīrtana*. We were preached to by our authorities that this was how to get your real association, but this was hard to accept. How often does one get a personal visit from Your Divine Grace? Still, we went, and tried to please you in that way.

What was that mercy they were speaking about? That is what we are now experiencing. *Ānandāmbudhi-vardhanam*. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are this ocean of transcendental bliss! You are personally present in your instructions! These instructions are your mercy. Kṛṣṇa Himself is present therein. Similarly so are Lord Caitanya and all the disciplic succession. Yes, Śrīla Prabhupāda, your mercy is exactly there, and we are begging you to keep us so properly situated, birth after birth.

*Your servants as Saṅkīrtana-pura,*

## New Vrindaban

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

My Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who is the friend of the poor and lord of the devotees. Almost four years have passed since your last visit. Actually, it seems much longer. We remember so clearly the joy and excitement of hearing, “Prabhupāda is coming! Prabhupāda is coming!” It was about every other year that Your Divine Grace visit us, but still, practically every year an invitation would be sent to you, and an acceptance always followed with a promised date. Then Śrīla Kīrtanānanda would begin chanting his *mantra*, and the whole community was set ablaze with the fever of anticipation and preparing for your arrival. Often, as the date drew nearer, there was a phone call or letter which always began, “I regret to inform you…” , and we didn’t have to read any further. We were crushed.

But there were also the four glorious times you did come. The chanting, “Prabhupāda is coming! Prabhupāda is coming!” spurred on the preparations (It always amazed us how much got done those last few days, just due to this wonderful *mantra*, “Prabhupāda is coming!”), reaching higher and higher levels of intensity, until finally the moment of our lives arrived, and you were there.

So now we don’t hear this *mantra* so much, and a new mood has overtaken New Vrindaban, perhaps a calmer, a more settled mood. It is a feeling of confidence and deep satisfaction in knowing that your palace is finished, and now, by Kṛṣṇa’s grace, you have taken up permanent residence there. Now there is nothing that can force us to let you go.

Sometimes you told us how the governor may visit the prison house, but that does not mean he has become a prisoner. But suppose the prisoners could somehow or other induce the governor to remain with them in loving reciprocation? Then that could hardly be called a prison house, could it? Anyway, whether we have captured you or you have captured us, the fact is that we can never be separated.

*Now together we remain,*

The Inmates of New Vrindaban

## New York

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

It is our habit to think of you in the past tense when we are anxious to remember our relationship with you. As one begins to remember past dialogues and experiences, your presence fills the mind and brings with it peace and fearlessness found only at the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa. *varābhaya-pradaṁ śāntaṁ smaret tan-nāma-pūrvakam.* We discuss with fond remembrance your pastimes, each one of us adding some little piece to the puzzle, until the complete picture of the event is manifest. Through such glimpses, we gain enthusiasm to continue with the work of today, as we strive to remember that you are always with us.

However, those remembrances of the past are full of the instructions that you gave for the present and future. Indeed, those instructions have become our present, and they will be our future as we push forward this movement in years to come. Because of this, we can find your presence at every turn, rather than simply dwelling on the glorious past. We realize that the future is always glorious, by your extraordinary mercy, as it contains nothing but a lifetime of activity conducted carefully under your guidance and instruction.

It is said that the glory of the disciple is that he always lives in the shadow of his spiritual master, as the order of his spiritual master is the only meaning to his life. When one has obtained the cooling shade of the lotus feet of the representative of Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu, then one has certainly gotten the shelter of the lotus feet of Lord Kṛṣṇa. But for us, born in the proud lineage of the demoniac West, the tendency towards independence and neglect of the spiritual master’s mercy is deeply ingrained. Like mad dogs who run about in the noonday sun, we are all too anxious to expose ourselves to the boiling heat of the sun rather than humbly accept the shelter that you are so mercifully giving. Therefore you have given us the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, and if we take shelter of it, our tendency towards thoughtless independence can be conquered, and we will be saved from our own madness. To our vision, the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is emanating forth from you, as before your arrival, we did not find Kṛṣṇa in this place. Kṛṣṇa is ultimately the source, and Kṛṣṇa has always been present, but you were the one who came to remind us of His presence and of our duty to return to Him as quickly as possible. Therefore, everywhere we look within ISKCON we see your presence, as you alone instituted every single aspect of our spiritual life. And it is your special mercy that you have brought us all here, where you first established this institution, as this has made the memory and realization all the more vivid.

A mighty tree may shake in the wind, but if it is properly secured, it will not bread or fall. Any limb, therefore, that is properly connected to the main trunk will withstand the bowing of the strongest gale. For us you are this connection, and we must be careful to maintain it by following your every instruction. The gale force winds of *māyā* are blowing strongly now, and the limbs and branches of this tree are trembling due to that dreadful force. But the tree of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu is composed of a substance stronger than ironwood, and, therefore, it cannot crack under any kind of strain. So we must hold fast to our link – the lotus feet of Your Divine Grace. Please give us your shelter in all circumstances, so that we can continue to flourish and grow until this merciful tree has extended itself so far and wide that it can give shade to the entire universe.

We are begging to remain always fixed in your eternal service.

*Your servants of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda Mandira,*

## Ottawa

*aṁśo bhagavato ’smy ahaṁ sadā dāso ’smi sarvathā*  
*tat-kṛpāpekṣako nityaṁ tat-preṣṭha-sāt karomi svam*

**“I am a tiny part and parcel of the Supreme Lord and I am always and in every way His eternal servant. As I am always hoping for His mercy and kindness, I offer myself to His most dear servitor, my spiritual master.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet, which are the shelter of all conditional souls within this material world.

As it is stated in the *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta*,

*kali-kālera dharma—kṛṣṇa-nāma saṅkīrtana*  
*kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana*

**“The fundamental religious system in the age of Kali is the chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. Unless empowered by Kṛṣṇa, one cannot propagate the** ***saṅkīrtana*** **movement.”**

Although the army of the blind king Dhṛtarāṣṭra saw Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa face to face on the battlefield, they could not recognize His position as the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Similarly, the conditioned souls may not be able to understand your exalted position. Fortunately, by your mercy, your disciples have come to know Kṛṣṇa, and by His grace we have come to know you.

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi*

In order to substantiate Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Gosvāmī’s authoritative statement that only an empowered preacher can push on Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement, there is no finer example than yourself. Taking a retrospective look over the last fifteen years, no other personality in all the three worlds could compete with what you have accomplished. To see over one hundred million books in over thirty different languages printed and distributed in such a short time, and to see the establishment of eighteen farming communities and one hundred and fourteen temples in major cities of the world is such a phenomenal task that Lord Kṛṣṇa had to choose someone extremely qualified. This was unseen before in the history of the world.

Despite your not having any money, not having a youthful body, not receiving any help from your Godbrothers, being in a foreign country, not even having a place to stay, and so many other factors – our tiny brains cannot conceive of how anyone could be so successful. You gave us the formula for success when you stated that you had no idea what the outcome would be but that the only thing you had was firm faith in the words of your own spiritual master and Kṛṣṇa. Since then, you have made *Hare Kṛṣṇa* a household phrase, and every town and village has heard about Kṛṣṇa.

On this glorious day, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we wish that the whole world would come to glorify your exalted position. You often said, “Shoot for the rhinoceros, and if your miss, people will not complain, they will only say, ‘It was an impossible feat anyway.’ ” You have shot the rhinoceros. Our only desire should be to awaken everyone to your glorious lotus feet, because simply in one eleventh of a second one’s life can become perfect:

*‘sādhu-saṅga’, ‘sādhu-saṅga’—sarva-śāstre kaya*  
*lava-mātra sādhu-saṅge sarva-siddhi haya*

**“The verdict of all revealed scriptures is that by even a moment’s association with a pure devotee, one can attain all success.”**

Although you have taught us that by always remembering Kṛṣṇa and never forgetting Him we will become eternally liberated from the miseries of *janma-mṛtyu-jarā-vyādhi*, birth, death, old age, and disease, and go back home, back to Godhead, we are beginning to understand that we are with you whenever we are preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and that is our real shelter.

Our debt to you is impossible to repay, but we pray for the guidance and assistance of yourself and your elected representatives to always be increasing our service to you by always preaching your message. It is a great honor for us to glorify you on your auspicious appearance day, but at the same time we are lamenting how unfortunate it is that the world at large is still lying in the lap of *māyā*, not being able to partake of this great festival.

We pray to always remain in the dust of your lotus feet.

*Your servants in New Kulīna-grāma,*

## Philadelphia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Who would have ever thought that the day would come that upon seeing the approach of an ISKCON devotee one might ask, “Whose disciple are you?” I suppose that of we would have given it serious thought five of six years ago, we would have realized that someday or other this would be the situation. But certainly, no one gave such a thought much serious attention. Even now, I think for some of us that question still evokes a sense of unreality and disbelief.

Nevertheless the situation has changed, and that question has become very relevant. You have expanded your function of *guru* into many, and you have empowered the many to expand your mission.

So now there are many disciples of many spiritual master, and your transcendental family has grown in that way. But still we find that you are the center and the focal point of ISKCON. In fact, to have a relationship with ISKCON is to have a relationship with you.

To your older disciples, you are just like a most intimate father, addressing your devotees as “My dear son” or “My dear daughter.” Sometime, if one would fall away, you might write, “My dear son, please come and stay with me at once. Everything can be rectified. Please do not waste your life.” Sometimes you might even ask for advice in a friendly manner.

Many of your older disciples did not have such intimate contact in this way. For them, you seem to be personal manifestation of awe and reverence. Some of your disciples, perhaps, never even saw you personally, but still they know you through your books and instructions and the association of their elder Godbrothers. Whatever our relationship with you is, Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is sure to become perfect if we simply become perfect in our service attitude towards you. And in that realization we will know you to be our lord and master birth after birth.

Now, as well as having many sons and daughters, you also have many grandsons and granddaughters. As far as their relationship with you is concerned, you have stated that sometimes the grandfather is even more kind to the child than the father. Of course, you kindness is not limited to the disciples of your disciples – you have left the legacy of your books, which will be the lawbooks for mankind for the next ten thousand years.

It is stated in the original ISKCON by-laws: “His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda is the Founder-Ācārya of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness, and his position can never be occupied by anyone else.” Therefore, this title of Founder-Ācārya is very special. It means that to serve ISKCON is to serve you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you are ISKCON. And we pray at your lotus feet that we always remember that to serve your order is to serve the mission of Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda.

*Your disciples and granddisciples at Philadelphia ISKCON,*
