## Portland

In the Mahābhārata the story is told  
Of the Pāṇḍava brothers, righteous and bold.  
Their kingdom was stolen for prestige and gold,  
Their honor was trampled, their sufferings so cold.

Those noble brothers were put to the test  
By Bhīṣma, Karṇa, Droṇa and the rest,  
All driven by passion for power and fame  
And wickedly using the dice fore their aim.

Cast into exile for thirteen years,  
Full of struggle, anguish, toil, and tears,  
Returning home they were cheated again  
And forced into battle with cousin and friend.

In this way, the story unfolds,  
A message of wisdom that never grows old,  
Inspiring our hearts – removing all fears,  
Shining kṛṣṇa-bhakti for thousands of years.

But somehow or other light become dim,  
Covered by ignorance, quarrel and sin.  
This terrible age of darkness and strife  
Has smothered the fire of spiritual life.

Even in India, Kṛṣṇa’s own home,  
Only a glimpse of Absolute Truth was still known.  
Impersonal yogīs, politicians, pretenders,  
Had twisted the *Gītā*, ignoring surrender.

And in America, so-called “land of the brave,”  
Māyā reigned queen, had the people enslaved,  
Chained by desires which were stronger than steel,  
Directed by leaders toward goals quite unreal.

It was then you appeared, by Kṛṣṇa’s grace,  
Destined to deliver the human race.  
Hard struggle for existence – they all held dear.  
Danger at every step – they thought, “Nothing to fear!”

And now another great story unfolds  
Of wonder, excitement – a thrilling tale to be told.  
How you struggled alone, even though you were old.  
How you preached in a land that was barren and cold.

And just like Arjuna, you fought with the best –  
The politicians, teachers, and scientists.  
All the rascals, cheaters, fools, and bluffs,  
You held them all down till they shouted, “Enough!”

But unlike Arjuna with his powerful friends,  
You had no allies on whom to depend.  
Your Godbrothers busy, with no help they mould send,  
So disciples you made from some fried “odds and ends.”

Undaunted, still you went preaching about,  
And when you were ready, you sent us all out.  
And even though we were ignorant fools,  
You gave us the knowledge to act as your tools.

You build a huge empire, found long-lasting fame,  
Using only one weapon – Kṛṣṇa’s holy name.  
And in this way you changed the whole land,  
Bringing back the honor and purpose to man.

Singing and dancing, distributing books,  
By your causeless mercy we all became “hooked.”  
Establishing temples – unheard of before –  
Now, Māyā would cringe at your terrible roar.

So, now a loving disciple writes down the whole story –  
One of struggle, adventure, heartbreak, and glory,  
A tale that will inspire and remove all the fears  
From your disciples’ disciples for thousands of years.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda

*From your disciples and devotees at New Maṇipura Dhāma,*

## Purto Alegre

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya*  
*bhū-tale śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Where are you?

I am lost in this world, chewing the already chewed, trying to find happiness where there is no happiness, wasting my time, my chance to serve you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, where are you?

Please enlighten me. Guide me through the path taken by all the great souls. Don’t let me deviate to the temptations offered by *māyā’s* wizardry. Allow me to always avoid this witch, who, appearing in disguise, of even showing her terrifying face, always makes me fall under her allurement.

Life in this world is a great danger, just like a dark forest. Surrounded by all kinds of ferocious animals, all I have to do is cry and, scared to death, beg your mercy

Oh! Śrīla Prabhupāda, where are you?

Help me an guide me towards the clearing of knowledge. Knowing well that this path is preaching, don’t let me forget it. The transcendental vibration of the holy name will spread all over the universe. Allow me to be your instrument for this to happen, however insignificant I may be.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are here.

Allow me to try to go on serving your beloved disciple Śrīla Hṛdayānanda Goswami, and just like a dog lives at the home of its owner, I will thus be able to take part in this movement.

Considering the fallen condition of the age of Kali, I am praying earnestly to remain an eternal servant of the servant of the fulfiller of your most intimate desire, publishing and distributing your nectarean books.

Gokulotsava dāsa adhikārī / Mahākāla dāsa adhikārī / Advaya dāsa adhikārī / Deva-varīyān dāsa adhikārī / Rāma-priya dāsa adhikārī / Nṛsiṁha-vallabha dāsa brahmacārī / Hṛdaya Govinda dāsa brahmacārī / Caitanya dāsa brahmacārī / Bhakta Paulo / Rohiṇī-devī dāsī / Bhūta-mātā-devī dāsī / Ānandamaya-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Vero / Bhaktin Mirian

## Puente Alto

Dear Spiritual Grandfather, Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet, and mercifully allow us to offer our respectful obeisances at the dust of the lotus feet of our beloved spiritual master, Śrīla Hṛdayānanda Goswami.

For being able to address ourselves to such a great soul as you, we should be nice poets in order to wisely express and sweetly state all the love that you can awaken in our sleepy *mleccha* hearts. But at the same time, we wish to express infinite gratefulness for your causeless mercy, forcing us tu come out of our impersonalist hearts and attempt to glorify your indescribable qualities as an authentic *guru*, even though we are like toads trying to glorify the ocean.

When you cam to the West, not only did our civilization find itself in the most desperate decadence, but those of us that saw it inevitably had no other way out, no other path. Truly, we can say little (our spiritual life was then still dead) about your transcendental activities at the beginning of ISKCON, but you certainly were the only Vaiṣṇava that had compassion on our infernal condition and penetrated into the fire of our lust, anger and greed in order to rip us away from a completely hopeless position, into which only a great soul like can enter without falling into the damned nets of *māyā*. So as light can penetrate water without getting wet because its nature is different from that of water, in the same way, you came to our consciousness, which was full of unrestricted enjoyment in material nature, far away from Kṛṣṇa, and transformed it without getting involved at all in our lost condition of sophisticated animals. So you left for us your most dear spiritual children as our spiritual master and indisputed guides of our movement, authentic lights that will take us, like you took them, directly to the spiritual world.

Accept our infinite gratitude, Parama-guru. We surrender through your instructions to the lotus feet of your beloved son, Śrīla Hṛdayānanda Goswami, our Guru Mahārāja, for humbly helping him to fulfill his desire to distribute your books to all the human beings on this planet.

## Quito

To Śrīla Prabhupāda, on the happy day of his advent.

I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.

By the infinite mercy of this great soul, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who was following the order of Kṛṣṇa and was executing the instruction of his spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, the whole world, where everyone was immersed in the most abominable condition of illusion, was inundate with bliss and spiritual knowledge.

It was thus that as the most expert spiritualist, he sowed in the hearts of all of us the seed of devotion and love of God, and in this way, under his kind protection, he is engaging us in this exalted mission – the propagation of the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

His spiritual presence will still remain, because through his instructions one can realize that the spiritual master, seated in the lotus flower of devotion, is always guiding and protecting all the sincere living entities who are engaged in cultivating Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

In me just remains a deep and great debt to Śrīla Prabhupāda, and my only hope is that with the mercy of your advanced disciples, I may be able to place myself in your service birth after birth, since in this way I will be able to learn how to eternally please Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg to surrender eternally as your insignificant servants at your feet.

*Humbly,*

Mṛganātha dāsa adhikārī / Rājarāma dāsa brahmacārī

## Recife

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most respectful obeisances unto your lotus feet, which are the proprietors of devotional service.

On this auspicious and most blessed event of your appearance day, we pray that you award us service at your lotus feet constantly.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the unlimited ocean of mercy personified. You are always in the presence of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī and assisting the *gopīs*, who at different times make different tasteful arrangements for the perfection of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa’s conjugal loving affairs within the groves of Vṛndāvana. Although dwelling in Vṛndāvana and always merged in transcendental ecstasy, relishing the mellows of pure devotion, you have descended for the welfare of fallen souls, and you are preaching the divine love of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa and delivering the whole planet from the darkness of illusion.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the abode of loving devotion to Kṛṣṇa. Dust from your lotus feet is conducive to devotional service, while service to you is itself the supreme perfection and the root of the tender creeper of divine love.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please place us in the ocean of joy by bestowing upon us happiness in the mellows of service at the feet of Śrī Rādhā in the groves of Vraja-dhāma.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, there is no one except you who can purify the fallen souls. All the *śāstras* therefore advise,

*‘sādhu-saṅga’, ‘sādhu-saṅga’—sarva-śāstre kaya*  
*lava-mātra sādhu-saṅge sarva-siddhi haya*

Even by a little association with you, the conditioned soul can get out of this miserable material condition.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, your heart is always the resting place of Lord Govinda. We desire the dust of your holy lotus feet in every birth we may take. Please be kind upon us.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, Kṛṣṇa is yours. You are able to give Him to us, for such is your power. We are indeed wretched, and simply run after you, crying “Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa!”

*Your insignificant servants from Recife Mandira,*

## Rio de Janeiro

Yesterday I was putting together some slides to be used in a preaching program when I came across dozens of pictures of Śrīla Prabhupāda engaged in different activities. While my eyes ecstatically feasted on all the nectar, I recalled that many times Śrīla Prabhupāda had taught us that just as Kṛṣṇa is nondifferent from His Deity form, so Śrīla Prabhupāda was nondifferent from his representation in the form of a photograph or painting. In this way, the different scenes of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s pastimes seemed to create a time lag, a momentary break in the illusion that Prabhupāda was no longer here, personally guiding us and inspiring us.

In some of the pictures Śrīla Prabhupāda could be seen preaching, vigorously and relentlessly. In others he was talking with his disciples, “polishing” them, just as one lovingly polished articled to be offered to the Deity. In some he was chanting, and playing various musical instruments. Others showed him smiling, and in still others he was grave and serious.

Somehow, seeing all these scenes filled with inspiration and strength, strength to execute Śrīla Prabhupāda’s order, strength to fight all obstacles – both internal and external – in order to see the *guru’s* mission accomplished. Actually, Kṛṣṇa consciousness means Prabhupāda consciousness, *guru* consciousness. That, in essence, is the purpose of Vyāsa-pūjā: to fill, to inundate our consciousness with the memory of Śrīla Prabhupāda – his example, his instructions, his determination, his spirit of sacrifice – for by so doing, he who is an actual disciple will become supercharged with transcendental potency and anxious to free himself of all personal desire in order to make *guru’s* desire his own.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have said several times, “I am ISKCON.” Thus we can understand that because ISKCON exists, because it is present, you are present as well. Just as Paramātmā is all-pervading and all-knowing, so you are all-pervading and all-knowing in the form of ISKCON. You are manifesting yourself through the eleven *ācāryas* chosen by you, through the GBC body, and through each devotee – disciple and granddisciple – who follows and serves the *paramparā*. Just as Kṛṣṇa is simultaneously one with and different from His energy, in the same way we should understand the relationship between Your Divine Grace and ISKCON and its members.

Therefore, on this holy day of Vyāsa-pūjā, let us make a sincere and determined vow: that you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and your divine mission will be glorified so widely that each and every living entity within this universe will eternally know and chant your glories.

*Your servants in Rio de Janeiro*

## Salvador

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

As one of your many disciples, I had always been very anxious to receive your personal association. However, I was instructed that it was more important to carry out your orders. Besides, I was told you were present in your photo (which, for a materialist like me, was difficult to understand). So the days passed, and in my heart I continued to cherish the idea of seeing you personally.

Then suddenly your health took a turn for the worse, an every night I would cant before your picture on the *vyāsāsana*, praying for your health to return. At that time I had enough money to travel and thus be at your side in a matter of hours. However, the desire to travel was outweighed by my desire to do so only with the permission of my authorities. So the time passed, and I continued to wait for the proper moment. I had plenty of service in the temple, and thus my presence seemed necessary there. However, what ultimately checked me was that by your causeless mercy you revealed your presence in your photo, which I chanted to every night. In this way anxiety was relieved.

Then one day you decided to return to Kṛṣṇa. I felt intense sadness – though gradually I began to adjust to the situation. However, within my mind I always harbored doubts as to whether I had acted correctly in not taking the liberty to visit you personally. This was aggravated by a conversation I had later with an older devotee, who related that in the same circumstances he had dove the impossible to travel – by hook of by crook – to be with you personally. How stupid I had been, I began to think, in not being firm enough in my decision to be with you. I was overwhelmed with lamentation – even to the point of offensively blaming my superiors for having been too strict with me. However, that very night you appeared in a dream, mercifully showing your loving glance, that same expression which the devotees who saw you never tired of describing. Once again you had relieved my anxiety.

On this day of your glorious appearance I want to pay you due homage. But what is the meaning of “due homage” for a person as exalted as yourself? There are neither words nor means to glorify you adequately. I do not know where you may be executing your pastimes, though I feel certain that you continue to carry out the great ISKCON pastimes somewhere. Just as when the father gives a toy as a gift for his son and makes it spin in front of the boy, in the same way, you brought us the “matchless gift” of ISKCON, an you made it turn, empowered with the mercy of Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Now it is up to us to keep ISKCON turning. However, since you yourself are the very force behind ISKCON, as long as we are obedient to your instructions, this great movement will never stop turning.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, when you receive this offering, I beg you to kindly forgive my countless offenses against your instructions. And please be tolerant with me, for of all your disciples certainly I am the most fallen. Again and again I offer my respectful obeisances at the dust left by your lotus feet and by the feet of all your sincere followers, such as the eleven ISKCON ācāryas and their respective disciples as well.

Your servants in Salvador, Brazil.

## San Diego

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“O my Lord, great philanthropic souls travel on the earth on behalf of the Supreme Personality of Godhead to show compassion to the fallen souls, who are adverse to the sense of subordination to the Lord.” (*****Bhāg.*** **3.5.3)**

It is said by Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta that the appearance of Kṛṣṇa mocks this mundane realm. In the same way, your appearance, activities, and disappearance also serve to mock this mortal world. You have given the example that the big, strong lion is controlled by the small lion-tamer simply due to the crack of the tamer’s whip. The lion is afraid that behind the threatening sound is so much power, so in this way he is made tu jump through the hoop. In the same way, we are all convinced that we are these material bodies and at the time of death everything is finished. Out of this fear of death we are all madly trying “to get what we can, while we can.” I this way we are becoming exhausted, jumping through *māyā’s* hoops, one after another, body after body, life after life, without ever finding any real peace, substance of satisfaction.

I remember before I ever saw you, the devotees described how “Śrīla Prabhupāda is just like swan.” I could not understand what that meant until I saw you giving *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class. The way you spoke, your gestures, your glance – everything about you convinced me that here was a great and saintly person fixed in transcendence and far beyond this material plane. The swan is described as just extracting the nectar, and the lotus blooms above the mire. Your life confirms that real pleasure is not found in this dead matter but in eternal devotion to Kṛṣṇa.

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are our savior, our master, our friend and only shelter, and you have given us everything. From total and abject darkness you have turned us towards the light. By your words everything becomes revealed.

We need not fear death, as we do not die (*na hanyate hanyamāne śarīre*), we do not belong here (*ābrahma-bhuvanāl lokāḥ*), human life is meant for understanding Kṛṣṇa (*janma karma ca me divyam*), and only by pure devotion can we obtain Him (*bhaktyā mām abhijānāti*). You have given us Kṛṣṇa and the ways and means to obtain Him.

“Of the different processes recommended for disentanglement from material life, the one personally explained and accepted by the Supreme Personality of Godhead should be considered all-perfect. That process is the performance of duties by which love for the Supreme Lord develops.” *(Bhāg*. 7.7.29)

You have given us the nectarean life of devotional service, pure, sublime and ever fresh. When will we fully appreciate, and how will we ever repay you?

*The servants of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Giridhārī*

## San Salvador

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Let us worship this auspicious day of Vyāsa-pūjā, the auspicious day when you began your pastimes in this world.

You have written that when the sun comes up, the human beings begin their activities, lighting fires for cooking their food, marching to their factories, and so on. In the same way, with your appearance we began our real human life. You dissipated our attachment for this dreadful material existence, finishing once and for all *māyā’s* constant kicks, which we have suffered for an eternity.

You appeared to give us Kṛṣṇa consciousness, for making us understand that the light and splendor of the sun, moon and fire emanate from the Supreme Personality of Godhead. You appeared to give us *ātma-tattva*, spiritual science, which we have gotten by your causeless mercy.

You have taught us that in the system for attaining material knowledge our own personal capacity and fitness of investigation is needed; but for attaining spiritual knowledge, the method is *avaroha-panthā*, or in other words, gaining spiritual knowledge through a bona fide source.

You appeared to show us the path of spiritual life, which is the goal for this human form of life, the real path that goes to the spiritual world, the world of complete and absolute freedom.

You appeared to reveal to us the light of the *Vedas*, the transcendental science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the pure truth, which was taught to Arjuna by the blessed Lord.

For millions of years we have been perishing, just like sparks falling our of fire; we became separated from transcendental nature when we fall down from the spiritual world to this detestable chain of birth and death.

But somehow, we have gotten the mercy of the bona fide spiritual master. As it is stated in the revealed scriptures:

*guru kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera prāmaṇe*  
*guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā karena bhakta-gaṇe*

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Ādi*** **1.45).**

So we have received your mercy, and we will instruct everyone to search out this true goal of life – the understanding of the existence of the spiritual world, one’s connection with the Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa, and the shelter of the spiritual master. Please give me the ability to spread your mercy to all mankind.

Preceptor, guide and eternal master, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let me prostrate myself again and again. Let me prostrate at your divine lotus feet, so my defiled heart can at least clean itself and perceive your love, the spiritual sun.

*Your humble servants of San Salvador Mandira,*

## Santiago

*Kṛṣṇas tu bhagavān svayam!*

Prabhupāda! Prabhupāda! You are the one who seats masters at your feet. Oh, mercy! You have kept a place for this damned fool who does nothing but pester you.

This age of Kali, sinister and torturous, sinks men in the deepest pain, but through your benevolent teachings you are giving clear hopes to the intelligent conditioned souls who aspire to leave this abominable place.

Prabhupāda! O Prabhupāda! This conditioned life is so difficult to overcome. I am dizzy in the wheel of continuous birth and death. Still attracted by the face of māyā, I hear your voice that awakens me sweetly to teach me the truth.

O Prabhupāda! Prabhupāda! *Sādhu-saṅga*! *Sādhu-saṅga*! Give me the association of your transcendental memory. I have penetrated the night of ignorance, attracted by some distant luminaries, dressed in the dreams of my whimsical illusion. So, from hope to hope I have woven a path of pain (*brahmāṇḍa bhramite kona*). Tied to the insatiable desire, I throw at your feet a request for help and pardon.

Prabhupāda! My life is your instruction. But how can I obey? I am completely disqualified and materialistic. Where will I get the force to do my duty? Prabhupāda! Do not deprive me of the association of Vaiṣṇavas. Give me purity to give me power. Kṛṣṇa is *pavitra*, but you are *para-pavitra*, because through the power of your purity you conquered lovely *pavitra* and you are purifying me. (The beautiful and splendorous qualities of Lord Kṛṣṇa, your beloved Lord, always take delight in reflecting in the clear mirror of your love.)

O Prabhupāda! What does my absurd intent of glorifying you consist of? You are not only the greatest in this universe but also a great personality in the spiritual world. You came to preach to this land of *mlecchas* and *yavanas* the message that only an elite of chosen few understood! (*Kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nāhe tāra pravartana*.) But you made it accessible inclusively for me. You are the selfsame store of love for God as Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Prabhupāda! I was afflicted so much when I heard of your lonely plight in New York. And what was this demon engaged in? O Prabhupāda, why did you not call on me? I could have done something. Even now don’t let me get fat and lazy. Get me out to fight for you! (*Cakhu-dan dilo jei, janme janme prabhu sei.*) I will let my śikhā grow so you can pull me wherever you want to take me.

Preaching and distribution of books is your life. Let me help you to live! Otherwise my existence will lose all its value. In the same way as the spark of life in the tree gives rise to the existence of the fruit and the flower, in the same way please, mercifully give me the vital force of the preaching of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, so that I can return to life under your lotus feet.

O Prabhupāda! Prabhupāda! O divine! Do not deprive me of the sustenance of your intelligence and renunciation. My memory is scarce and my attachments strong. Keep me tied to your *vyāsāsana* like a dog by its collar.

Taking resolutely the order of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Prabhupāda, you went to walk in the snow in the capital city of demons and accepted any condition, even during your old age. While the weak and feminine materialists sought for shelter in their lust, you risked your life preaching love of God. Being mendicant and renounced, Kṛṣṇa gave you the greatest wealth and biggest family. Who can understand this? That is why I, the biggest sinner, lie at your lotus feet in the hope of being your servant. Śrīla Prabhupāda ki jaya!

*Your children of Chile Yātrā,*

## Sao Paulo

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to your divine teachings and to your wonderful mission of spreading Lord Caitanya’s movement to every town and village in this decadent civilization.

I was just listening to your grave and lovely voice reveal the real picture of our fallen condition in this world: “I have got lots of desires in my heart. I am always angry, falsely prestigious and puffed up by false pride. I am captivated in the subject matters of sense gratification and am almost crazy. My ornaments are enviousness and false pride. I am subdued or I am conquered by sleep and laziness…” I could never imagine that someone would come to the arrogant West and, so boldly, tell us: “You are all demons, because you are manifesting the symptoms of demoniac nature. You have been conditioned by a civilization ruled by demons, and thus you have received first-class training from childhood to become first-class demons. Now you all must give up these demoniac habits and take up your divine nature as eternal devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa. You can choose either eternal conditioning under the clutches of material energy or eternal freedom in the loving embrace of the Lord’s intimate spiritual energy, full of knowledge and bliss….” Who, hearing your invitation, could decline to accept? You made your invitation in such a way that anyone who listens to the instructions of your books will have to immediately give up his material commitments and engage in serving these lotus instructions by telling everyone about both your greatness and your books.

“Every man of knowledge,” you have told us, “must be ready to sacrifice everything for a grater cause. Human society is presently under the spell of a godless civilization. Therefore, we invite every man and woman advanced in knowledge to sacrifice everything for the greater cause of reawakening God consciousness in human society.” Though we may not be men or women of knowledge, we are immediately drawn to enthusiastically accept your invitation. Is there any better cause to sacrifice oneself for than yours? Afghanistan? Iran? Pakistan? Democracy? Communism? Modern science? Propaganda? Or even welfare work? Are these great causes to fight and die for? Economic development and sense gratification have clearly proven to be but illusory goals for human society. Spiritual reawakening of one’s Kṛṣṇa consciousness – this is the real goal for human civilization!

By your grace we will continue throughout our lives to read your books and be inspired by your divine pen to engage ourselves in preaching your message, which is Kṛṣṇa’s, to everyone everywhere.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us your blessings that here in Brazil we can remain always loyal to your mission of giving everyone the benediction of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. No, not only Brazil, the whole world!

*Your disciples and granddisciples of Nova Kurukṣetra,*

## Sao Paulo Farm

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

To our great fortune, another opportunity has arrived to glorify you on your appearance day. Our goal in life is to be able to constantly glorify you, and to do that we beg your causeless mercy.

This day marks the beginning of the turning point for the entire planet. Motivated by lust, anger, and greed, we were all destined to infernal existence. But on this blessed day you, the pure representative of Kṛṣṇa, have saved us from this abominable fate. Actually, you have given us much more: a chance to help. You have left us a sturdy house, ISKCON, and a large bank account, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *saṅkīrtana* movement, and your instructions on how to use it. You have given us everything to make us secure.

Now, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we must ask for just one more thing. Since you have given so much, we are feeling very obliged. Even though in our childlike minds we cannot conceive of its value, as we mature we are realizing that it is an immense treasure. As your loyal children, we want to utilize this treasure appropriately. We must beg you to give us the intelligence to continue your eternal mission. We need the constant association of your oldest sons, who gave demonstrated their pure devotion to you, to guide us on the spiritual path. They will show us how to intelligently invest your assets so that they increase unlimitedly. They can also teach us how to glorify you twenty-four hours a day. That is their constant occupation, and the goal of our lives.

We need this special mercy to keep us from deviating from the path of devotional service. And that mercy is already here, in the form of your divine instructions. These instructions are our only qualification. They can qualify us to leave the illusory world behind and take shelter of your lotus feet, birth after birth.

*Your insignificant servants,*

## Seattle

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Once again we have the unique opportunity to observe the auspicious occasion of your appearance in this mundane world. We see this yearly celebration of your appearance not as a scheduled, ritualistic performance one is obliged to attend out of protocol, but rather, we view it as a time to remember you especially as the one personality in this dark atmosphere who brought us real hope for a brighter future. Our future is bright because you taught us real renunciation, that is, renunciation which comes from “real spiritual emotion.” Just as the ground that is not sufficiently watered produces no fruits of vegetables, dry renunciation has no effect upon the soul. Conversely, an absence of renunciation allows the soul to rot in the muck of sense gratification. So real renunciation is needed, and it is very nicely described by you:

If there is only the Supreme Lord, then a true *sādhu,* even if his wealth increases, gives up his desires for profit, adoration and prestige in this material world. Your gold, brother, is the father of sense gratification. This meaningful statement was spoken by Prabhupāda himself. Give up your wealth for preaching. Sit down together and make a special effort to come to a decision. The Supreme Lord Himself says that everything is His. Don’t protest. Just unite and preach.

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s Vyāsa-pūjā  
Fifth Vaiśiṣṭya, 1961

The message is clear: preach. Without preaching, renunciation is not possible, and if renunciation not achieved, spiritual life is ruined. Consequently, if we do not preach, our spiritual life is finished. While remembering you on this day, we cannot forget your main desire, which is that we must preach vigorously, as you have done. Therefore, we must unite together to reestablish full-scale book distribution, because this is the message you left us with: book distribution is the best preaching.

Here in Seattle we are, by your mercy, attempting to distribute full sets of your books, consisting of fifty volumes. You are Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotee, you are *jagad-guru,*  and you are the founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness, and moreover, you are our spiritual master, and with these and unlimited other qualifications that you possess, we can rest assured that if you bestow your mercy upon us, we may become instrumental, in some insignificant way, to fulfill your desire that a set of books be put in “every gentlemen’s house.”

*Your servants at ISKCON Seattle,*

## Singapore

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.”**

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“Our respectful obeisances unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

Please accept our repeated humble obeisances at your lotus feet. We have no words to describe your glories.

We were so fallen as to have taken birth in this *mleccha-deśa,* a place where they would not even allow a pure devotee like you to enter, they being such *narādhamas.* Yet out of your causeless mercy, even without seeing us, you initiated these fallen souls.

Also, you wanted to shower your complete blessing onto all the fallen souls here by your desire to have a center in Singapore.

Now, by the mercy of Śrīla Haṁsadūta Swami, all this is coming true.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda. All glories to Śrīla Haṁsadūta Swami. All glories to all the Vaiṣṇava *ācāryas.* All glories to ISKCON.

*Always begging to be the servants of your servants,*

## Spain

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
On this auspicious day  
we all united pray  
that you may be so kind  
to accept at your lotus feet  
the New Vraja-maṇḍala community,  
prostrated in respect.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
through the purports to the *Gītā* and *Bhāgavatam*,  
*Caitanya-caritāmṛta* and *The Nectar of Devotion*  
you shower upon men  
the immortal nectar  
of the wisdom of the Absolute.

Blessed Gurudeva,  
by thee Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself  
is becoming revealed to all humanity.

O loving father,  
destroyer of man’s rebirth  
into the darkness of this mortal world,  
unto you we repeatedly prostrate.  
Adoration to you, O Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Thou art of mighty intellect  
and sweet words  
as fragrant as the petals  
of the full-blown lotus.

It was you  
who brightened this lamp of wisdom,  
filling it with the oil  
of the Bhaktivedanta purports.  
We adore you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

You are the shelter of conditioned souls,  
and all who take refuge  
at your lotus feet  
become free from lust, anger and greed.

You are the kalpa-vṛkṣa tree  
for the devotee.  
You have given thousands of fruits,  
and the eleven gurus are the most sweet.

You are the surabhi  
of wisdom and knowledge.

You are the milker  
of the ambrosia of the *Gītā*.

The Upaniṣads  
are as a herd of cows.

You are the milker.

Your best fruits,  
the eleven gurus, are the calf.

The supreme nectar of the *Gītā*  
is the milk.  
And the wise man  
of purified intellect  
is the drinker.

You have no fear,  
because you are surrendered  
to Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet.

O destroyer of man’s illusion,  
O supreme bliss of the devotees,  
O guru of the universe,  
teacher of the worlds,  
unto you we offer our prostrated obeisances.

Please accept them.  
Indeed, among men we are the lowest.

May the spotless lotus  
of the Bhaktivedanta purport  
that shines on the water  
of this material ocean,  
which destroys the sinful attitude  
of the man of Kali-yuga  
and is sweeter than nectar,  
and on which daily light  
the nectar-seeking souls  
as so many bees  
swarming joyously –  
may this fragrant lotus  
bestow on us the highest good.

In this Kali-yuga  
there are great calamities.  
Atheism is like an ocean;  
bars, cinemas, clubs and gambling  
houses are the mighty waves;  
false ego is the strong hurricane;  
there are sharks like the desire for  
lording over and fame;  
family attachments are like alligators;  
and sex is the very whirlpool.

But the Bhāgavatam is the secure boat,  
and you are the mighty captain.

Glory, glory to you,  
the embodiment of supreme mercy,  
by whose grace compassion  
the dumb become eloquent  
and the lame scale mountains.

To you we surrender,  
we offer our prayers,  
we fall down like sticks  
to offer obeisances  
at your lotus feet.

Let us remain  
forever your servants.

Let our meditation be fixed  
on your lotus feet.

## Sri Lanka

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

May Your Divine Grace, out of causeless compassion, accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. You are the most magnanimous personality in such a hellish time as ours. Our only glitter of hope is to take shelter at your lotus feet, to hear and relish the divine words emanating from your lotus mouth, and to follow your instructions as our life and soul. Crores of millions of times we meekly desire to hear about the kingdom of God, Kṛṣṇa.

You are verily a transcendental ocean of ecstatic love, mercy and compassion for the impious live of this yuga, and your divine *bhakti-yoga* is the miracle heater for those in material affliction. Your soft, rosy, compassionate and comforting outpourings of your heart are soothing beams of the moon, relieving us from our bondage. We are fully and unconditionally surrendering unto your unlimited transcendental mercy. Your s*aṅkīrtana* movement frees us from the perilous nets of sense enjoyment and material contamination. The harshness of worldly measures and futile and fragile wantings and desires ebb into nothingness as the inspiration of your *saṅkīrtana* movement, with all its dearness to you, permeates our little minds and hearts. Bereft of your physical presence before season, in tearsome solemn loneliness we are beggars for your causeless mercy and compassion.

Wonderfully traversing the globe by all three elements you have annihilated opposing elements by sowing Vedic wisdom. Your words are desire tree full of unlimited nectar, feeding us with spiritual knowledge in the field of devotional service. Skimming over the barriers of age, sex, position, race, creed, caste, color and birth, you went everywhere to give everyone a chance to know Kṛṣṇa, to love Kṛṣṇa, and to know that there is a soul within this body.

We thank you for another year of devotional service. Our only desire is that we give up all self-motivation and thus always remember your lotus feet. We humbly pray for the strength to carry on your work. Please be especially merciful unto us! All glories to Your Divine Grace! To you, lord and master, we offer our prostrated obeisances countless crores of millions of times.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, accept this Vyāsa-pūjā offering from your very fallen servants of ISKCON Sri Lanka.

Your eternal servants,

## State College

*purojjvala-sakhevaiva*  
*yaḥ parair vacanair api*  
*jīvān prārthayatīśārthaṁ*  
*prabhupādaṁ māmāmi tam*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is like Ujjvala, the** ***priya-narmā*** **friend of Lord Kṛṣṇa, canvassing with irresistible entreaties on the Lord’s behalf.”**

*āṭopāvaraṇaṁ nṣṭaṁ*  
*ku-karmi-jñāni-yoginām*  
*kṛṣṇa-bhaktiṁ praṇītāni*  
*hṛdayāni tu yena vai*

**“He is expert at loosening the dresses of vanity worn by** ***karmīs, jñānīs and yogīs*** **alike, and melting their hearts towards loving devotional service to Kṛṣṇa.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We went about our materialistic lives, cloaking our selfishness with pleas of economic necessity, nationalism, religion, altruism, ethical codes, Biblical codes, health food fads, peace movements, civil and intellectual libertarianism, progress, legal responsibility, family affection, and goals of starvation, but you knew that all these things were actually being performed only for personal sense gratification.

When the city nightclubs and theaters began to compete with the small-town church suppers, the churches became empty, proving the real cause of their existence. The farmers quit growing food and went to idle in the supermarket sense gratification of the metropolises, and the simple believers gave up their faith and taught their children evolution theory when the so-called scientists ridiculed them for their Adam-and-Eve stories. At very best, there remained a little begrudging obedience, but mostly only agnosticism. The whole world was turning under the shadow of *tamisra,* anger, and its conditioned residents thought only, “Why shall I not be a free enjoyer like the Supreme Lord?” And no one understood God, or even wanted to love Him.

Then you came, divine master, teaching, “But above all this is a transcendental stage, in which one feels himself to be only an eternal servitor of Kṛṣṇa, the Absolute Personality of Godhead.” O Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your irrefutable logic, *paramparā* presentation, and clear personal example, you have given us the ability to break free from the dungeon of utter doubt and to believe in God, Kṛṣṇa. And by the wonderful process that you have given us, we are able to realize His presence as the supreme controller, the supreme proprietor, and the supreme enjoyer more and more every day. We know the goal of life, the supreme occupation. You have exhibited your inconceivable potencies – wonderful even among so many great past *ācāryas* – by setting us free from this *puruṣa* misconception, this sex desire, which is the source of fear in all beings, eve up to the limits of the universe. You have done this by most kindly delivering the unique gift of Lord Caitanya, *harīnāma saṅkīrtana.*

We can never begin to repay you, dear master, but at least we can offer you the rest of our existence as your eternal servants. Please allow us the privilege of fighting for your ISKCON, which is you, and which is also the boat which you have left behind to carry all the souls across the ocean of birth and death. You have said that “to award fearlessness to the common man is the greatest act of charity.” O Prabhupāda, you are giving us Kṛṣṇa, who is feared by fear itself, in your books and magazines, in the Deity, in the holy name, in *prasāda,* in so many ways. Now that we have sighed relief from a thousand million lives of fear, we ask that you may so kindly accept us as your instruments for delivering these merciful gift indiscriminately to the fallen conditioned souls. Please just let us go on serving *prasāda,* preaching “the car and the driver,” and distributing your books for as long as it pleases you.

*Your eternal servants at Śrī Kṛṣṇa-nāma-pracārita Mandira,*

Stambha dāsa brahmacārī / Ṛkṣarāja dāsa brahmacārī / Līlānanda dāsa adhikārī, Dyuti-devī dāsī / Kaulinī-devī dāsī / Dāsya-rasa-devī dāsī

## St. Louis

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet, which are to this day traversing all over the world, spreading the glories of the *saṅkīrtana* movement. All glories to you Śrīla Prabhupāda. All glories to your beloved appearance day.

Now it has been nearly three years since your physical departure, and we are all feeling the weight of that physical separation. Of course, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are always with the one who carries out your order, but we are longing for those morning walks at *Bhāgavatam* class, a chance for some little menial service, and again evening *darśana.* These were actually the only good events that occurred in our entire lives – maybe for millions of lives.

Just like the time that there was an evening *darśana* in Māyāpur in 1976. Someone brought in some sweets and your servant put them away. You asked, Śrīla Prabhupāda, why he didn’t serve the *prasāda.* The servant said there were too many people in the room, and replied that he would pass out the *prasāda* as everyone was leaving. Then you quickly replied, “They will never leave.” All the assembled devotees resounded, “Jaya, Śrīla Prabhupāda,” and we felt secure that we would never leave you.

Another time, on a morning walk in Vṛndāvana, a plane was flying overhead. You stopped and said the plane was going to Bombay. The secretary said, “No, Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is heading for Delhi.” You insisted Bombay, and secretary again said, “To Delhi. Śrīla Prabhupāda, this is north, and this is east, and the plane is headed for Delhi.” At that time you struck your cane on the ground and forcefully said, “Bombay.” At that point, to all our amazement, the plane turned in the opposite direction and headed for Bombay.

Than in New Vrindaban, in 1976, you were quoting one verse in *Bhāgavatam* and asked the *sannyāsīs* to find it. For five minutes the *sannyāsīs* were looking in the index and all through the book but could not find the quote. You said the devotees should know our books like a lawyer knows his lawbooks. Then a devotee handed you the *Bhāgavatam,* and you turned to the page of the verse, without looking, and the devotee read the verse. We were all very proud to be disciples of the greatest spiritual master of all times. The air resounded with “Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda” as you shook your head, “*Āchā,”* and smiled, pleasing the hearts of all devotees.

Your pastimes are unlimited, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and to remember them is the perfection of the mind.

*akṣṇoḥ phalaṁ tvādṛśa-darśanaṁ hi*  
*tanoḥ phalaṁ tvādṛśa-gātra-saṅgaḥ*  
*jihvā-phalaṁ tvādṛśa-kīrtanaṁ hi*  
*sudurlabhā bhāgavatā hi loke*  
*(Hari-bhakti-sudhodaya 13.2)*

**“O devotee of the Lord, the perfection of the eyes is to see you, the perfection of the senses is to touch you, the perfection of the tongue is to glorify you, for it is very rare to associate with such a great personality.”**

Quote frankly, we have no understanding of the Personality of Godhead, but we do understand to some extent you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Therefore, as stated in the Vedic literature,

*ārādhanānāṁ sarveṣāṁ*  
*viṣṇor ārādhanaṁ param*  
*tasmāt parataraṁ devi*  
*tadīyānāṁ samarcanam*  
*(Padma Purāṇa)*

**“Of all types of worship, the best is the worship of Viṣṇu. But better than that is worshiping the paraphernalia and devotee of Viṣṇu.”**

We pray, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we may never forget your instructions and always work cooperatively to carry out your order. Your order is our life and soul, and we would rather give up our lives if we cannot fulfill that order. And we pray that your transcendental pastimes may ever be enacted in our minds, so that at the time of death we can again join in your association and remain your eternal servants.

*Your servants of New Kṛṣṇaloka, your eternal servants,*

## Switzerland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances prostrated in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to you, because you are the foremost apostle of Lord Caitanya’s *harināma saṅkīrtana* movement.

Another year has passed, and by your mercy you continue to engage me in the mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Preaching and traveling, chanting, distributing books, arranging Ratha-yātrā, seeking a new temple for Gaura-Nitāi, worshiping Their lotus feet – these and countless other devotional opportunities have absorbed my attention, taxed my brain, challenged my steadiness and filled my days and nights since I last wrote you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Even though any number of your disciples may perform these services far more expertly than I, you still see fit (inexplicably so) to confide these matters to me. This is a great to me and others as well. It proves your superlative ability to bring Kṛṣṇa consciousness within the grasp of any conditioned souls. Of this I am fully confident, as of nothing else. And for this I am eternally indebted to Your Divine Grace.

*Your menial servants,*

Guru Gaurāṅga dāsa / Jaya Gurudeva dāsa / Rohiṇī-suta dāsa / Śaṅkarāraṇya dāsa / Keśināśana dāsa / Prema-rasa dāsa / Ujjvala-nīlamaṇi dāsa / Guru-śakti dāsa / Puruṣottama dāsa / Gaura-līlā dāsa / Parama-jyoti dāsa / Ānanda-vardhana dāsa / Vāsudeva dāsa / Balabhadra dāsa / Bhakta Philippe / Bhakta Ramayā-devī dāsī / Gopī-priya-devī dāsī / Prema-bhakti-devī dāsī / Rādhā-vinoda

## Sydney

To our most beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. By your causeless mercy we have been given the opportunity to engage in this sublime process of devotional service. For this topmost privilege we are eternally indebted to you. We have not yet fully realized the full greatness of Your Divine Grace’s appearance and activities in this world, but we sincerely pray that we may one day appreciate you fully and thus be able to glorify you in a most proper manner. For the present, we humbly beg you to bear with us and accept our feeble offering.

Into the midst of the cyclonic disturbances of this world, you came. Valiantly you struggled in this chaotic place to establish ISKCON – the still point at the center of the storm. In here we can see that everything is in perfect order. Like a perfectly opening lotus flower, your movement is spreading everywhere with exact precision.

The special significance of your divine appearance day is that it is an added impetus for us to remember that you are the center of ISKCON. You once said, “I am ISKCON,” and this is perfectly true. An ordinary *yogī* can manifest cities of even a whole planet that can fly in the sky. But you have created a universal movement in the womb of which Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself has taken birth. ISKCON is your body, and from this body the Supreme Lord is becoming manifest in the material world – just as Lord Brahmā became manifest on the lotus that is the expansion from the body of Viṣṇu. Your constant, heroic endeavor to establish ISKCON and push on the preaching, combined with your simple instructions, tirelessly repeated, are the unshakable roots of this wonderful creation. From you is coming all the inspiration and energy needed to push on this movement. May these remembrance of you remain forever in our hearts.

We take this opportunity to offer this prayer. May we become perfectly chaste disciples of Your Lordship, eternally fixed in serving mood, unswayed by *māyā’s* allurements. Although most unworthy and unqualified, we beg that we may develop real and pure love for you and that you kindly keep us with you wherever you may go to preach Lord Caitanya’s mission.

*We are your humble servants,*

Bhāgavatāśraya dāsa / Bhṛgupati dāsa / Vasuśreṣṭha dāsa / Trībhuvanapati dāsa / Ādi-puruṣa dāsa / Kārṣṇa dāsa / Kāliya Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Nīlācala dāsa / Vrajarāja dāsa / Madhumaṅgala dāsa / Guru-kṛpā dāsa / Gajendranātha dāsa / Dayāmaya dāsa / Jāmbavān dāsa / Bhakta Ronald / Bhakta Richard / Satyabhāmā-devī dāsī / Satyarūpā-devī dāsī / Rādhikā-ramaṇa-devī dāsī / Arcā-mūrti-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Nerida

## Tehran

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Of all your unlimited manifestations, it is your preaching mood, your vigilant, active, consistently forward, progressive and pushing preaching mood that we strive to emulate. We pray to stay day and night in this mood forever. We’d rather suffer war, famine, disease, rejection, separation … any distress, but we pray not to give up this mood of preaching that you have so kindly bestowed upon us. Everywhere and always, regardless of the reactions, we wish to speak the truth as you have told us and shown us, that truth confirmed by Kṛṣṇa’s words and His pure devotees.

O determination fixed, we feel together.  
The darkness immense, but that does not matter.  
A spark from your fire is what we see.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you’re our master eternally.  
We are angry; we feel so mad.  
Brothers/sisters all dying, our mother sad.  
Fighting is the life source. Except that, all dead.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg you – keep your feet on our heads.  
We fight with passion each day throughout the year,  
But have no compassion, only envy, for our peers,  
Yet we’re the medium for your mercy when someone comes near.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you saved the world with your tears.  
Cool, defensive, fearful, inactive we were – you made us intense.  
From skeptic to front-line men, now we impregnate truth and give common men sense.  
Books, Deities, temples, programs … you gave these transcendental instruments.  
But, our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, never let us forget, your mood is the essence.  
Our inner fight with māyā is on two fronts –  
the inner world of our hearts, and  
this world of our service.  
On the first front, we cannot succeed  
unless we capture your mood with our deeds.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, aspiring only for your preaching mood,  
we beg the shade of your lotus feet.

*Your fallen servants in Tehran, with branches in San Francisco and Saudi Arabia*

## Tekari

**“The spiritual master is receiving benediction from the ocean of mercy. Just as the cloud pours water on the forest fire to extinguish it, so the spiritual master extinguishes the blazing fire of material existence. I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master.”**

My most worshipable Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Kindly accept the Tekari temple and devotees’ most worthless obeisances at your lotus feet. ISKCON Tekari is in Bihar, the country of the great demon Jarāsandha. Please bestow your mercy upon us and give us the dust of your lotus feet for preaching here in Bihar.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the mercy incarnation of Kṛṣṇa. You are compared to a rain cloud, which pours rain freely everywhere. By your causeless mercy the preaching work of your disciples has become successful in every corner of the world. In this way your mercy has become available to everyone. By this mercy one can perceive the burning desire in the hearts of the fallen souls to enjoy separately from Kṛṣṇa. The living entity is described as being like an animal lost in a vast desert. The animal is always seeing a mirage, thinking it to be water. The animal continues like this, over and over again, until finally it meets death. This is the position of the lost conditioned souls within this material world. They are always hoping and searching for some relief from the miseries of material existence. By your causeless mercy you have given the opportunity to cross over this desert of material existence by distributing profusely this knowledge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. By giving this knowledge of the all-attractive Supreme Lord and His eternal abode, you are convincing conditioned living entities to dovetail their sinful desires into pure desire to serve Kṛṣṇa and to return to their rightful place in the eternal abode of the Supreme Lord.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are unable to describe your beautiful qualities by words. Even a foolish person can understand you by your activities, what to speak of a scholar. One can actually experience the spiritual world simply by visiting one of your beautiful temples, which you single-handedly established all over the world. And this was your vision. Actually, your vision was the same as that of Lord Caitanya.

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi-grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

The order of Lord Caitanya is to spread this movement in every town and village in every country of the world. And you have followed the instructions perfectly. Although we are very fallen, you have allowed us to assist you in your mission to save the world. We have nothing to offer you in return, because everything we have, you have given us. We only hope that you will kindly accept our bodies, minds, and words in pushing on this *saṅkīrtana* movement.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, innumerable times you have reminded us that everyone in the material world is the enemy of Kṛṣṇa and His devotees. So, the solution is that ISKCON devotees must get together. United we will stand and expand; divided we will scatter and fall. “Push on this movement,” that is your instruction. So, let us follow that sincerely. The cooperative devotional spirit of all of your disciples is what makes this movement, so cooperation is the basic, essential thing.

Actually, you have challenged all of us by uttering the statement that you are going to watch our behavior after you are no longer physically present with us. Falling at your feet, we humbly beg again and again, O beloved spiritual master, that you would please be merciful enough to instill that spirit of cooperation in core of our hearts, so that there may be some meaning to our existence. Otherwise, we remain,

*Your eternal, insignificant servants of ISKCON Tekari,*

## Tennessee Farm

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. As you have requested, we, the devotees at the Murāri-sevaka farm community, are trying very hard to make this a self-sufficient ISKCON farm. If we can somehow or other please your very dear disciple Śrīla Hṛdayānanda dāsa Goswami, then we know that you will also be pleased. Please give us the intelligence and sincerity to accept the mercy which you have bestowed upon this whole planet through your personal presence, your books, and now through your pure devotee disciples.

*Your eternal servants, the devotees at Murāri-sevaka*

## Toronto

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

We often shudder at the thought of what horrors could have befallen us if we had not taken shelter at your lotus feet. Being completely under the control of material nature, our lives were totally unpredictable and foreboding: drug addiction, alcoholism, prostitution, criminality … Life was a gamble, like a game of Russian roulette: spin the chamber and take a chance.

Man proposes and God disposes. However, because Kṛṣṇa is not responsible for the reactions of our chosen activities, the people of this age are living in their own self-made hells, helpless victims of their perverted, renegade senses. We, with them, were plunging into the darkest regions of ignorance, until by your causeless mercy you rescued us from this holocaust and illuminated our lives with the torchlight of knowledge. You gave us new life, a new course, and Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet.

It is apparent when we visit with old acquaintances that there is a vast gulf between us, solely due to your holy association. Our old friends are drowning quickly in the ocean of material desires, their senses more and more covered from reality by the lure of *māyā’s* enticing song. We, too, were floundering in this flood of misery until you came and gently lifted us into your transcendental boat.

You taught us everything: how to eat, how to sleep, how to keep clean, and above all, how to know and serve Lord Kṛṣṇa. O divine father, we have literally been born again by your causeless mercy, and this new life of Kṛṣṇa consciousness is delivering us from the manifold miseries of material life and taking us back home, back to Godhead.

The value of a precious gem can only be appreciated by a trained gemologist. Similarly, the value of service to your lotus feet can only be fully comprehended by your surrendered servants. Desiring to realize the full realm of your mercy, we, in this neophyte position, can only beg for your unfailing compassion, so that we can see beyond the apparent glimmer of your greatness and enter into your pastimes as your pure, unalloyed servants. We owe a great debt to you. O great master, a debt far greater than we can ever hope to redeem. We can only beg you to allow us to try … eternally.

*Your servants at New Remuṇā Dhāma,*

## Trinidad

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most auspicious day please accept my humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet. Please forgive this inadequate attempt in trying to give some glorification of your unlimited, magnanimous activities.

The conditioned soul is struggling very hard to overcome the stringent laws of material nature, but this is impossible and prevents the living entity from becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious. But there is one path to become Kṛṣṇa conscious, and that is by the mercy of Your Divine Grace. As is stated in the scriptures, “The conditioned soul becomes Kṛṣṇa conscious by the mercy of the beloved spiritual master, who voluntarily preaches the spiritual injunctions and helps him to become Kṛṣṇa conscious. The conditioned soul is liberated from the clutches of Māyā, who gives him up.” O my beloved spiritual master, you mentioned that our love for you depends upon our cooperation with each other in your absence, which is what you want most of all. Your divine instruction is to engage in *bhakti*, and one’s advancement in *bhakti* is tested by seeing whether one is advancing in devotional service and pleasing your kind heart. One must be detached from material enjoyment. Such detachment means that the conditioned soul is liberated from the *māyā* of illusory enjoyment. When one is advanced in Kṛṣṇa consciousness he does not consider himself as good as Kṛṣṇa, but he thinks as Caitanya Mahāprabhu describes:

*nāhaṁ vipro na ca nara-patir nāpi vaiśyo na śūdro*  
*nāhaṁ varṇī na ca gṛha-patir no vana-stho yatir vā*  
*kintu prodyan nikhila-paramānanda-pūrṇāmṛtābdher*  
*gopī-bhartuḥ pada-kamalayor dāsa-dāsānudāsaḥ*

This is the sign of the Vaiṣṇavas who have your divine instructions interlocked within their hearts and are making those instructions their life and soul. We beg for your causeless mercy to remain your beloved children.

*Your aspiring servants at Śrī Kiṁpuruṣa-varṣa Dhāma (ISKCON Trinidad),*

## Urban Spiritual Development

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

The special function of yourself and Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī is to establish the feelings of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. These feelings are His desires that His special mercy be spread throughout the world in this Kali-yuga. Our eternal father, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have so expertly made arrangements to carry out this mission perfectly. What is there left for us to do other than to also become absorbed in this same mood? We thank you for somehow or other keeping us in your service, especially book distribution. We must constantly beg forgiveness for remaining such third-class devotees. Every day I am praying that you force us at any cost to become pure servants. If you do not apply force, then we will surely be doomed.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, the first devotional service I did even before I became a devotee was to buy your books and try to distribute them to various libraries. Although I was a *karmī* then, little did I know that this would basically be the nature of my ongoing service to you. I am realizing more and more that unless I am distributing your books of making arrangements for others to distribute your books, I feel disconnected and very much like a cheater. Thank you very much for allowing me to assist in selling your books to university and college libraries around the world and door to door to homes throughout the U.S.A. Now you have given us the U.S.D. (Urban Spiritual Development) program, which is, on one hand, an amalgamation of different devotee businesses and preaching centers, as we have restaurants, catering services, boutiques, jewelry businesses, a furniture store and cane shop, as well as a college program and prison program. Our main business, however, is to serve as an outpost for distributing books and sets of books, and also making manpower to distribute books throughout Africa.

Some of us are now here in Africa, where we are bombing the streets with thousands of your books and distributing sets of books to the libraries. Please give us the intelligence how to do all of these programs perfectly. Through this book distribution I pray that one day you will allow us to bring millions of conditioned souls to take shelter of your lotus feet. Please help us to play a small role in spreading this Kṛṣṇa consciousness to literally every town and village. Is there any other reason to be alive?

## Vancouver

All glories to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami, who is *jagad-guru,* the spiritual master of all the world and universe. In a world that is raging with sinful chaos, filled with gloom and despair, fast approaching cataclysmic war, only the serenity of your devotional truth and the vision of beauty that you have revealed give hope to this ugly place. You have said that we, your disciples, are the flower of our generation, like flower growing from a garbage dump. If we are flowers, it must be remembered that you are the gardener that planted the seed, watered and tended the tender creepers, and pulled the weeds also.

Once you were asked why it was the America, although practically godless and full of sinful practices, seemed to be flourishing with material opulences. You reply was, “Just wait.” Your understanding of spiritual science is such that you cannot be fooled of bewildered in any circumstance. You could easily see the false foundation of America’s might, and now that America is swiftly fading in economic disasters and political humiliation, once again we can see how perfect is vision. Every one of your ideas for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness was brilliant and perfect. We need only have unflinching faith in your ideas; you set the directions perfectly. For example, I can remember in the early seventies when you began to talk about a luxurious hotel and gala preaching center in Bombay. What a great but impossible dream! At least, that’s the way it seemed to the small-minded like myself. In those days, our temples were so small and struggling. Who could have imagined? This year I visited the Bombay center and saw the tens of thousands of happy guests thronging to the extravagant temple that was built there at your insistence. You also preached constantly about book distribution. You said over and over again that we should increase book distribution and that all other benefits and facilities would come. As the devotees tried, a little doubtfully, to distribute these books, the most amazing thing happened. Books began to go out in ever-increasing numbers, and with the books came all the other facilities of big temples, restaurants, farms, etc., etc., that indeed seemed to manifest from the *lakṣmī* and enthusiasm created by the book distributors.

In your earliest days in America, when you had no disciples and hardly a friend, you spoke to one man on a park bench in New York. He related to me ten years later that you had assured him that you would have temples, farms, books, and schools. He repeated this in great wonder, having returned for the first time to one of your ISKCON temples after so many years and finding that your confidence had not been the nostalgic dreaming of an old, sentimental holy man, but had become a concrete fact. He also described that even at that time, when you had no money at all, you would distribute apple *prasāda* to the destitute. Who could ever compare to your transcendental, elevated position? We should simply take your instructions on our head and follow with complete faith. How can we possibly compare our own small, bewildered intelligence to yours? If we simply follow obediently and faithfully in your footsteps, we are completely assured of attaining the goal of love of God and Kṛṣṇa consciousness that you have promised us.

Everything you have done is perfect. In the last few years, since you entrusted the precious treasure of ISKCON to your most trusted and advanced disciples, the wisdom of the arrangement has become obvious. You set up the movement so that the combined body of the GBC would be greater than any of its parts, and their combined intelligence would make sure that this Kṛṣṇa conscious movement would go on and on to save the world from its dreadful, atheistic fate.

You have given each one of your disciples the order to spread this movement by giving up sense gratification, thoughts of “I” and “mine,” and dedicating one’s self to the great challenge of pushing out the barriers of our temples until the whole world is included. You have asked us not to fight, but to cooperate together to see your goals fulfilled. This may be the hardest request of all, as you once pointed out that we are trained up to fight. Each and every one of your disciples is absolutely obligated to take up your mission without reservation or personal consideration, and those who do will be with you always. I pray for the intelligence to make that my only goal. Attachment to your lotus feet is the perfection that fulfills all desires.

This year has become a time of great crisis for the *karmīs,* as the inevitable punishment for their sinful culture looms before them. Both the devotees and *karmīs* seem to feel that war is imminent. A crisis, however, also seems to be facing ISKCON. We must admit that the great, expanding force of book distribution has shrunk, and indeed it may even be that the despair facing the world has been allowed to grow faster without the purifying influence of our book distribution. For this year’s Vyāsa-pūjā offering, let us rededicate our selves to the order of Śrīla Prabhupāda to distribute books and preach, and have unflinching faith in his transcendental goals!

*Your servants in New Gokula,*

## Vṛndāvana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who has delivered the fallen souls in this age of Kali by your presentation of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* to the Western countries. We are viewing your glories from the place where you started, Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma, from where you left your peaceful rooms at Rādhā-Dāmodara to go to a land where practically no one had the least idea who Kṛṣṇa is. Vṛndāvana is by nature free from the influence of material nature, and you being an eternal resident of Śrī Vṛndāvana, having the same quality, remained completely transcendental as you preached among the residents of the material world. By your brilliant presentation of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* you were able to influence the entire world, just as the sun by its potency illuminates the world.

Now, as you have explained in the case of your eternal spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, you have left our vision and entered into your eternal pastimes of preaching the glories of Śrī Kṛṣṇa elsewhere, beyond our limited vision. On the celebration of your most glorious appearance, we should remember that from that very day you descended to this world, your glories were known. Even by astrological calculations you were deemed to possess all of the finest qualities that one could have, and also it was told that you were capable of changing the tide of Kali-yuga. Your glories were predicted, and simply time separated you from executing the unbelievable task of conquering the enemies of impersonalism and atheism rampant in the world. Your glories were heralded from that very day, and now we are trying to spread your glories further throughout the world. Why is that? Because worship of Your Divine Grace is considered even greater than the worship of the Lord Himself.

*ārādhanānāṁ sarveṣāṁ*  
*viṣṇor ārādhanaṁ param*  
*tasmād parataraṁ devi*  
*tadīyānāṁ samarcanam*

**“The most exalted system of worship is the worship of Lord Viṣṇu. Greater than that is the worship of** ***tadīya,*** **or anything belong to Viṣṇu.” (*****Padma Purāṇa*****)**

You belong to Kṛṣṇa, and in fact, according to the revealed scriptures, you are considered nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa.

*guru kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera pramāṇe*  
*guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā karena bhakta-gaṇe*

**“According to the deliberate opinion of all revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of the spiritual master delivers His devotees.” (Cc.** ***Ādi*** **1.45)**

So actually, you are nondifferent from Śrī Kṛṣṇa, appearing as His greatest devotee to deliver us. We are not worthy of this mercy, but by your sweet will you decided to pick us up from the material world. How can we express our gratitude to you for delivering us?

*naivopayanty apacitiṁ kavayas taveśa*  
*brahmāyuṣāpi kṛtam ṛddha-mudaḥ smarantaḥ*  
*yo ’ntar bahis tanu-bhṛtām aśubhaṁ vidhunvann*  
*ācārya-caittya-vapuṣā sva-gatiṁ vyanakti*

**“O my Lord! Transcendental poets and experts in spiritual science could not fully express their indebtedness to You, even if they were endowed with the prolonged lifetime of Brahmā, for You appear in two features—externally as the** ***ācārya*** **and internally as the Supersoul—to deliver the embodied living being by directing him how to come to You.” (*****Bhāg*****. 11.29.6)**

If even the experts cannot fully express their gratitude to their spiritual master, then what can we, insignificant menial servants, hope to achieve? We just pray for your causeless mercy that you never let us go.

Falling at your lotus feet, we beg to remain

*Your eternal servants, the residents of ISKCON Vṛndāvana,*

## Washington, D.C.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*yogyatā kichu nāhi pāi, tomāra karuṇā sāra*

**“If you examine me, you will find no qualities; your mercy is all that I am made of.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In 1965 you came to America under great hardship, your Guru Mahārāja’s instructions your only shelter. After ten months of inconceivable struggling you founded ISKCON, the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. You said the goal of ISKCON was to provide a means for the world to become Kṛṣṇa conscious.

We have all spent our lives studying in the *asura-kula,* but you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have come dancing and chanting into our lives. We are like the rich man’s son who has forgotten who his father is and who now is wandering aimlessly, completely destitute. But dancing, chanting and distributing *kṛṣṇa-prasāda* you have come. “Wake up! You have forgotten your real identity. You are the eternal servant of Kṛṣṇa; you’re the son of the richest man. Wake up! Your constitutional position is serving Kṛṣṇa. Wake up! Take to this chanting, dancing, and feasting. Reawaken your loving propensities towards Kṛṣṇa. Make your life sublime.”

You came, a transcendental pied piper, dancing into our lives. You captured our hearts and now you are leading us out of the snare of *saṁsāra.* No one else would come, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Such is your mercy. *Patita pavana tomā vine keho nai:* “You are the savior of the fallen. Without you, there is no one.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have made us fortunate, given us everything, given us Kṛṣṇa. So now, with your blessings, let us go out and make others fortunate. Everyone we meet, we will give them Kṛṣṇa and thus try to make the first installment on a debt we can never repay.

Though we are completely unqualified, please keep us as your eternal servants.

*The residents of New Hastināpura,*

## Prabhupāda-līlā

In July of 1970, Śrīla Prabhupāda attended the Ratha-yātrā festival in San Francisco. After driving up to Lord Jagannātha’s cart, His Divine Grace paid *daṇḍavats* in the street, got up, and began to dance in ecstasy.
